# Minnow — project context

Product overview: [`README.md`](../README.md). Developer setup and commands: [`getting-started.md`](getting-started.md). **Community Discord:** https://discord.gg/U4FPzv9K4X — setup plan: [`documentation/plans/discord-community-setup.md`](plans/discord-community-setup.md).

**Feature gap audit (2026):** [`documentation/plans/feature-audit-roadmap.md`](plans/feature-audit-roadmap.md) — shipped vs partial vs missing across agents, Reef, trace/replay, and settings. **Electron desktop wrapper (in progress):** [Linear project](https://linear.app/minnowai/project/electron-wrapper-549883cbfbe1) — [MIN-109](https://linear.app/minnowai/issue/MIN-109) runtime extraction shipped; [MIN-110](https://linear.app/minnowai/issue/MIN-110) `electron/` shell + `electron:dev`; [MIN-111](https://linear.app/minnowai/issue/MIN-111) in-process Connect + `sirv` host for `electron:prod`; [MIN-112](https://linear.app/minnowai/issue/MIN-112) `WebContentsView` preview via `window.minnow.preview` ([`electron/preview-host.ts`](../electron/preview-host.ts)); [MIN-113](https://linear.app/minnowai/issue/MIN-113) Windows NSIS packaging via `electron-builder` (`npm run package` → `release/`); index [`documentation/plans/electron-wrapper.md`](plans/electron-wrapper.md). **Local eval harness (audit #21, shipped MIN-57):** task packs, suite matrix runner, LLM rubric grading, leaderboard in Settings → Evals; `~/.minnow/evals/`. Plan: [`documentation/plans/Build out/feature-21-local-eval-harness.md`](plans/Build%20out/feature-21-local-eval-harness.md). QA: [`documentation/plans/verification/feature-21-eval-harness.md`](plans/verification/feature-21-eval-harness.md). **In-app LLM Benchmark screen (Bench, planned):** [`documentation/plans/benchmark-system-implementation.md`](plans/benchmark-system-implementation.md) — active-model integration battery + run history (complements Feature 21). **#03 Context budgets per agent (plan):** [`documentation/plans/Build out/feature-03-context-budgets.md`](plans/Build%20out/feature-03-context-budgets.md). **#07 Sub-agent budgets + structured summaries (shipped MIN-43):** [`documentation/plans/Build out/feature-07-sub-agent-budgets.md`](plans/Build%20out/feature-07-sub-agent-budgets.md). **#09 Sampler presets per agent (built):** per work-agent and sub-agent-type `sampler` objects merged at send time in [`src/agents/resolve-sampler.ts`](../src/agents/resolve-sampler.ts) → [`src/tools/loop.ts`](../src/tools/loop.ts) and [`src/agents/sub-agent-runner.ts`](../src/agents/sub-agent-runner.ts); defaults in [`src/agents/defaults/work-agent-samplers.json`](../src/agents/defaults/work-agent-samplers.json) and [`src/agents/defaults/sub-agents.json`](../src/agents/defaults/sub-agents.json). Plan: [`documentation/plans/Build out/feature-09-sampler-presets.md`](plans/Build%20out/feature-09-sampler-presets.md). **#10 Constrained decoding for tool calls (MIN-46, built):** optional `response_format` JSON Schema on tool turns when probe + settings allow; [`src/providers/capability-probe.ts`](../src/providers/capability-probe.ts), [`src/providers/tool-call-schema.ts`](../src/providers/tool-call-schema.ts), [`src/tools/parse-tool-arguments.ts`](../src/tools/parse-tool-arguments.ts); persistence `~/.minnow/providers/<id>/capabilities.json` and `config.json` → `toolCalls.useConstrainedDecoding`. Plan: [`documentation/plans/Build out/feature-10-constrained-decoding.md`](plans/Build%20out/feature-10-constrained-decoding.md). **#12 Prompt diffing (shipped MIN-47):** compare-to-shipped-default in Settings (`src/ui/prompt-diff-panel.ts`, `src/chat/prompts/text-diff.ts`); `GET ?baseline=builtin` on `/api/prompts/.../prompt` and work-agent prompt routes; per-part reset on custom prompt configs. Plan: [`documentation/plans/Build out/feature-12-prompt-diffing.md`](plans/Build%20out/feature-12-prompt-diffing.md). **#13 Prompt profiles / setup bundles (shipped MIN-49):** named portable bundles under `~/.minnow/profiles/` (prompt meta + agent overrides + tool permissions); export/import, one-click activate, per-workspace default + optional auto-apply on workspace switch. Plan: [`documentation/plans/Build out/feature-13-prompt-profiles.md`](plans/Build%20out/feature-13-prompt-profiles.md). APIs: `server/profiles/`; UI: Settings → Prompting (`src/ui/settings-profiles.ts`). **#20 Multi-model conversation (plan):** [`documentation/plans/Build out/feature-20-multi-model-conversation.md`](plans/Build%20out/feature-20-multi-model-conversation.md). **Sub-agent orchestration** is documented below under **Sub-agent orchestration (Step 09)**; verification: [`documentation/plans/verification/step-09.md`](plans/verification/step-09.md).

**To-fix roadmap:** Backlog in [`documentation/plans/to-fix.md`](plans/to-fix.md). **Implementation build plans** (with tests and todos): [`documentation/plans/Build out/`](plans/Build%20out/) — `switch-chats-while-waiting`, `reef-files-minnow-home`, `reef-optional-save-prompt`, `no-auto-open-terminal`, `no-restart-finished-chat`, `llm-mode-switch-suggestions`, `fix-chat-titles-thinking-leak`, `files-sidebar-close-arrow` (line numbers in each plan link to `to-fix.md`). Product backlog plans remain `feature-01` … `feature-30` in the same folder. **Persistence contract (Step 02+):** `~/.minnow/sessions/state.json` — single session blob, not per-chat files. **Tests (Step 02+):** `npm test` → [`test/run-all.mjs`](../test/run-all.mjs) auto-discovers `test/**/*.test.{js,mjs,mts,ts}` and batches by runner (`node --test` for pure `.test.js`, `tsx` + [`test/test-loader.mjs`](../test/test-loader.mjs) for `.test.mjs`/`.mts`/`.ts` that import `src/`). `npm run test:check-coverage` fails CI on orphan files. Gate: [`.github/workflows/ci.yml`](../.github/workflows/ci.yml) (MIN-383).

## Odysseus port plan pack

Implementation-ready plans for the 13 selected Odysseus-to-Minnow candidate ports live in [`documentation/plans/Odysseus port/`](plans/Odysseus%20port/). Start with the folder [`README.md`](plans/Odysseus%20port/README.md) for dependency order and Linear tracking ([project](https://linear.app/minnowai/project/odysseus-to-minnow-port-9e0acc5cf9c9)). **Prompt-injection defense (shipped MIN-124):** [`src/lib/untrusted.mjs`](../src/lib/untrusted.mjs) + [`server/security/untrusted.js`](../server/security/untrusted.js) fence untrusted text with `<<<UNTRUSTED_SOURCE_DATA source="…">>>` … `<<<END_UNTRUSTED_SOURCE_DATA>>>`; policy in [`default.full.md`](../src/chat/prompts/base/default.full.md) / [`default.lite.md`](../src/chat/prompts/base/default.lite.md) and sub-agent prompts. Injection sites: memory retrieve (`server/memory/retrieve.js`), web fetch/RAG (`server/tools/fetch-web-content.js`), Deep Research extraction (`server/research/extractor.js`), `read_document` + composer text attachments, research report discuss (`src/research/panel.ts`), and server tool middleware (`server/runtime/tools-middleware.js` for web/search/MCP). Deferred v1: skill body injection, browser CDP text, sub-agent structured outcomes. Tests: `test/security/untrusted.test.mjs`, `test/memory/untrusted.test.mjs`. **Encrypted credential storage (shipped MIN-117):** [`server/security/secret-box.js`](../server/security/secret-box.js) — AES-256-GCM envelopes for secrets at rest; file key at `~/.minnow/.key` (`0o600` on Unix). Provider `secrets.json` files migrate from plaintext on first read via [`server/providers/store.js`](../server/providers/store.js). Shared helpers: `readEncryptedJsonFile` / `writeEncryptedJsonFile` for downstream webhook/email/calendar/voice/image stores. **Key loss:** deleting or rotating `.key` makes encrypted secrets unrecoverable — re-enter credentials in Settings → Providers. Tests: `test/security/secret-box.test.mjs`, `test/security/secret-migration.test.mjs`. **Semantic memory + embeddings (shipped MIN-122):** hybrid keyword/vector retrieval with local `@xenova/transformers` embedder (optional provider `/v1/embeddings`), JSON vector sidecar (`server/memory/vector-store.js`), CRUD sync (`server/memory/vector-sync.js`), reindex API, Settings → Memory embeddings panel. Plan: [`documentation/plans/Odysseus port/odysseus-port-01-semantic-memory.md`](plans/Odysseus%20port/odysseus-port-01-semantic-memory.md). **Memory synthesis + skill auto-learning (shipped MIN-127):** post-turn async extraction proposes durable memories and reusable skills into review queues (`~/.minnow/memory/proposals.json`, `~/.minnow/skills/proposals.json`); accept flows call `createEntry()` and `saveUserSkillContent()`. Default is suggest-and-confirm — nothing auto-saves. Server: `server/memory/synthesis.js`, `server/memory/skill-synthesis.js`, `server/memory/synthesis-routes.js`, `server/memory/proposals.js`, `server/skills/proposals.js`. Client hook: `src/tools/loop.ts` → fire-and-forget `POST /api/memory/synthesis/run` after normal completion (throttled every N message pairs; skill gate: `round_count >= 2` OR `tool_count >= 2`). Review UI: Settings → Memory → **Auto-learning cadence** (`src/ui/settings-memory-synthesis.ts`, throttle + enable) and **Suggested memories & skills** (`src/ui/memory-proposals-panel.ts`); source excerpts wrapped with #13 untrusted fences. Config: `config.json` → `synthesis` (enabled, confidence threshold, utility model overrides). Plan: [`documentation/plans/Odysseus port/odysseus-port-08-memory-skill-synthesis.md`](plans/Odysseus%20port/odysseus-port-08-memory-skill-synthesis.md). Tests: `test/memory/synthesis-parse.test.mjs`, `test/memory/proposals.test.mjs`, `test/skills/skill-synthesis.test.mjs`. **Voice I/O (MIN-123 + built-in voice Phases 1–5, shipped):** provider-backed STT dictation + TTS read-aloud; **Phase 1** extends `config.json` → `voice` with nested `audio`, `stt` (`local` Whisper + `provider` API), and `tts` (`local` Qwen3-TTS + `provider` + `browser`) blocks ([`src/config/voice-meta.ts`](../src/config/voice-meta.ts), [`server/config/validators.js`](../server/config/validators.js)); GPU-first defaults (`whisper-base` / `whisper-small`, Qwen 0.6B CustomVoice). **Settings → Audio** ([`src/ui/settings-audio.ts`](../src/ui/settings-audio.ts)) — mic/speaker devices + capture constraints; **Models → Voice** ([`src/ui/models/voice-panel.ts`](../src/ui/models/voice-panel.ts), `#/app/models/voice`) — runtime card + STT/TTS catalogs/downloads/settings + mic/TTS test; legacy `#/settings/voice` redirects via [`src/os/router.ts`](../src/os/router.ts). **Phase 2** — Python provisioner ([`server/voice/provision.js`](../server/voice/provision.js)), worker skeleton, lifecycle [`server/voice/runtime-manager.js`](../server/voice/runtime-manager.js), runtime APIs. **Phase 3** — Whisper HF downloads ([`server/voice/download.js`](../server/voice/download.js) → `~/.minnow/models/voice/`, manifest `~/.minnow/voice/installed.json`), local STT bridge ([`server/voice/local-stt.js`](../server/voice/local-stt.js)), worker STT routes ([`server/voice/python/worker.py`](../server/voice/python/worker.py)), STT settings UI ([`src/voice/settings-form.ts`](../src/voice/settings-form.ts), [`src/voice/voice-fit.ts`](../src/voice/voice-fit.ts)), download client ([`src/voice/api-client.ts`](../src/voice/api-client.ts)). **Phase 4** — Qwen3-TTS HF downloads (auto Tokenizer-12Hz dependency), local TTS bridge ([`server/voice/local-tts.js`](../server/voice/local-tts.js)), worker TTS routes + ref upload APIs, mode-gated TTS settings UI, composer/voice-controls backend routing + audio device ids. **Phase 5** — Settings voice panel removed (Models → Voice only); provider backend preserved when no local models installed; `test/voice/refs.test.mjs`. Plan: [`documentation/plans/built-in-voice-system.md`](plans/built-in-voice-system.md). Tests: `test/stt/transcribe.test.mjs`, `test/tts/synthesize.test.mjs`, `test/voice/*.test.mjs`, `test/ui/voice-controls.test.mts`. **Blind A/B model compare (shipped MIN-119):** MinnowOS **Compare** app (`#/app/compare`) — **2–6 models** per run with **Parallel / Sequential** toggle (parallel kicks off all generations via `Promise.all`; sequential activates one blind slot at a time through `POST /api/compare/:id/activate-slot/:index`). Dual+ hidden-identity streams, per-slot vote buttons + Tie / All bad, reveal, win-rate history; response panes render streamed prose through [`setAssistantBubbleContent`](../src/markdown/renderer.ts) (same GFM markdown + syntax highlighting as chat bubbles, per-column debounce while streaming). Model pickers use shared [`fillProviderSelect` / `fillModelSelect`](../src/ui/settings-model-binding.ts) (humanized model labels via [`formatModelLabel`](../src/lib/format-model-label.ts)); provider rows use `.settings-select`, model rows use the same custom combobox list as the top-bar picker ([`mountAuxiliaryModelSelectCombobox`](../src/ui/model-select-picker.ts)). Call `listProviders()` on open — do not gate on `isProvidersApiAvailable()` before the first fetch. Server blind sessions: [`server/compare/`](../../server/compare/) (`POST /api/compare/start` with `{ models[], parallel? }`, `POST /api/compare/:id/activate-slot/:index`, `POST /api/compare/:id/vote`, `GET /api/compare/history`, column streams `/api/compare/:id/stream/:index` plus legacy `left`/`right`). Client: [`src/ui/compare-page.ts`](../src/ui/compare-page.ts), [`src/compare/persistence.ts`](../src/compare/persistence.ts), [`src/compare/win-rates.ts`](../src/compare/win-rates.ts), [`src/compare/continue-in-chat.ts`](../src/compare/continue-in-chat.ts) (**MIN-169:** per-column **Continue in chat** after a run settles — seeds a new chat with the chosen model, original prompt + other responses as context, and the selected response as the opening assistant turn; closes Compare and opens desktop chat like Research **Discuss**). **Launcher icon:** raster glyph at [`public/icons/compare.png`](../public/icons/compare.png), rendered via [`createAppIcon`](../src/os/icons.ts) (`mn-os-icon-raster` CSS mask tinted with `currentColor` / accent on dock tiles and compare page header). Parallel preference: `localStorage` key `minnow.compare.parallel`. Persistence: `~/.minnow/compare/history.json` + active `sessions.json`. Plan: [`documentation/plans/Odysseus port/odysseus-port-03-ab-compare.md`](plans/Odysseus%20port/odysseus-port-03-ab-compare.md). Tests: `test/compare/*.test.mjs`, `test/compare/*.test.mts`, `test/os/compare-app.test.mts`. **Models app (MIN-115, shipped M1–M4):** MinnowOS **Models** app (`#/app/models/<section>`) — hardware-aware recommendations, downloads, local serve, voice I/O models shell, and consolidated provider/model settings. Sections: `recommend`, `installed`, `settings`, `voice`, `providers`, `routing`, `sampler`, `thinking`, `usage`. Hardware probe: `GET /api/system/hardware` ([`server/system/hardware.js`](../server/system/hardware.js), 24h cache, `?fresh=1` rescan). Fit scoring ports Odysseus `fit.py` / `models.py` verbatim constants in [`src/models/fit.ts`](../src/models/fit.ts) + bundled [`src/models/catalog.json`](../src/models/catalog.json). **M3 download:** `POST /api/models/download`, SSE `GET /api/models/download/:id/stream`, `POST /api/models/download/:id/cancel`, `GET /api/models/downloads` + `GET /api/models/installed` — jobs persist at `~/.minnow/models/downloads.json`, artifacts under `~/.minnow/models/artifacts/` ([`server/models/download.js`](../server/models/download.js), [`server/models/hf-client.js`](../server/models/hf-client.js)). **M4 serve:** `GET /api/models/runtimes`, `POST /api/models/serve`, `POST /api/models/serve/:id/stop` — llama.cpp via `llama-server` + auto provider registration ([`server/models/serve.js`](../server/models/serve.js), [`server/models/runtime-detect.js`](../server/models/runtime-detect.js)); Ollama/LM Studio register existing endpoints when reachable. UI: [`src/ui/models-page.ts`](../src/ui/models-page.ts), recommend + installed panels ([`src/ui/models/recommend-panel.ts`](../src/ui/models/recommend-panel.ts), [`src/ui/models/installed-panel.ts`](../src/ui/models/installed-panel.ts)). Legacy `#/settings/{providers,model-routing,usage,sampler,thinking}` redirects to Models sections via [`src/os/router.ts`](../src/os/router.ts). Tests: `test/os/models-app.test.mts`, `test/system/hardware.test.mjs`, `test/models/fit.test.mts`, `test/models/validate.test.mjs`, `test/models/models-api.test.mjs`. **Scheduled tasks + reminders (shipped MIN-120):** MinnowOS **Scheduler** app (`#/app/scheduler`) — menubar calendar-clock icon (left of notifications) + dock tile; local recurring agent jobs under `~/.minnow/scheduler.json` (prompts encrypted at rest via secret-box); interval (`60s` minimum) and five-field cron schedules; 15s server tick + global concurrency cap of 2; headless execution via `node bin/minnow.mjs run --json` subprocess (per-job `workspacePath` or default `~/.minnow/scheduler-workspace`; optional per-job `providerId`/`modelId` in the editor with menubar-default fallback — runner resolves missing model from `sessions/state.json` active chat via [`server/scheduler/resolve-job-model.js`](../server/scheduler/resolve-job-model.js)); run history in `~/.minnow/scheduler-runs/<jobId>.json`; in-app reminders via `~/.minnow/scheduler-notifications.json` + client poll → `noteAgentMessage('scheduler', …)`. **Jobs only run while Minnow is open** (`npm start` / desktop shell). Server: [`server/scheduler/`](../server/scheduler/). UI: [`src/ui/scheduler-page.ts`](../src/ui/scheduler-page.ts), [`src/ui/scheduler/scheduler-panel.ts`](../src/ui/scheduler/scheduler-panel.ts) (multi-provider model picker via [`populateMultiProviderModelSelect`](../src/api/models.ts)), icon [`public/icons/scheduler.svg`](../public/icons/scheduler.svg) (inline SVG in [`src/os/icons.ts`](../src/os/icons.ts)). Legacy `#/settings/scheduler` redirects to the app. Plan: [`documentation/plans/Odysseus port/odysseus-port-05-scheduler-reminders.md`](plans/Odysseus%20port/odysseus-port-05-scheduler-reminders.md). Tests: `test/scheduler/*.test.mjs`, `test/os/scheduler-app.test.mts`, `npm run test:scheduler`. **Calendar, CalDAV, and ICS (shipped MIN-129):** MinnowOS **Calendar** app (`#/app/calendar`) — SQLite store at `~/.minnow/calendar/calendar.db`, month/week UI, `.ics` import/export + RRULE expansion, encrypted CalDAV accounts (#12), bidirectional sync via `tsdav`, `manage_calendar` agent tool, and upcoming-event reminders via scheduler notification queue (`GET /api/calendar/upcoming`). Server: [`server/calendar/`](../server/calendar/). UI: [`src/ui/calendar-page.ts`](../src/ui/calendar-page.ts). Plan: [`documentation/plans/Odysseus port/odysseus-port-10-calendar.md`](plans/Odysseus%20port/odysseus-port-10-calendar.md). Tests: `npm run test:calendar`. **Email integration (shipped MIN-126):** MinnowOS **Email** app (`#/app/email`) — read-only IMAP triage first, then AI summaries/tags, agent tools, draft replies, and explicit-user-action SMTP send. Encrypted account passwords via #12 at `~/.minnow/email/secrets/`; account registry at `~/.minnow/email/accounts.json`; message cache at `~/.minnow/email/cache/<accountId>/`. Server: [`server/email/`](../server/email/) (`imapflow` + `mailparser` fetch, `nodemailer` send). Email bodies wrapped with #13 `wrapUntrusted(…, { source: 'email' })` before LLM triage ([`server/email/triage.js`](../server/email/triage.js)). Agent tools: `list_mail`, `draft_reply` (no auto-send). Opt-in background polling per account ([`server/email/poller.js`](../server/email/poller.js)). **Outlook caveat:** v1 uses IMAP/SMTP basic auth — many Outlook tenants block this; Graph/OAuth is future work. UI: [`src/ui/email-page.ts`](../src/ui/email-page.ts), [`src/email/client.ts`](../src/email/client.ts). Plan: [`documentation/plans/Odysseus port/odysseus-port-09-email.md`](plans/Odysseus%20port/odysseus-port-09-email.md). Tests: `npm run test:email`. **Outgoing webhooks (shipped MIN-118):** HMAC-signed JSON POST deliveries for \chat.completed\, \session.created\, and \scheduler.job_completed\ (scheduler hook reserved). Server: [\server/webhooks/\](../../server/webhooks/) — encrypted signing secrets at \~/.minnow/webhooks/secrets/<id>.json\ (#12 \writeEncryptedJsonFile\), subscriptions at \~/.minnow/webhooks.json\, bounded delivery log at \~/.minnow/webhooks-deliveries.json\. SSRF guard resolves DNS and blocks private/link-local addresses at save and delivery time (\server/webhooks/ssrf.js\). Signing headers: \X-Minnow-Event\, \X-Minnow-Delivery\, \X-Minnow-Timestamp\, \X-Minnow-Signature\ (\sha256=\ HMAC over \{timestamp}.{rawJsonBody}\). APIs: \GET/POST /api/webhooks/subscriptions\, \PUT/DELETE /api/webhooks/subscriptions/:id\, \POST …/test\, \GET /api/webhooks/deliveries\, \POST /api/webhooks/events/session-created\. Event sources: \markComplete()\ in [\server/generations/store.js\](../server/generations/store.js) (\chat.completed\ with \chatId\ from generation create), client [otifySessionCreated\](../src/webhooks/client.ts) on new chat. Settings UI: **Webhooks** (\src/ui/settings-webhooks.ts\, \#/settings/webhooks\). Dev-only local targets: \config.json\ → \webhooks.allowLocalHttp: true\ for \http://127.0.0.1\. Plan: [\documentation/plans/Odysseus port/odysseus-port-06-webhooks.md\](plans/Odysseus%20port/odysseus-port-06-webhooks.md). Tests: \	est/webhooks/*.test.mjs\.

## MinnowOS shell (desktop launcher)

Shipped on branch `cursor/minnowos-redesign-30a6`. **Desktop window shell (Phases 1–6 shipped):** full plan and completion checklist in [`documentation/plans/desktop-window-shell.md`](plans/desktop-window-shell.md). Wraps the SPA in an OS-style shell: menubar + concierge input (brand mark uses [`MINNOW_GLYPH_HEADER_HTML`](../src/ui/minnow-glyph.ts) — same filled fish glyph as the legacy topbar `logo-mark`), desktop (concierge hero; bottom **dock** launcher in [`dock-launcher.ts`](../src/os/dock-launcher.ts) on `#osDockLayer`, always visible on desktop, fully hidden during **immersive** surfaces — fullscreen presentation apps (`Code`, `Email`) and maximized floating windows — via [`shell-chrome.ts`](../src/os/shell-chrome.ts) + `.mn-os-stage.is-immersive-app`; non-immersive in-app surfaces still use the up-arrow reveal tab and down-arrow hide control (chat composer gets extra bottom padding in app view so the reveal tab does not overlap the input — `minnowos-shell.css`); dock tiles get `is-open` / `is-active` classes and a small open dot (`.mn-os-tile-open-dot`) when an app instance (or desktop research / research library window / desktop experts) is running or foreground; legacy `grid`/`concierge` `desktopLayout` values auto-migrate to `dock`), and hash routes `#/desktop` / `#/app/{code|bench|compare|models|brain|scheduler|calendar|email|settings}` (`#/app/chat`, `#/research`, `#/app/research`, `#/experts`, and `#/app/experts` redirect to `#/desktop` + desktop chat/research/experts).

### Desktop shell architecture (window manager + embedded surfaces)

**Stage layers** inside `#osStage` ([`shell.ts`](../src/os/shell.ts)):

| Layer | Element | Contents |
|-------|---------|----------|
| Desktop | `#osDesktopLayer` | Wallpaper, hero greeting, desktop chat rail + transcript, composer dock, research card overlay, Experts' Lab hub overlay |
| Windows | `#osWindowsLayer` | Floating `WindowFrame` instances (Settings from desktop/dock, Models, Brain, Bench, Compare, Calendar; scheduler job editor; calendar event editor) |
| Side panels | `#osSidePanelsLayer` | Scheduler task list rail (~320–360px) |
| Fullscreen apps | `#osAppsLayer` | Code, Email, and **Settings when opened from Code** (`.is-in-app-fullscreen`) |

**Z-order (bottom → top):** wallpaper → floating transcript → collapsed chat rail tab → windows → expanded chat rail drawer → side panel → composer dock → menubar.

**Presentation modes** ([`app-registry.ts`](../src/os/app-registry.ts) + [`types.ts`](../src/os/types.ts) + [`presentation-mode.ts`](../src/os/presentation-mode.ts)): `fullscreen` | `window` | `desktop` | `sidePanel`. Settings defaults to `window`, but when launched while **Code** is foreground it resolves to **fullscreen** via `LaunchOptions.returnToApp: 'code'` ([`instances.ts`](../src/os/instances.ts), [`router.ts`](../src/os/router.ts), [`settings-page.ts`](../src/ui/settings-page.ts) — topbar gear, menubar gear, and in-composer deep links); closing Settings (back button or `closeSettings`) restores the Code workspace hash/section instead of the desktop. [`app-host.ts`](../src/os/app-host.ts) routes each app; [`window-mounted-apps.ts`](../src/os/window-mounted-apps.ts) mounts window-mode content inside [`window-manager.ts`](../src/os/window-manager.ts) + [`window-frame.ts`](../src/os/window-frame.ts) with edge snap ([`window-snap.ts`](../src/os/window-snap.ts)); bounds persist in `localStorage` key `minnow.os.windows`. A `ResizeObserver` on `#osStage` calls `windowManager.reclampWindowsToStage()` so floating / snapped / maximized windows stay inside the viewport when the Electron shell is resized. Non-maximized floating windows clamp above the dock via [`window-stage-bounds.ts`](../src/os/window-stage-bounds.ts) (measures `#osDockLayer` panel top or falls back to desktop CSS tokens); maximize / immersive surfaces still fill the stage and hide the dock. **`WindowFrame.focus()`** (MIN-179) keeps DOM focus on inputs / CodeMirror inside the window body when raising z-order so keyboard entry is not lost until the user alt-tabs away. Titlebar minimize / maximize / close buttons render inline SVG glyphs via [`createOsIcon`](../src/os/icons.ts) (`minimize`, `maximize`, `close`). **App launcher icons** use [`createAppIcon`](../src/os/icons.ts), which renders a PNG mask (`background-color: currentColor` + `mask-image`, `.mn-os-icon-raster`) for ids in `RASTER_ICON_SRC` — currently **Compare** (`/icons/compare.png`) and **Brain** (`/icons/brain-circuit.png`) — and falls back to inline SVG `PATHS` otherwise. **`windowManager.open({ activate: false })`** is used during app-host sync so re-mounting multiple floating windows does not briefly steal focus from the foreground instance; only the explicit foreground pass raises z-order. **Minimize:** titlebar `_` toggles `is-minimized` (hidden on `#osWindowsLayer`); sync must not undo minimize — `open({ activate: false })` skips restore, while user re-launch via [`launchInstance`](../src/os/instances.ts) restores minimized window-mode apps. Open apps (including minimized window-mode apps) show an open dot on the dock tile; open windows stay on `#osWindowsLayer` only.

**Desktop workspace panels (MIN-288):** Right-edge launcher tabs on `#osDesktopLayer` (mirroring the left chat rail) for **Files**, **Browser**, and **File preview** — [`desktop-workspace-rail.ts`](../src/os/desktop-workspace-rail.ts), [`desktop-workspace-state.ts`](../src/os/desktop-workspace-state.ts), [`desktop-workspace-mounts.ts`](../src/os/desktop-workspace-mounts.ts), [`desktop-workspace-rail-resize.ts`](../src/os/desktop-workspace-rail-resize.ts), styles [`desktop-workspace-rail.css`](../src/styles/desktop-workspace-rail.css). When the drawer is expanded, edge tabs hide; a **segmented header** (`.mn-os-workspace-rail-drawer-segments`) switches between Files / Browser / File preview without collapsing. Workspace path shows in the header meta row on the Files tab only. Reparents `#fileSidebarFilesView`, `#previewPane`, and `#fileViewerPane` from hidden `#appBody` into drawer hosts when the desktop view is foreground (not only `chatActive`); restores on Code foreground. On restore, each node re-applies its original Code `.hidden` state (only preview/viewer panes — not `#fileSidebarFilesView`, which must stay visible in the Code sidebar). Clicking a file in the tree switches to the **File preview** tab via [`openFileInViewer`](../src/ui/file-viewer.ts) + `isDesktopWorkspaceHostingActive()`. Git-panel worktree sync ([`syncFileTreeToPanelWorktree`](../src/ui/file-tree.ts)) is a no-op while the desktop drawer hosts the tree so Code cwd cannot overwrite the desktop listing root. Drawer width defaults to **560px** (up from 380px) and is **draggable** on the left edge (persisted in `localStorage` as `minnow.os.desktopWorkspaceDrawerWidth`). **`syncDesktopWorkspaceMounts()`** sets the file-tree listing root to **`~/.minnow/workspace`** on desktop (Code project path on Code foreground) via [`file-tree-listing-root.ts`](../src/ui/file-tree-listing-root.ts) and refreshes the tree when the root changes. Desktop chat sessions and tools scope to **`~/.minnow/workspace`** ([`desktop-workspace.ts`](../src/lib/desktop-workspace.ts), [`desktop-chat-sessions.ts`](../src/os/desktop-chat-sessions.ts)); legacy Chat app keeps `~/.minnow/chats`. Electron preview host visibility includes the desktop browser drawer ([`preview-electron-visibility.ts`](../src/ui/preview-electron-visibility.ts) — active when `#desktopPreviewMount.is-active`, not only Code foreground); `browser_navigate` **awaits** opening the browser drawer + guest layout sync via [`revealPreviewPanelForAgentNavigation`](../src/ui/preview-panel.ts) before `navigateAndWait`. Plan: [`documentation/plans/MIN-288-desktop-file-viewer-browser.md`](plans/MIN-288-desktop-file-viewer-browser.md).

**Desktop chat** ([`desktop-state.ts`](../src/os/desktop-state.ts)): `idle` → `chatActive` on first send or `launchApp('chat')`; session rail ([`desktop-chat-rail.ts`](../src/ui/desktop-chat-rail.ts)) is a **left-edge overlay** on `#osDesktopLayer`: the collapsed chat-icon tab (`#btnDesktopChatRailToggle`) is visible on the desktop (idle, research, experts, and chat modes) but stacks **below** floating windows (`z-index: 12` vs `#osWindowsLayer` at `15`); it is hidden during immersive surfaces (fullscreen presentation apps and maximized windows — see [`shell-chrome.ts`](../src/os/shell-chrome.ts)); when expanded the drawer raises above windows (`z-index: 20`) — Code-style sidebar (`Chats` header, collapse chevron, `+ New chat`, full `chat-list` rows) without the tab beside the panel — opening the rail or picking a session from idle calls `activateDesktopChat` so the transcript appears; transcript `#desktopChatCol`, docked composer in [`concierge.ts`](../src/os/concierge.ts) (`.mn-os-desktop-input-row`: attach + mic + textarea + context ring + send; textarea auto-grows up to **8 lines** then scrolls — [`autoResizeDesktopComposer`](../src/os/desktop-chat.ts); mic is chromeless until hover/recording; send uses [`MINNOW_GLYPH_HEADER_HTML`](../src/ui/minnow-glyph.ts) with a swim animation while desktop chat streams — `syncDesktopComposerFishSwim()` in [`composer-send.ts`](../src/ui/composer-send.ts)); submit/Enter routing is owned by the concierge (`renderConcierge` → `submit`) — [`wireDesktopComposerControls`](../src/os/desktop-chat.ts) wires attach/voice/context only; seeded hero sends call `activateDesktopChat({ seed })`, which creates a **fresh assistant thread** in [`bootstrapDesktopChat`](../src/os/desktop-chat.ts) before auto-sending via `applyDesktopSeed`; FLIP animation [`composer-motion.ts`](../src/os/composer-motion.ts). Chat removed from dock — desktop **is** chat. When idle, `.mn-os-desktop-chat` is `position: absolute` (out of flex flow) so `.mn-os-desk-hero` centers on the stage; when `is-chat-active`, the hero is clipped out of flow, `.mn-os-desk-stage` uses top/bottom insets (`--os-chat-stage-top`, `--os-chat-composer-reserve`) so `.mn-os-chat-transcript` scrolls between the menubar and the floating composer without overlapping the dock (`minnowos-desktop.css`). Docked composer bottom offset (`--os-composer-dock-bottom`) is derived from dock panel inset + dock block height + gap—not `16vh`. Transcript layout: centered `760px` column (`.mn-os-chat-col`) with flex message rhythm (`--os-chat-message-gap`, default `14px`), `20px` horizontal scrollport padding, and `--os-chat-transcript-bottom` (matches composer reserve) so the last bubble clears the docked composer. **Mobile (≤640px):** dock scrolls horizontally; app grid is single-column; composer and menubar respect safe areas; dock reveal tab and panel lift above the iOS home indicator (`env(safe-area-inset-bottom)`); transcript and Chat app scrollports reserve space for the dock reveal + composer so the last message is not clipped; session rail opens as a fixed drawer with `.mn-os-chat-rail-backdrop` dismiss ([`desktop-chat-rail.ts`](../src/ui/desktop-chat-rail.ts)); menubar uses flex overflow containment; hides brand label, status chrome (app view), status text, clock, workspace path label, and scheduler (≤400px); Desktop + app name collapse to icons only (`minnowos-shell.css`).

**Desktop research** ([`research-desktop.ts`](../src/os/research-desktop.ts)): `researchIdle` / `researchActive`; dock Research queues activation even with no `LaunchOptions` (boolean queue flag in [`desktop-state.ts`](../src/os/desktop-state.ts)); entering research docks the desktop composer with placeholder “What would you like to research?” and swaps hero copy to **Deep research.** + research subline (restored on exit); **composer toolbar** (`.mn-os-composer-research-actions`) exposes **Rounds** (`#desktopResearchMaxRounds` — Auto/1–5, maps to `maxRounds` on start; Auto → engine cap 8) and **Scope** (`#desktopResearchScope` — Web / Codebase / Web + Codebase); both disable while a run is active; **Library** opens [`library-window.ts`](../src/research/library-window.ts) as a floating OS window (`RESEARCH_LIBRARY_INSTANCE_ID`, `manageInstance: false`) via the control in `.mn-os-composer-research-actions`; opening a saved report from the library promotes to `researchActive` (hides hero, scrollable result card between menubar and composer); on `researchActive`, `.mn-os-desktop-research` participates in `.mn-os-desk-stage` flex (not absolute `inset: 0`) so `#desktopResearchResultMount` caps at the stage and `.dr-report` scrolls internally; stage bottom clearance uses `--os-research-composer-reserve` (chat reserve + Library toolbar row); **Cancel** sits in the progress card chrome (`#desktopResearchProgressMount` → `#desktopResearchProgressBody` for the stepper); **Close** dismisses the result card (`#desktopResearchResultMount` chrome → `#desktopResearchResultBody` for the report, returns to `researchIdle`); progress + result cards on `researchActive`; `openResearch` / topbar button route through `launchApp('research')` so Code view navigates to desktop; `research-page.css` loads via [`shell.ts`](../src/os/shell.ts).

**Desktop experts** ([`experts-desktop.ts`](../src/os/experts-desktop.ts)): `expertsIdle` / `expertsActive`; dock **Experts** enters **idle** first — hero shows a grid of specialist buttons (`#desktopExpertsHeroMount`) plus **Open Experts' Lab** / **New expert**; clicking a specialist starts an expert sandbox chat; **Open Lab** promotes to `expertsActive` and mounts `#expertsView` in `#desktopExpertsMount` (full roster/detail/create UI); lab header **Close** (after **New expert**) demotes to idle hero; `#/experts` / `#/app/experts` → `#/desktop` + queue activation; composer placeholder “Message an expert…”; hero copy **Experts' Lab.** + picker subline; `openLab` / create/edit steps open the panel immediately; [`app-host.ts`](../src/os/app-host.ts) does **not** activate `#expertsView` in `#osAppsLayer` when returning to desktop (desktop overlay owns experts UI; only preserves `is-open` when the lab panel is reparented to `#desktopExpertsMount`); **New chat** from the lab opens expert threads on the **desktop chat** surface; docked composer uses `pointer-events: none` on `.mn-os-composer-dock` with interactive children only so lab **Edit** / **Delete** and chat-row actions stay clickable above the composer reserve; styles in `minnowos-desktop.css` + `experts-hub.css`.

**Scheduler side panel** ([`scheduler-side-panel.ts`](../src/os/scheduler-side-panel.ts)): list-only panel; add/edit via [`job-editor-window.ts`](../src/ui/scheduler/job-editor-window.ts) auxiliary window (`SCHEDULER_EDITOR_INSTANCE_ID`). Stays open while a scheduler instance exists and any non-Code app is foreground (window apps, desktop chat/research/experts, or bare desktop); hides only for fullscreen Code or when the scheduler instance is closed.

**Calendar window** ([`calendar-constants.ts`](../src/os/calendar-constants.ts)): default 960×640, compact toolbar + collapsible rail + scrollable grid; event editor child window ([`event-editor-window.ts`](../src/ui/calendar/event-editor-window.ts)).

**Legacy `#chatView`:** DOM retained for non-OS mode and notification deep-links; desktop chat (`#desktopChatCol`) is primary when the OS shell is enabled. Full removal deferred — see [`desktop-window-shell.md`](plans/desktop-window-shell.md) known items. **Page bridge** ([`page-bridge.ts`](../src/os/page-bridge.ts)): `shouldHideAppBody()` keeps `#appBody` visible when Code is the foreground fullscreen app even if desktop chat/research/experts mode is still active (desktop state is preserved when returning from Code). `#osDesktopLayer.is-suppressed-by-fullscreen-app` and `.mn-os-stage.is-immersive-app` (via [`shell-chrome.ts`](../src/os/shell-chrome.ts)) hide the dock launcher, docked desktop composer, chat rail, and hero during immersive surfaces — fullscreen presentation apps and maximized floating windows — research-mode composer `z-index: 40` previously stacked above the apps layer. **Code session restore** ([`code-launch.ts`](../src/os/code-launch.ts) `restoreCodeSessionOnForeground()`): opening Code without launch options switches off the desktop assistant thread (`~/.minnow/workspace`) back to the remembered project-workspace chat and re-renders `#chatArea`; [`desktop-chat.ts`](../src/os/desktop-chat.ts) calls `rememberWorkspaceActiveChat()` before activating assistant chat so the handoff is persisted in `lastActiveChatIdByWorkspace`.

**Tests:** `test/os/window-manager.test.mts`, `window-apps.test.mts`, `code-app-settings.test.mts`, `presentation-mode.test.mts`, `desktop-chat-state.test.mts`, `desktop-workspace-rail.test.mts`, `desktop-research-state.test.mts`, `desktop-experts-state.test.mts`, `page-bridge-code-foreground.test.mts`, `scheduler-app.test.mts`, `calendar-app.test.mts`, `router.test.mts`. **Instance focus on close:** [`closeInstance`](../src/os/instances.ts) → `pickNextForeground` only rotates among `window` / `sidePanel` instances, except when the closing app has `launchOptions.returnToApp` (Settings-from-Code returns to the Code instance); closing the last floating app returns to desktop instead of resurrecting a background fullscreen Code session.

**Desktop launcher** ([`concierge.ts`](../src/os/concierge.ts)): the docked composer always starts a **normal desktop chat** on submit (`activateDesktopChat({ seed })`); no LLM app routing or quick-start chips. Once Chat or Research is active, submit delegates to `handleDesktopSend` / `handleDesktopResearchSubmit`. **Composer model label** ([`composer-model-trigger.ts`](../src/ui/composer-model-trigger.ts), [`composer-model-trigger.css`](../src/styles/composer-model-trigger.css)): compact producer logo + model name + chevron above the desktop composer (right-aligned) and to the left of the Code composer tools button in `.composer-controls__trail` (trail is right-aligned on the full `.input-bar` width — `#composerControls` spans the send column via the shared input-bar CSS grid in [`input.css`](../src/styles/input.css)); **desktop/Code variants show and edit the active chat’s model**; the **menubar default-model chip** ([`menubar-model-chip.ts`](../src/os/menubar-model-chip.ts)) shows and edits the global default only; opens a fixed popover (positioned above the composer trigger, flipping below only when clipped) wired to the shared `#modelSelect` catalog (search + All / Local / Cloud filter + loaded-only toggle + refresh / load-unload icon actions in [`model-select-picker.ts`](../src/ui/model-select-picker.ts) + grouped rows). Syncs via `minnow:model-select-synced` and `#modelSelect` change events. Agent-driven app switches use **`launch_minnow_app`** in chat (offer first, switch only after user confirms — see [`launch-minnow-app.md`](../src/chat/prompts/tool-usage/launch-minnow-app.md)). **Menubar status pill** (`.mn-os-mb-status`, `#osStatusDot` / `#osStatusText`): in `.mn-os-mb-left` after `.mn-os-mb-status-sep` (`.mn-os-mb-app-sep` before app name in app view only); on desktop: **Minnow** `|` status; in app view: **Code** `|` status; mirrors the legacy `.status-pill` (`#sDot` / `#sText`); [`setStatus`](../src/ui/status.ts) updates both so Ready / loading / error feedback stays visible in the menubar while the topbar is hidden. The popover includes a **search field**, **All / Local / Cloud** host filter, and when **Local** is selected a **loaded-only** dot toggle ([`model-select-picker.ts`](../src/ui/model-select-picker.ts); preferences `minnow-model-host-filter`, `minnow-model-local-load-filter`; search is ephemeral per menu open) that filters rows by `data-provider-host` on each `<option>` (derived from provider `baseUrl` in [`format-model-label.ts`](../src/lib/format-model-label.ts) via [`provider-host.ts`](../src/providers/provider-host.ts)) and by `modelCache` load state for local models. The legacy top-bar picker uses the same filter bar inside `.model-select-popover` (refresh + load/unload icons beside All / Local / Cloud; top-bar text **Load** hidden on narrow viewports). **Menubar notifications bell** (`.mn-os-mb-bell`): session inbox in [`src/notifications/`](../src/notifications/) — [`store.ts`](../src/notifications/store.ts) holds up to 100 `NotificationRecord` rows (kinds: chat turn complete/error/tool failure, orchestrate task/sub-agent lifecycle, scheduler, research, synthesis); [`push.ts`](../src/notifications/push.ts) dedupes, respects prefs, rings the bell (`.is-ringing` animation in `minnowos-shell.css`), and plays optional sounds when the window is unfocused (Electron: even when `visibilityState` is `hidden` after alt-tab; browser: only when the tab stays `visible`); [`initNotificationAudioUnlock`](../src/notifications/sound.ts) unlocks autoplay on the first pointer/keyboard gesture app-wide; popover UI in [`src/os/notifications-menu.ts`](../src/os/notifications-menu.ts) shows title, kind label, preview, and relative time — row click calls [`openNotificationTarget`](../src/notifications/navigate.ts) (Code or Chat app + chat deep-link via [`chat-launch.ts`](../src/os/chat-launch.ts) and `LaunchOptions.chatId` — `appId` resolved by [`app-for-chat.ts`](../src/notifications/app-for-chat.ts) from `~/.minnow/chats` workspace; popover rows show the source app launcher icon from `record.appId`; synthesis opens Settings → Memory). Opening a chat via [`switchChat`](../src/ui/sidebar.ts), [`activateDesktopChatSession`](../src/os/desktop-chat.ts), or notification navigate calls [`acknowledgeChatViewed`](../src/notifications/acknowledge.ts) (`chat.unread` + [`markNotificationsReadForChat`](../src/notifications/store.ts) for that `chatId`). Producers: [`producers.ts`](../src/notifications/producers.ts) + [`chat-turn.ts`](../src/notifications/chat-turn.ts) (`notifyChatTurnEnded` after `finalizeRun` in [`loop.ts`](../src/tools/loop.ts); board/sub-agent via `initNotificationProducers()` in [`main.ts`](../src/main.ts)). Background detection: [`background.ts`](../src/notifications/background.ts) (`shouldNotifyForChatTurn` — alerts when on Desktop, a non-Code app, another chat, or the transcript is not mounted; not the same as `isStreamDomVisible`). Prefs: `localStorage` keys `minnow.notifications.*` ([`prefs.ts`](../src/notifications/prefs.ts)); dropdown **Silence notifications** toggle (`muted`, MIN-249) persists and blocks new inbox rows, bell ring, and sounds until cleared; Settings → General → Notifications ([`settings-notifications.ts`](../src/ui/settings-notifications.ts)). Sounds: `public/sounds/notifications/*.wav` ([`sound.ts`](../src/notifications/sound.ts)). Legacy per-app dock badges still updated via [`noteAgentMessage`](../src/os/instances.ts) from `pushNotification`. Plan: [`documentation/plans/notifications-complete.md`](plans/notifications-complete.md). Tests: `test/notifications/*.test.mjs`. **Menubar clock** (`.mn-os-mb-time`): `startMenubarClock()` in [`menubar.ts`](../src/os/menubar.ts) ticks on minute boundaries and resyncs on `visibilitychange` (replaces the old 30s `setInterval`). **Electron frameless shell (MIN-78):** the desktop `BrowserWindow` drops the native OS title bar — Windows/Linux use `frame: false` + `thickFrame: true`; macOS uses `titleBarStyle: 'hiddenInset'` with `trafficLightPosition` so native traffic lights sit in the menubar inset. Shell minimize / maximize / close ride `window.minnow.window` IPC ([`electron/ipc-channels.ts`](../electron/ipc-channels.ts), [`electron/preload.ts`](../electron/preload.ts), [`electron/main.ts`](../electron/main.ts)); Win/Linux controls mount at the far right of `.mn-os-mb-right` via [`menubar-window-controls.ts`](../src/os/menubar-window-controls.ts) (shared `.mn-os-window-btn` factory in [`window-control-buttons.ts`](../src/os/window-control-buttons.ts)); macOS hides custom controls and pads `.mn-os-mb-left` when `html.is-electron-shell[data-platform='darwin']`. Menubar drag uses `-webkit-app-region: drag` with `no-drag` on interactive children; double-click toggles maximize. Plain browser tabs (`npm start` + external browser) skip frameless chrome. Plan: [`documentation/plans/MIN-78-frameless-shell-header.md`](plans/MIN-78-frameless-shell-header.md). Tests: `test/os/menubar-window-controls.test.mts`. **Code app** reparents existing `#appBody` + topbar + `#welcomeView` into `#osAppsLayer`; the legacy `#topbar` is **hidden** in Code (model picker via composer model trigger; workspace in `.mn-os-mb-right`). `#workspacePathLabel` + `#btnWorkspace` reparent via [`workspace-menubar.ts`](../src/os/workspace-menubar.ts). Mobile chat drawer: `.mn-os-mb-chat-toggle` in the menubar (replaces `#btnSidebarToggle`). When the workspace is still default, `openWelcome()` hides `#appBody` and applies `.welcome-page--os-overlay` so the picker fills the Code app below the menubar only. Settings, Research, Experts, Benchmark, and **Compare** reuse their full-page `<main>` elements inside the app layer. **Blind compare** (`#/compare`, [`src/ui/compare-page.ts`](../src/ui/compare-page.ts)): user picks **2–6 models**, optional **Parallel / Sequential** mode, runs a shared prompt; [`server/compare/store.js`](../server/compare/store.js) randomly assigns picks to screen columns and blind aliases (A/B/C… parallel, 1/2/3… sequential); parallel kickoff uses `Promise.all` on the server, sequential activates slots via `POST /api/compare/:id/activate-slot/:index`; provider/model ids stay hidden until vote via [`/api/compare`](../server/compare/routes.js) redacted SSE streams ([`src/compare/stream.ts`](../src/compare/stream.ts)). Per-column vote buttons plus Tie / All bad; **Continue in chat** on each completed column ([`src/compare/continue-in-chat.ts`](../src/compare/continue-in-chat.ts), MIN-169). History and win rates persist under `~/.minnow/compare/`. Router: [`router.ts`](../src/os/router.ts). Boot: `initOsPageBridge()` → `initOsShell()` → `initOsRouter()` in [`main.ts`](../src/main.ts). CSS: `src/styles/minnowos-*.css`. Design prototype: [`documentation/reference/minnowos/`](../reference/minnowos/). Plan: [`documentation/plans/minnowos-redesign.md`](plans/minnowos-redesign.md). Tests: `test/os/*.test.mts` (includes `code-launch.test.mts`).

**Scheduler app** (`#/app/scheduler`, [`src/ui/scheduler-page.ts`](../src/ui/scheduler-page.ts)): In MinnowOS, opens as a **side panel** on the desktop ([`scheduler-side-panel.ts`](../src/os/scheduler-side-panel.ts)); `initSchedulerPage()` skips `hashchange` in OS mode (router owns `#/app/scheduler` — avoids double `launchApp`). Legacy non-OS mode still uses the full-page `#schedulerPanelMount`. Job list in [`scheduler-panel.ts`](../src/ui/scheduler/scheduler-panel.ts) — uptime notice, mono job/enabled stats, stacked bordered job rows (humanized schedule via [`schedule-display.ts`](../src/scheduler/schedule-display.ts), next run, workspace + model labels, prompt preview, inline actions), sticky **Add task** footer, and run-history table (summary shows `assistantFinal`; rows with a linked `chatId` open the Code app chat via `switchChat`). Create/edit uses [`job-editor-window.ts`](../src/ui/scheduler/job-editor-window.ts) — friendly schedule builder ([`schedule-field.ts`](../src/ui/scheduler/schedule-field.ts): interval shorthand or cron presets + live next-run preview), optional per-job model (multi-provider picker — same combobox UI as the composer model trigger; empty = active session model at run time), and optional per-job workspace (folder picker; unset → `~/.minnow/scheduler-workspace`), teaching empty state. Default workspace: [`server/scheduler-workspace/paths.js`](../server/scheduler-workspace/paths.js) (`GET /api/scheduler/workspace`). Run-time model resolution: [`server/scheduler/resolve-job-model.js`](../server/scheduler/resolve-job-model.js) → explicit job fields or active chat from `~/.minnow/sessions/state.json` (`providerId` + `modelId` on chats preserved by [`validateSessionState`](../server/config/validators.js)). Styles: [`scheduler-page.css`](../src/styles/scheduler-page.css), [`scheduler-side-panel.css`](../src/styles/scheduler-side-panel.css), [`scheduler-editor-window.css`](../src/styles/scheduler-editor-window.css). Persistence: `~/.minnow/scheduler.json` (MIN-120). Manual and scheduled runs spawn [`bin/minnow.mjs`](../bin/minnow.mjs) with `--persist-chat --chat-id <runId> --scheduler-run` so each run transcript lands in `~/.minnow/sessions` ([`src/headless/persist-chat.ts`](../src/headless/persist-chat.ts)); `chatId` is stored on run rows in `~/.minnow/scheduler-runs/`. Headless runs **`await ensureToolConfigReady()`** before building the tool catalog ([`src/headless/runner.ts`](../src/headless/runner.ts)) so scheduler subprocesses load full `tools.json` permissions instead of the small default seed (which previously exposed only `save_memory` / web search). Subprocesses use the Vite-resolved origin from [`server/scheduler/server-base-url.js`](../server/scheduler/server-base-url.js) (`http://localhost:PORT`, not `127.0.0.1` — Vite on Windows often binds IPv6 loopback only).

**Calendar app (MIN-129, shipped; Phase 5b window redesign):** MinnowOS **Calendar** app (`#/app/calendar`, `presentationMode: window`) — local-first SQLite store at `~/.minnow/calendar/calendar.db` ([`server/calendar/store.js`](../server/calendar/store.js)). **Window shell:** default **960×640** centered ([`calendar-constants.ts`](../src/os/calendar-constants.ts)), min **520×400** resize; `launchApp('calendar')` mounts `#calendarView` in `WindowFrame` via [`app-host.ts`](../src/os/app-host.ts) + [`window-mounted-apps.ts`](../src/os/window-mounted-apps.ts). **Window layout** ([`calendar-panel.ts`](../src/ui/calendar/calendar-panel.ts), [`calendar-window.css`](../src/styles/calendar-window.css)): compact toolbar (prev/Today/next · title · Month/Week · **+** · ⋯ overflow menu), collapsible **calendar rail** (48px collapsed / 180px expanded; auto-collapsed under 720px width), scrollable grid (no outer card border); `.calendar-hdr` hidden in window mode. **Event editor** opens as a child window ([`event-editor-window.ts`](../src/ui/calendar/event-editor-window.ts), `CALENDAR_EVENT_EDITOR_INSTANCE_ID`, `manageInstance: false`). Month/week grid — cell-anchored **day popover** for `+N more` and date-label clicks; event chips use tinted background + full border (no left accent stripe). View-specific inline limits via [`src/calendar/prefs.ts`](../src/calendar/prefs.ts) (`minnow.calendar.prefs`); **Settings** overflow modal [`src/ui/calendar/calendar-settings.ts`](../src/ui/calendar/calendar-settings.ts); sidebar **CalDAV accounts** in the collapsible rail. `.ics` import/export ([`server/calendar/ics.js`](../server/calendar/ics.js)), remote iCal URL import ([`server/calendar/ics-feed.js`](../server/calendar/ics-feed.js) — `POST /api/calendar/import/ics-url`), RRULE expansion ([`server/calendar/recurrence.js`](../server/calendar/recurrence.js)), encrypted CalDAV accounts (#12 secret-box at `~/.minnow/calendar/secrets/`), bidirectional sync via `tsdav` ([`server/calendar/caldav.js`](../server/calendar/caldav.js)), SSRF validation ([`server/calendar/caldav-ssrf.js`](../server/calendar/caldav-ssrf.js)), REST `/api/calendar/*` ([`server/calendar/middleware.js`](../server/calendar/middleware.js)), agent tool `manage_calendar` ([`server/calendar/tool-handler.js`](../server/calendar/tool-handler.js)), upcoming-event reminders ([`server/calendar/reminders.js`](../server/calendar/reminders.js)). Client API: [`src/calendar/client.ts`](../src/calendar/client.ts). Styles: [`src/styles/calendar.css`](../src/styles/calendar.css), [`src/styles/calendar-window.css`](../src/styles/calendar-window.css). Tests: `npm run test:calendar`, `test/os/calendar-app.test.mts`.

**Email app (MIN-126 + agent redesign; Phase 0 correctness MIN-350):** MinnowOS **Email** app (`#/app/email`) — **agent-first** UX: **chrome header** ([`src/ui/email/email-panel.ts`](../src/ui/email/email-panel.ts)) — three-zone layout (account identity + mono provider hint, centered segmented view nav with icons, utility icon buttons for sign out/settings/test); default **inbox dashboard** ([`src/ui/email/email-dashboard.ts`](../src/ui/email/email-dashboard.ts)) with digest headline, stats-strip-style triage instrumentation, narrative brief (72ch), divider-based **attention queue** rows (click-through to thread), and **quick reply chips** (send / reprompt); **three-pane mail view** ([`src/ui/email/email-layout.ts`](../src/ui/email/email-layout.ts) — sidebar stays mounted on folder switch; initial load bootstraps folders from account metadata then fetches messages + remote folder list in parallel; list-pane loading overlay instead of blank panes) with **Compose** CTA in folder sidebar (unread badges), icon bulk toolbar (read/unread/flag/archive/delete + search + pagination), list rows with avatars, inline star toggle, attachment icon, and urgency badges; **reading pane** with prominent Reply / Reply all / Forward actions, flag/archive/delete + overflow menu (mark unread, mark spam, move to folder), attachment chips, **Formatted / Plain text** body toggle when both `bodyHtml` and `bodyText` exist ([`src/ui/email/email-body.ts`](../src/ui/email/email-body.ts) — DOMPurify HTML render or plain-text alternative; default formatted), and **inline compose** ([`src/ui/email/email-compose.ts`](../src/ui/email/email-compose.ts) — reply modes, Cc on reply-all/forward (sent end-to-end via SMTP), **Draft / Redraft** in compose header with inline reprompt field, **alt draft chips** above the message body, **selection revise bubble** (Improve / Fix / Shorter / Tune via `POST /api/email/improve-text`), **rich-text toolbar** via [`src/ui/email/email-compose-editor.ts`](../src/ui/email/email-compose-editor.ts) — bold/italic/underline/strike, lists, links; optional `bodyHtml` on send with server sanitization); shared mail icons in [`src/ui/email/email-icons.ts`](../src/ui/email/email-icons.ts); styles in [`src/styles/email.css`](../src/styles/email.css) (`--mn-*`, flat chrome, mail list scroll via `.email-layout` flex chain + `.email-list-rows` overflow, ≤900px list↔reader swap with back button); **automations** builder ([`src/ui/email/email-automations.ts`](../src/ui/email/email-automations.ts)). Orchestrator: [`src/ui/email/email-panel.ts`](../src/ui/email/email-panel.ts) — SSE subscriptions keyed by mount element (no leak on re-render). Server: encrypted accounts ([`server/email/accounts.js`](../server/email/accounts.js); **sign out** via `DELETE /api/email/accounts/:id` removes credentials, local cache, and account automations; promotes another default when needed), **IMAP** sync ([`server/email/transport.js`](../server/email/transport.js); parses `replyTo` header), **mail mutations** ([`server/email/imap-actions.js`](../server/email/imap-actions.js), [`server/email/mail-actions.js`](../server/email/mail-actions.js) — read/unread, flag, archive, move, delete, bulk), cache flags + inbox summary + reply variants + sanitized `bodyHtml` ([`server/email/cache.js`](../server/email/cache.js), [`server/email/sanitize-html.js`](../server/email/sanitize-html.js); re-sync hydrates older rows), **reply headers** ([`server/email/reply-headers.js`](../server/email/reply-headers.js) — `Reply-To` precedence, self-skip, accumulated `References` chain; mirrored in [`src/email/reply-headers.ts`](../src/email/reply-headers.ts)), **agent layer** ([`server/email/agent.js`](../server/email/agent.js) — digest, variants, `runAgentHooksAfterFolderSync` on manual sync + poller for triage/variants/summary backfill; `recordTriageFailure` persists `failedAt` via `updateMessageTriage`; LLM via [`resolveSynthesisModel`](../server/memory/synthesis-config.js): `config.json` synthesis utility → titles model → active chat menubar model (composite select key decoded) → active provider), **compose AI** ([`server/email/compose-ai.js`](../server/email/compose-ai.js) — selection improve), **SSE events** ([`server/email/events.js`](../server/email/events.js) — `GET /api/email/events`), **automations** ([`server/email/automations.js`](../server/email/automations.js) at `~/.minnow/email/automations.json`), poller with auto-triage ([`server/email/poller.js`](../server/email/poller.js)), LLM-assisted draft + explicit-confirm send ([`server/email/smtp.js`](../server/email/smtp.js) — `sendReplyVariant` computes headers from cached thread without an extra LLM call; `cc`/`bcc` on send; draft/variant/compose/triage LLM calls use [`completeStructuredJson`](../server/email/llm-json.js) with `stripProse: true` and a single JSON repair retry; agent backfill caps at **8 messages per sync** (newest UIDs first) with **30-minute cooldown** after triage failures so dashboard load does not hammer the utility model). REST: `/api/email/accounts/:id/summary`, message flags/move/archive/bulk, reply-variants + send, `POST /api/email/draft-reply`, `POST /api/email/improve-text`. Agent tools: `list_mail`, `draft_reply`, `summarize_inbox`, `generate_reply_variants`, `email_action` ([`server/email/tool-handler.js`](../server/email/tool-handler.js)). Client: [`src/email/client.ts`](../src/email/client.ts), [`src/email/client-ext.ts`](../src/email/client-ext.ts). Plan: [`documentation/plans/email-agent-redesign.md`](plans/email-agent-redesign.md), Phase 0 in [`documentation/plans/email-world-class-spec.md`](plans/email-world-class-spec.md). **No auto-send** — user always confirms send (MIN-126 policy). Tests: `npm run test:email` (includes [`test/email/reply-headers.test.mjs`](../test/email/reply-headers.test.mjs), [`test/email/agent-triage-failure.test.mjs`](../test/email/agent-triage-failure.test.mjs)).

**Auto-update (MIN-384):** packaged desktop installs update from GitHub Releases (`DukkyGames/Minnow`) via `electron-updater`. Main-process controller [`electron/updater.ts`](../electron/updater.ts) (check on launch + every 4h, background download, `quitAndInstall` on restart; channel persisted in `~/.minnow/updater.json`, Beta = GitHub pre-releases via `allowPrerelease`) drives the pure state machine in [`electron/updater-core.ts`](../electron/updater-core.ts); background check failures are silent-with-log, manual checks surface inline. **Windows** (unsigned NSIS) and **signed macOS** (Developer ID + notarization — see [`guides/macos-signing.md`](guides/macos-signing.md)) are supported; unsigned macOS builds disable auto-update at runtime via `codesign` inspection. IPC `minnow:updater:*` ([`electron/ipc-channels.ts`](../electron/ipc-channels.ts)) → `window.minnow.updater` ([`electron/preload.ts`](../electron/preload.ts)); renderer wrapper + display copy helpers in [`src/electron/updater-client.ts`](../src/electron/updater-client.ts). UI: **Settings → General → App updates** ([`src/ui/settings-updates.ts`](../src/ui/settings-updates.ts) — status strip, version/check KV rows, Stable/Beta segment, Check + Restart actions, release-notes expander; dev/browser shows a packaged-installs-only hint, unsigned macOS shows a disabled signing note) and a **menubar pill** before the settings gear ([`src/os/update-menubar.ts`](../src/os/update-menubar.ts) — hidden when up to date, `↓ N%` while downloading, accent `Restart · x.y.z` + popover when ready). electron-builder `publish` config emits `latest.yml` / `latest-mac.yml`; local packaging always runs `--publish never` (releases are uploaded manually — see [getting-started.md → Packaging](getting-started.md#packaging-a-desktop-build)). Tests: `test/electron/updater-core.test.mts`, `test/settings/settings-updates.test.mts`.

## Theme system (palette tokens)

Sixteen composed themes on `<html data-theme="{family}-{mode}">` (families: **swamp**, **desert**, **ocean**, **coral**, **mono**, **matrix**, **human**, **mint**; modes: **dark**, **light**). Palette hex/rgba lives only in [`src/styles/tokens.css`](../src/styles/tokens.css); the rest of the app uses **`--mn-*`** CSS variables (22 core tokens per theme plus extended semantics derived with `color-mix`). Renamed families (e.g. `sage`→`swamp`) are migrated on read in [`src/theme.ts`](../src/theme.ts).

| Key | Purpose |
|-----|---------|
| `minnow.theme` | Explicit `ThemeId` (e.g. `swamp-dark`) when not following the OS |
| `minnow.theme.followSystem` | `'1'` when mode tracks `prefers-color-scheme` |
| `minnow.theme.family` | Active family while follow-system is on (default **swamp**); when follow-system is off, `getStoredFamily()` derives from `minnow.theme` so the settings picker stays in sync |

**Runtime:** [`src/theme.ts`](../src/theme.ts) (`getStoredTheme`, `applyTheme`, `setThemeFamily`, `setThemeMode`, `setFollowSystem`, legacy `light`/`dark`/`system` migration). [`src/ui/theme.ts`](../src/ui/theme.ts) syncs highlight.js dark stylesheet, xterm (**full 16-color ANSI palette** via [`src/ui/terminal-xterm-theme.ts`](../src/ui/terminal-xterm-theme.ts) — light mode maps shell colors to dark `--mn-*` inks so PowerShell/npm highlighting stays readable; selection uses `--mn-selection-bg` on dark themes and a stronger accent mix on light PTY canvas), **custom color overrides** ([`src/appearance/custom-theme.ts`](../src/appearance/custom-theme.ts); simplified editor derives full palettes via [`src/appearance/theme-derive.ts`](../src/appearance/theme-derive.ts)), and **font stacks** ([`src/appearance/fonts.ts`](../src/appearance/fonts.ts)); CodeMirror selection uses `--mn-selection-bg` + `minnowEditorColorSchemeTheme` in [`src/ui/codemirror-theme.ts`](../src/ui/codemirror-theme.ts) (overrides CM6 light `#d9d9d9` when `cm-dark` is unset). **Syntax contrast (MIN-243):** light themes map `--cm-attr` and `--mn-syntax-{command,name,inline,link}` to `--mn-accent-ink` / `--mn-fg` in [`tokens.css`](../src/styles/tokens.css); chat/composer consumers include slash-picker command badges ([`skill-picker.css`](../src/styles/skill-picker.css)), inline code + tool titles ([`messages.css`](../src/styles/messages.css)), file-ref names ([`code-ref-link.css`](../src/styles/code-ref-link.css)), and light-mode hljs class remaps in fenced blocks. **FOUC:** inline script in [`index.html`](../index.html) sets `data-theme` and reads custom `--mn-bg` for `theme-color` when custom colors are enabled; critical CSS uses per-family fallbacks until `tokens.css` loads. `applyTheme()` clears any legacy inline tokens. `initTheme()` adds `theme-ready` and removes `theme-no-transition` after first paint. **Caret (MIN-168):** [`src/styles/theme-transitions.css`](../src/styles/theme-transitions.css) omits `color` transitions on text-entry controls (Chromium can stop painting the insertion caret when `color` animates while `caret-color` is `auto`); [`src/styles/global.css`](../src/styles/global.css) sets `caret-color: var(--mn-fg)` on inputs/textareas/contenteditable (composer `#msgInput` also in [`input.css`](../src/styles/input.css); CodeMirror already in [`file-panel.css`](../src/styles/file-panel.css)). Split/Brain resize drags clear `document.body.style.cursor` on `lostpointercapture` / window `blur` ([`init-file-panel.ts`](../src/ui/init-file-panel.ts), [`brain/inspector-resize.ts`](../src/ui/brain/inspector-resize.ts)). **Settings → Appearance:** live bench preview strip, 2×2 theme family grid with window mocks, segmented dark/light toolbar, wallpaper grid (16:10), side-by-side font specimens, collapsible custom token editor with per-token color swatch + alpha sliders ([`src/appearance/color-format.ts`](../src/appearance/color-format.ts), [`src/ui/settings-appearance-colors.ts`](../src/ui/settings-appearance-colors.ts)), and `settings-select` dropdown styling ([`src/styles/settings-appearance.css`](../src/styles/settings-appearance.css)); preset families ([`src/ui/settings-theme.ts`](../src/ui/settings-theme.ts)), font presets/uploads, and desktop wallpaper catalog. **Desktop wallpaper:** [`src/os/wallpaper.ts`](../src/os/wallpaper.ts) — underwater (default), minnow fish ([`minnow-fish.ts`](../src/os/wallpaper/minnow-fish.ts) boids canvas tinted with `--mn-accent` via [`minnow-glyph-white.svg`](../public/logos/minnow-glyph-white.svg)), aurora, starfield, grain, mesh, gradient, flat, custom image (IndexedDB via [`src/appearance/asset-store.ts`](../src/appearance/asset-store.ts)); prefs in `minnow.os.*` ([`src/os/desktop-prefs.ts`](../src/os/desktop-prefs.ts)). Settings/onboarding wallpaper thumbnails call `renderWallpaper({ preview: true })` inside a `.mn-os` preview shell; core `--os-*` tokens are defined on `:root` as well as `.mn-os` ([`minnowos-tokens.css`](../src/styles/minnowos-tokens.css)) so previews render outside the desktop shell. **Reef iframes:** [`src/chat/reef/theme-forward.ts`](../src/chat/reef/theme-forward.ts) forwards `--mn-*`, radii, fonts; watches `data-theme` and inline `style` for custom overrides. **Tests:** `test/theme.test.mts`, `test/theme-contrast.test.mts`, `test/ui/terminal-xterm-theme.test.mts`, `test/appearance/*.test.mts`, `test/chat/reef/theme-forward.test.mts`. Plans: [`documentation/plans/token-theme-system.md`](plans/token-theme-system.md), [`documentation/plans/custom-themes-backgrounds.md`](plans/custom-themes-backgrounds.md). Design source: Color Scheme Exploration (PDF/HTML).

| Key | Purpose (custom appearance) |
|-----|-----------------------------|
| `minnow.appearance.customEnabled` | `'1'` when inline `--mn-*` overrides are active |
| `minnow.appearance.customTokens` | JSON map of core token keys → color values |
| `minnow.appearance.customAdvanced` | `'1'` when the per-token advanced color editor is active (default: simplified four-seed mode) |
| `minnow.appearance.fonts` | `{ ui, mono }` preset or upload refs |
| `minnow.os.wallpaper` | Desktop wallpaper mode id |
| `minnow.os.wallpaperImageId` | IndexedDB asset id when mode is `custom` |
| `minnow.os.wallpaperImageFit` | `cover` or `contain` for custom image |

**Desktop agent tools:** MinnowOS desktop chats (`modeId: desktop`) expose browser-native **`get_appearance`**, **`update_appearance`**, and **`upload_appearance_asset`** ([`src/tools/appearance-tools.ts`](../src/tools/appearance-tools.ts)) — the only supported write path for theme family/mode, custom `--mn-*` tokens (26 core keys in [`CORE_THEME_TOKEN_KEYS`](../src/appearance/types.ts)), font presets/uploads, and desktop wallpaper. The `appearance` tool group is **desktop-only** ([`src/chat/modes/tool-groups.ts`](../src/chat/modes/tool-groups.ts)). `update_settings` appearance keys (`appearance.theme.*`, `appearance.wallpaper`) are **read-only**; legacy server `clientPatches.theme.wallpaper` still applies via [`src/settings/client-sync.ts`](../src/settings/client-sync.ts). Plan: [`documentation/plans/agent-appearance-control.md`](plans/agent-appearance-control.md).

## What it is

Minnow is a **Vite + TypeScript** single-page web client for **LM Studio** and other **OpenAI-compatible local providers** (multi-provider routing via `~/.minnow/providers/`). UI markup lives in [`index.html`](../index.html); styles and logic are modular under [`src/`](../src/). Production output is emitted to [`dist/`](../dist/) via `npm run build`.

**LM Studio tools + attachments:** The default send path runs an OpenAI-style **tool loop** (`sendMessageWithTools` in [`src/tools/loop.ts`](../src/tools/loop.ts)). **62** built-in tools are defined in [`src/tools/definitions.ts`](../src/tools/definitions.ts) (Orchestrate `board_*` trio, bug tracker `bug_*` trio, sub-agent spawn/status, mode handoff, memory, LSP, Impeccable, desktop **appearance** trio, etc.); **35** are `serverRequired` and execute on the Node side via **`npm start`** (`server.js` → `POST /api/tools`, including **7** CDP `browser_*` tools). **24** are browser-routed (`serverRequired: false`), including web/utility tools, `ask_question`, mode handoff, sub-agent/board orchestration tools, and desktop **`get_appearance` / `update_appearance` / `upload_appearance_asset`** (spawn/status via [`src/tools/sub-agent-executor.ts`](../src/tools/sub-agent-executor.ts) / [`src/tools/board-tools.ts`](../src/tools/board-tools.ts), not raw `POST /api/tools`). File **attachments** (images, text/code, PDF, office documents) use the composer paperclip and multimodal API payloads when a **VLM** model is selected. Non-image files render as compact **preview cards** (extension badge, filename, size) in the composer strip ([`src/attachments/store.ts`](../src/attachments/store.ts)) and sent-message bubbles ([`src/ui/user-message-bubble.ts`](../src/ui/user-message-bubble.ts)); shared layout in [`src/attachments/file-card.ts`](../src/attachments/file-card.ts) with styles in [`src/styles/input.css`](../src/styles/input.css) / [`src/styles/messages.css`](../src/styles/messages.css) (MIN-410). Workspace file-tree drops queue the same cards via [`addWorkspaceFileReference`](../src/attachments/workspace-ref.ts). **`browser_screenshot`** returns inline PNG bubbles via `ToolResultMessage.attachments` and `GET /api/browser/screenshot/:id`.

## Repository layout (Vite)

```
Minnow/
├── index.html              # Vite shell (MIN-387 incremental retire): boot theme script, loader, static markup — event wiring via `src/ui/shell-handlers.ts` (`npm run index-html:coupling-map` tracks id coupling)
├── server.js               # Dev server: Vite + /api/* (npm start)
├── server/                 # Config, tools, providers, generations, MCP, memory, …
├── package.json
├── tsconfig.json
├── vite.config.ts          # base: './', outDir: dist, vendor manualChunks, chunkSizeWarningLimit 1500
├── public/                 # Copied verbatim to dist/ (not bundled)
│   ├── benchmark-packs/    # Standard eval JSON (mini + full tiers; fetched at bench run time)
│   ├── manifest.json       # PWA manifest (start_url: ./)
│   ├── sw.js               # Service worker (cache: minnow-v7)
│   └── icons/              # PWA icons; add-folder.png (**New group**); folder.png (collapsed rail group folders); board-group.png (Orchestrate board folders in sidebar); mode-*.png (operating mode glyphs — selector + sidebar); bugs.png (**All bugs** `#btnAllBugs`); stats.png (**Inference metrics** `#btnStats`); agent-activity.png (**Agent activity** `#btnAgentActivity`); console.png (**Terminal** `#btnTerminal`)
├── src/
│   ├── main.ts             # Entry: CSS imports, initTheme(), shell-handlers, initApp()
│   ├── types.ts            # Messages, ApiMessage, ToolCall, ContentPart
│   ├── constants.ts        # STORAGE_KEY, PRESET_STORAGE_KEY; theme keys re-exported from theme.ts
│   ├── theme.ts            # 16 palette themes (8 families × dark/light), storage, applyTheme, initTheme
│   ├── app-state.ts        # streaming flags, modelCache, abort controllers
│   ├── chat/streaming-state.ts # per-chat streaming helpers (active vs background)
│   ├── chat/context-usage.ts # MIN-13 context budget + breakdown sections
│   ├── agents/             # Sub-agent orchestrator, runner, work agents, UI Designer
│   ├── providers/          # Multi-provider store, fetch-models, resolve
│   ├── state/sessions.ts   # Sessions API + ~/.minnow mirror
│   ├── api/models.ts       # fetchModels (multi-provider), modelCache, resolveModelInfo, composite select keys
│   ├── api/reasoning.ts    # extractReasoningDelta, splitThinkingSegments (LM Studio)
│   ├── api/inline-thinking.ts # inline content thinking split + stream routers (MiniMax/Qwen/Gemma)
│   ├── api/chat.ts         # SSE/stream helpers, mergeToolCallDelta, sendMessagePlain
│   ├── api/generations.ts  # Backend-owned generations client (POST/subscribe/cancel)
│   ├── chat/
│   │   ├── messaging.ts    # sendMessage → sendMessageWithTools
│   │   ├── generation-resume.ts # boot re-subscribe via currentGenerationId
│   │   ├── modes/          # Step 05: registry, tool-policy
│   │   ├── orchestrate/    # Orchestrate: plan paths, list plans (find_files), send gate
│   │   ├── reef/           # Reef mode: widget iframes + bridge (Phase 2)
│   │   ├── prompts/        # Step 04 composer; `prompts/titles/` for title templates (Step 07)
│   │   └── titles/         # Step 07: schedule, generate, sanitize
│   ├── ui/                 # sidebar, hub.ts (empty-chat landing), theme.ts (Appearance), settings, stats, messages, tool-approval-modal, question-cards-modal, …
│   ├── state/file-panel.ts # file sidebar + viewer prefs
│   ├── lib/

│   ├── skills/               # Step 13: SKILL.md pack, client, builtin-manifest.json
│   ├── tools/
    │   │   ├── definitions.ts      # 61-tool catalog (OpenAI function schemas)
│   │   ├── config.ts           # tools.json sync, permissions, enabled defs

│   │   ├── client.ts           # ping, executeTool router, approval gate, ask_question → UI queue
│   │   ├── permission-gate.ts  # modal + path policy before tool runs
│   │   ├── approval-queue.ts   # serialized approval requests
│   │   ├── ask-question-queue.ts
│   │   ├── ask-question-types.ts
│   │   ├── tool-approval-types.ts
│   │   ├── describe-invocation.ts
│   │   ├── path-args.ts

│       ├── fonts.css tokens.css global.css topbar.css sidebar.css
│       ├── messages.css input.css settings.css stats.css file-panel.css tool-approval.css question-cards.css responsive.css

├── dist/                   # Production build (gitignored)
```
