| [`src/state/orchestrate-board-actions.ts`](../src/state/orchestrate-board-actions.ts) | Shared ops: `startTask`, `startTaskTesting`, `startFinalIntegrationTest`, `stopTask`, `moveTaskStatus`, `startWave`, `autoDelegateNext`, `rehydrateBoardWorktreeRoots` / `rehydrateAllBoardWorktreeRoots` (boot worktree re-bind), `pauseAllRunningBoardsForShutdown` (passes `stopBoardAutoRun` reason `system` → sets `board.systemPaused`, not `userStopped`), `resumeBoardExecutionAfterReload` (reload boot), **`recoverInterruptedMergesAfterReload`** (boot wrapper over **`reconcileMergingTasks`** — supervise live fixer via `shouldSuperviseBoardChatOnReload` or re-run `finalizeMergeFixerOnStreamEnd` + `drainTaskQueue`), **`reconcileMergingTasks`** (live safety net: called at top of `autoDelegateNext` before `isBoardRunning` gate, from `waitForNoActiveFixer` timeout after 60s cap, boot/OOM recovery, and **`recoverMergingBoardTask`** — uses `isTaskChatActive` at runtime), **`recoverMergingBoardTask`** (manual merge recovery: stop fixer supervision + reconcile; `continueBoardTask` / `restartBoardTask` / `moveTaskToNewChat` delegate when `status === 'merging'`), `setBoardExecutionMode`, `activateAfk` / `requestPendingAfk` / `cancelPendingAfk` (MIN-213: shared AFK confirm path + `pendingAfk` gate; AFK mode still requires **Start** like Auto), `finalizeBoardTaskOnStreamEnd` (successful build → `testing`; auto-pilot launches **Tester**; **stopped build** → always leaves `in_progress`: user/`board.userStopped` (unless `board.systemPaused`) → **`quarantined`** with requeue note; shutdown/OOM system pause → `planned` + `stopRetries`; timeout/system stop reason → bounded retry via `task.stopRetries` + `MAX_STOP_RETRY_ATTEMPTS` then quarantine; clears `chatId` + tears down chat resources so stall-restart cannot loop; **success clears `stopRetries`**), `resolveTaskChatStopReason`, `finalizeTaskTestingOnStreamEnd` / `finalizeFinalTestOnStreamEnd` / `finalizeMergeFixerOnStreamEnd` / `finalizeEnvFixerOnStreamEnd` (**report-driven** via `resolveBoardReport`; Tester `VERDICT:` transcript fallback only). **Unified board report + stall (2026-06-27):** all member chats call **`board_report`** once; **`runTaskChatNudge`** accepts `{ chatId }` for stall nudges on builder/tester/fixers; **`resolveStallHealPhase`** maps stalled chat → self-heal phase; **`startTaskChatSupervision`** uses **3× `progressStallMs`** (`TASK_CHAT_STALL_MULTIPLIER`) for every supervised chat (first stall → nudge; recurrence → self-heal). **Merge queue:** `waitForNoActiveFixer` gates on live fixer streams (`isTaskChatActive`) with a 60s cap; dead fixers reconcile via **`reconcileMergingTasks`** → report-driven `finalizeMergeFixerOnStreamEnd` (no heartbeat git-poll); `drainTaskQueue` coalesces overlapping drains via per-group in-flight guard; **`requeueBoardTask`** cascades to dependents quarantined solely as `blocked by quarantined <root>` and clears **`completionShownAt`**, **`finishReport`**, **`wrapUpPending`**, **`terminalBlocked`**, matching **`unresolvedIssues`**, per-task heal/report fields, bumps **`lifecycleRun`** per requeued task, and synchronously clears orchestrator report dedupe keys so a re-run can re-emit completion reports. **`syncBoardRunCompletion`** / **`onTasksQuarantined`** centralize final-test vs all-quarantined plan-complete when the board becomes terminal (including BFS quarantine); all-quarantined sets **`terminalBlocked`** and surfaces blocked copy via plan-complete UI + **`board_blocked`** notification. **`listRunningBoardTaskSlots`** / **`countRunningTaskChats`** enumerate concurrency-slot chats (build / test / fixer / final integration); **`restartBoardTask`** / **`continueBoardTask`** / **`moveTaskToNewChat`** / **`stopRunningBoardSlot`** (MIN-222): **Restart** re-seeds the original build prompt in a fresh chat via `pendingBuildSeed` + resets `testAttempts`; **Continue** nudges the existing chat (`runTaskChatNudge`) or smart-routes to move when derailed/oversized (`shouldRouteContinueToNewChat` + `continueSmartRoute`); **Move to new chat** seeds a progress summary (`buildMoveToNewChatSeed`); all three call **`clearTaskFailureState`** (snapshots `prevFailure`, clears `error` / `testVerdict` / `testSummary`; **does not** reset `merging` — use **Reconcile merge**). Task `in_progress` is set only when the **Builder** chat begins streaming (`markBoardTaskInProgressFromChat` — skips `workAgentId: tester` and `testing` status); stalled `testing` tasks check `testChatId` (`isTaskStalledForRestart`) and auto-pilot resumes via `startTaskTesting`. Background chat launches are skipped only when `MINNOW_TEST=1` in Node (guard is browser-safe). Test failures reopen with a **fresh Builder chat** (compact retry seed, ≤3 attempts → `blocked`) |

Planner (parse) chats link via `Chat.boardGroupId` and sit in the folder via `Chat.groupId` (`linkPlannerChatToBoardFolder` in `getOrCreateBoardGroup`); `SessionState.activeBoardGroupId` selects which folder’s kanban fills `#chatArea`. Planner chat is **always first** in board folder member lists (`sortBoardGroupMembers` in `buildSortedWorkspaceSidebarEntries`); title convention **`Orchestrator - {plan-basename}`** via [`planner-chat-title.ts`](../src/chat/orchestrate/planner-chat-title.ts) (`syncOrchestratorPlannerChatTitle` on link, plan pick, board init, session repair). **MIN-211 (shipped):** board folder members render under collapsible per-wave subgroups in [`sidebar.ts`](../src/ui/sidebar.ts) (`appendBoardGroupWaveMembers`) — planner row first, then one header per wave with started chats (hidden when no member chats yet); waves with all tasks `complete` auto-collapse; manual caret toggles persist `BoardWave.collapsed` via `scheduleSaveSessions`. Non-board groups stay flat. Styles: `.chat-wave-subgroup-header` / `.chat-wave-members` in [`sidebar.css`](../src/styles/sidebar.css). **`deleteGroup`:** plain sidebar groups only ungroup member chats; **board folders** delete all linked chats (`listBoardGroupChatIds` — `groupId`/`boardGroupId` members plus task `chatId`/`testChatId`/`fixerChatId` and `finalTest.chatId`), stop streams, best-effort `cleanupBoardWorktrees`, and remove the folder. **`removeChatById`** (sessions): deleting a planner chat copies `orchestratePlanPath` onto the folder and clears `plannerChatId` so boards stay listed. **`dismissActiveBoardView`** clears board session focus only (full DOM/listener teardown: `exitBoardViewForNavigation`). **`getGroupActivityAt`** / **`buildSortedWorkspaceSidebarEntries`** — sidebar main list interleaves groups and ungrouped chats by newest member `lastMessageAt` (empty groups use `createdAt`), not static group-first order. **Drag** chats onto headers (`src/ui/sidebar-chat-dnd.ts`); board folders (`.chat-group-header--has-board`) open board view on header click (`openBoardGroup` focuses planner chat when returning from a task chat) and show `.active` selection while open (sidebar chat rows suppress `.active` highlight during board view) |
| [`src/chat/orchestrate/plan-complete-ui.ts`](../src/chat/orchestrate/plan-complete-ui.ts) | One-shot deterministic completion message when all tasks are terminal (`board.completionShownAt`; requires `finalTest.status === 'passed'` when any task completed) plus **one** planner wrap-up LLM turn (`buildOrchestratePlanCompleteWrapUpSeed`); **`board.wrapUpPending`** + stream-end retry when wrap-up is skipped during planner streaming or before model binding resolves; headless finish-report enrichment unchanged |
| [`src/chat/orchestrate/plan-from-history.ts`](../src/chat/orchestrate/plan-from-history.ts) | `findLastPlanSavePath(history)` — newest successful `save_file` under `documentation/plans/` (used by plan screen preview on `subscribeChatStreamEnd`) |
| [`src/chat/orchestrate/plan-preview.ts`](../src/chat/orchestrate/plan-preview.ts) | `parsePlanFrontMatter`, `splitPlanMarkdown`, `planMarkdownForDisplay` (body after YAML, or synthesized GFM when body empty), `mountPlanPreviewContent` / `buildPlanPreviewDom` — full plan markdown via `setAssistantBubbleContent` on `.plan-preview__body.msg-bubble--md` (hub plan picker + plan-screen preview phase) |
| [`src/chat/orchestrate/board-boot-resume.ts`](../src/chat/orchestrate/board-boot-resume.ts) | `bootOrchestrateBoardResume` — OOM probe: pause all boards (`systemPaused`), then **`recoverInterruptedMergesAfterReload`** per board (reconcile dead `merging` fixers without resuming auto-run); normal path: same merge reconcile for every board, then for running boards rehydrate worktrees, re-subscribe stream-end handlers, re-attach heartbeat supervision, and `autoDelegateNext` when `autoRunning === true` |
| [`src/chat/orchestrate/board-shutdown.ts`](../src/chat/orchestrate/board-shutdown.ts) | `registerOrchestrateBoardShutdownHandler` — `pagehide` → `pauseAllRunningBoardsForShutdown` (`stopBoardAutoRun` reason `system` → `systemPaused`, not user Stop quarantine) |

**Task chat:** `startTask` creates or reuses a **Build-mode** chat (planner’s provider/model, default **Builder** work agent), sets `Chat.boardTaskId` to the board task id, seeds a user message (plan path + task id + build/specs), and runs `runChatTurn` in the background without switching the active chat. **Board member tool scoping (MIN-333):** chats with `boardTaskId` get `board_get_state` / `board_report` injected (`injectBoardMemberSubsetTools` — build mode omits the orchestrate `board` group) then intersect enabled tools via `applyBoardMemberToolFilter` — builder gets files-write + browser + web + git-write; tester gets web + git-write + browser (no files-write); fixer matches builder but **no browser**; all roles get `board_get_state` / `board_report` only (no `ask_question`). **Testing (2026-06):** successful builds land in **Testing**; `startTaskTesting` runs the **Tester** work agent ([`src/chat/prompts/work-agents/tester/`](../src/chat/prompts/work-agents/tester/)) in a linked `testChatId` chat (headless per-task: typecheck/lint/unit/build + static review; no browser). All board member chats (builder, tester, merge/env fixer) call **`board_report`** once when done (`task.boardReport` + legacy field mirrors); stream-end finalizers read `resolveBoardReport` (legacy `testVerdict` / `buildOutcome` fallback for hydrated sessions). Builder `pass` → `testing`; `env_blocked` → env fixer; missing/fail → self-heal (no prose-only advance). Tester `pass` → auto-commit + merge → `complete`; `fail`/`env_blocked`/missing → self-heal (Tester keeps `VERDICT:` transcript fallback). **Board member chats** (Builder/Tester/fixers) cannot call `board_update_task`, `board_init`, or `delegate_tasks` — only the orchestrate planner moves cards via tools. Merge conflicts spawn a **fixer** chat (`fixerChatId`, status `merging`) in the integration worktree with the merge **already in progress** (`MERGE_HEAD` set); the fixer resolves markers, finishes with `git commit --no-edit`, and calls **`board_report`**. **`finalizeMergeFixerOnStreamEnd`** / **`finalizeEnvFixerOnStreamEnd`** are report-driven; dead fixers recover via **`reconcileMergingTasks`**; merge fixer stream-end also runs **git fallback** (`check_merged` + `verify_integration`) when the fixer never called `board_report`. **Env-fixer test-phase pass:** `finalizeEnvFixerOnStreamEnd` moves the card to `testing`, pre-reserves the tester launch slot, clears fixer linkage, then `await startTaskTesting` with **`enqueueTaskAtFront`** + **`allowPreReservedTestChat`** so a queued sibling cannot steal the single concurrency slot before testing resumes; **`startTaskTesting`** excludes its own pre-reserved tester slot from the concurrency check and releases the reservation on enqueue/skip early returns (MIN-377). **`resumeBoardTask`** / **`continueBoardTask`** pass **`allowPreReservedTestChat`**; **`isTaskChatActiveForStallCheck`** treats reserved-but-not-streaming tester chats as idle for stall recovery. **Merge-fixer pass + verify fail:** completion still requires **`verifyIntegrationMerge`**; when `board_report` is `pass` but verify fails, the board restores integration, increments `fixerAttempts`, and re-spawns the merge fixer with a seed that includes the verify failure summary and attempt number (not silent complete). **Stream-end fallback:** when `fixerChatId` was cleared before stream-end delivery, **`resolveFixerTaskForStreamEnd`** routes via `Chat.boardTaskId` (`merging` + report → merge finalize; `fixerKind === 'env'` → env finalize) before drain-only fallback. **MERGE_HEAD preflight** injects synthetic `boardReport: pass` when merge already committed or clean re-merge succeeds. **Unified stall watchdog (AFK/auto):** all board-linked chats share **`startTaskChatSupervision`** at **3× `progressStallMs`**; first stall → **`runTaskChatNudge(..., { chatId })`**; recurrence → **`runSelfHeal`** via **`resolveStallHealPhase`**. Progress age uses **`getProgressAgeMs`** ([`wrapper.ts`](../src/agents/controller/wrapper.ts) monotonic baseline — same clock as sub-agent watchdog) so tab-focus `resetHeartbeatBaselines` does not false-trigger `stopGeneration` ~7s after each task start (UI: "Generation stopped" / log: `build stopped`). **AFK display-off (2026-07):** the Electron main window sets `backgroundThrottling: false` ([`electron/main.ts`](../electron/main.ts)) so timers and SSE delivery stay live when the monitor sleeps; stall supervision **freezes** progress/heartbeat age while `document.hidden` (hidden interval excluded from `monotonicDelta`) and **`runChatToolBatch`** bumps progress on tools-phase entry and each tool start via **`notifyChatStreamActivity`** → board `bumpProgress`. A per-task **`fixerFinalizeInFlight`** guard prevents double-finalize races, and unmatched stream-ends while the board is running still call `safeDrain` so the queue never idles at the derived **Paused** header badge. On fixer failure, `restoreIntegration` resets to `mergePreSha` and retries once (`fixerAttempts`); after 2 attempts the task is `blocked`. Auto-merge and fixer success both gate on **`verifyIntegrationMerge`** (branch ancestry, no conflict markers, clean tree — no per-merge build). Otherwise `blocked`. **`isTaskReadyForAuto`** is DAG-first: tasks with explicit `dependsOn` start when deps are `complete` (merged); wave barriers apply only when a task has no deps. When every task is `complete`, **`startFinalIntegrationTest`** runs Tester with browser smoke (single shared final pass); board completion UI waits for `finalTest.status === 'passed'`. Manual mode: **Run tests** / **Run final integration test** buttons. **`board_get_state`** resolves from any board-linked chat; **`board_update_task`** requires an Orchestrate-mode planner chat. **Stop** calls `stopGeneration` on build and test chats and keeps transcripts. Default **`maxConcurrentTasks`** = 3 on the board (queued wave starts drain on `notifyChatStreamEnded`). **Concurrency slots:** each distinct task holds one slot from build start through test pass + merge; `countRunningTaskChats` counts streaming/setup-pending chats plus synchronous **`reservedLaunchChatIds`** (set before the first `await` in `startTask` / `startTaskTesting` / `startFinalIntegrationTest`, released in `runChatTurn` `.finally()` via **`releaseLaunchSlotAndDrive`**, which also calls `drainTaskQueue` so build→test and test→next-task handoffs recover once capacity frees — the stream-end microtask drain alone is insufficient while the ended chat still holds its reservation) so the build→test handoff gap cannot over-subscribe; `drainTaskQueue` sorts in-testing tasks ahead of queued builds in all auto modes so at-capacity testers resume before new builds. **Task errors** (`.board-task-card__error`, from failed sub-agent runs or `board_update_task`) are cleared when a task is **Reopen**ed (`moveTaskStatus` → `planned`) or **Start**ed again. **Board return:** task-card chat rows call `switchChat`; reopening the board (sidebar folder header or board toggle) calls `openBoardGroup`, which focuses the planner chat when a member task chat was active. While `ChatGroup.viewMode === 'board'`, `isStreamDomVisible` / `appendBubble` suppress live DOM for **all** active chats (including Build-mode task streams), except the transient orchestrator init split.

**Worktree isolation (MIN-275):** In **Auto** mode, parallel tasks get per-task `git worktree` dirs under `~/.minnow/worktrees/<repoKey>/<boardId>/` on branches `minnow/board/<boardId>/task/<taskId>`; on tester **pass** the board **auto-commits** the task worktree (per-task isolation only) and **merges** into `minnow/board/<boardId>/integration` inside a dedicated integration worktree (main checkout untouched). The integration worktree **seeds** dependency dirs (`node_modules`, `vendor`, `.venv`, etc.) as junctions from the main workspace via [`server/worktree/dep-symlinks.js`](../server/worktree/dep-symlinks.js); new **task** worktrees symlink those dirs from the **integration** worktree (with `realpath` collapse while the seed is still a junction → main). After a verified merge, when the merge diff touched a manifest or lockfile, **`refresh_integration_deps`** materializes any seed junctions then runs the matching install in the integration worktree via [`server/worktree/dep-install.js`](../server/worktree/dep-install.js) so later tasks see packages a prior task added without polluting the user's main `node_modules`. Merges are serialized per board (`mergeQueueByGroupId`); a fixer chat blocks concurrent merges. On merge conflict, `merge` leaves the integration worktree mid-merge (no `abort_merge`) and returns `conflictedFiles` + `integrationSha` (pre-merge tip for recovery). Server ops: `commit`, `check_dirty`, `check_merged`, `abort_merge`, `merge`, `restore_integration`, `verify_integration`, `refresh_integration_deps`, **`integration_stats`**, **`commit_integration`**, **`push_integration`**, **`open_pr`** (MIN-208 finish dashboard: `workspace_landing_stats` compares integration branch vs workspace checkout; **Commit & push** runs `merge_integration_into_workspace` then workspace `git commit` / `git push` / `open_workspace_pr`; after a successful merge+commit the finish dashboard primary action becomes **Clear worktrees** (`integrationLandedAt` on the board → `cleanup` with `includeIntegration: true`, clears task `worktreePath` and linked chat `worktreeRoot`; `worktreesClearedAt` when done); legacy `commit_integration` / `push_integration` / `open_pr` remain for the isolated worktree). `startTask` sets `Chat.worktreeRoot`; `startFinalIntegrationTest` binds the final Tester chat to the integration worktree when `integrationBranch` is set (fail-fast if the worktree is missing); on reload `rehydrateBoardWorktreeRoots` re-attaches server worktrees and re-binds linked chats before generation/board resume. The tool loop resolves scope via `resolveChatToolWorkspaceRoot` (worktree, else board member `chat.workspacePath` — never the live top-bar workspace) and forwards it as `workspaceRoot`; **`boardWorktreesRootsFromState`** plus `extraPathRoots` on the permission gate treat `~/.minnow/worktrees/…` as in-scope (no false “server will reject” prompts). ([`worktree-isolation.ts`](../src/state/worktree-isolation.ts), [`worktree-service.ts`](../src/state/worktree-service.ts), [`server/worktree/`](../server/worktree/), `POST /api/worktree`). Server session validation persists `worktreeRoot`, `worktreePath`, `isolationMode`, `integrationBranch`, `fixerChatId`, `fixerAttempts`, and `mergePreSha`. Sequential/manual → isolation off unless overridden. **Per-task ports (2026-06):** each isolated task gets `devPort` (Vite client) + `apiPort` (Express/API, default offset +100) via [`allocateTaskPorts`](../src/state/worktree-isolation.ts); `execute_command` / `start_background_command` merge `PORT`, `VITE_PORT`, `MINNOW_*` env when cwd matches the worktree ([`server/workspace/board-task-ports.js`](../server/workspace/board-task-ports.js)). Task chat end tears down dev-servers and background runs ([`board-task-teardown.ts`](../src/state/board-task-teardown.ts)). **Build-failure retry (2026-06):** in auto/afk, `finalizeBoardTaskOnStreamEnd` calls `applyTaskBuildFailureState` (global `maxBuildAttempts`, default 2) before terminal `failed`; below the cap, auto-retry **continues in the same build chat** via `runTaskChatNudge` (not `pendingBuildSeed` + `startTask` force-new-chat). `resolveTaskChatStreamOutcome` consults the latest `TurnRunRecord.status` first so partial assistant prose after a failed turn is not misread as success. On terminal build failure with a dirty per-task worktree, `check_dirty` + auto-commit preserves orphaned files on the task branch (recoverable error note). Manual mode stays terminal. Manual test guide: [`documentation/guides/MIN-275-worktree-isolation-testing.md`](guides/MIN-275-worktree-isolation-testing.md). Spec: [`documentation/specs/MIN-275-worktree-isolation.md`](specs/MIN-275-worktree-isolation.md).

**Tests:** `test/chat/concurrent-streams.test.mts`, `test/state/chat-groups.test.mts`, `test/state/orchestrate-board-hydrate.test.mts`, `test/chat/orchestrate/board-boot-resume.test.mts`, `test/ui/sidebar-order.test.mts`, **`test/orchestrate/quarantine-completion-hooks.test.mts`**, **`test/orchestrate/orchestrate-quarantine-completion.test.mts`**, **`test/orchestrate/board-report.test.mts`** (MIN-386: `board_report` happy path, quarantine-last blocked terminal, requeue re-announce, mid-turn report deferral), `test/orchestrate/plan-complete.test.mts`, **`test/orchestrate/plan-complete-ui.test.mts`**, `test/orchestrate/board-store.test.mts`, `test/orchestrate/delegate-tasks.test.mts`, `test/orchestrate/task-stream-end.test.mts`, `test/orchestrate/task-build-retry.test.mts`, `test/orchestrate/build-failure-preserve.test.mts`, `test/orchestrate/merge-fixer-finalize.test.mts`, `test/orchestrate/merge-fixer-stall.test.mts`, **`test/orchestrate/board-task-chat-stall.test.mts`** (AFK/auto task-chat stall watchdog; `getProgressAgeMs` monotonic baseline + hidden-page freeze — avoids false "Generation stopped" after tab-focus reset or display sleep), **`test/tools/chat-tool-batch-stall.test.mts`** (tool-phase `notifyChatStreamActivity` bumps for board stall credit), **`test/orchestrate/fixer-recovery.test.mts`** (unified stall recovery, `reconcileMergingTasks`, report-driven merge/env fixer finalize, `systemPaused`, self-heal ceiling), **`test/orchestrate/_board-flow-helpers.mts`** + **`test/orchestrate/board-flow-e2e.test.mts`** (lifecycle E2E harness: `driveBoardToConvergence` drives `autoDelegateNext` + scripted stream-end outcomes across build → test → merge → final test; wave barrier, self-heal/fix/quarantine mix, fixer-deadlock regression, reload resume), `test/tools/board-member-tool-filter.test.mts`, `test/server/worktree-ops.test.mjs`, **`test/server/worktree-middleware.test.mjs`** (finish-dashboard `commit_integration` / `push_integration` wired in middleware), `test/server/board-task-ports.test.mjs`, `test/server/dep-symlinks.test.mjs`, `test/server/dep-install.test.mjs`, `test/chat/orchestrate/plan-from-history.test.mts`, `test/chat/orchestrate/plan-preview.test.mts`, `test/chat/orchestrate/planner-chat-title.test.mts`, `test/chat/orchestrate/task-activity.test.mts`, `test/chat/orchestrate/task-chats.test.mts`, `test/ui/orchestrate-board-header-status.test.mjs`, `test/ui/orchestrate-board-live-update.test.mjs` (wave collapse, task card activity/chats), `test/chat/modes/composer-modes.test.mts`, `test/ui/orchestrate-hub.test.mts`, `test/ui/orchestrate-plan-screen.test.mts`, **`test/notifications/producers-quarantine.test.mjs`** (`board_blocked` for all-quarantined boards).

### Settings page (Step 20 + MIN-130 rebuild)

Full-page settings at `#/settings/<category>` or legacy `#/settings/<area>` (`src/ui/settings-page.ts`, `src/ui/settings-page-types.ts`, `src/ui/settings-catalog.ts`, `src/ui/settings-layout.ts`, `src/ui/settings-controls.ts`, `src/ui/settings-switch.ts`, `src/ui/settings-sections.ts`, `src/styles/settings-page.css`, shared control primitives in `src/styles/settings-controls.css`). **Complete settings inventory:** [`documentation/guides/settings-reference.md`](guides/settings-reference.md). **Information architecture (MIN-130 redesign, Phase A):** unified **grouped sidebar** (`settings-nav` in `index.html`) lists all **6 categories** as uppercase group headers (`data-settings-category` on `.settings-nav-group__label`) with every **area** (or integrations **hub**) as a one-click `.settings-nav__item` (`data-settings-nav-area` / `data-settings-nav-hub`). Selecting an item shows **one area page** (`.settings-area.is-active`; integrations still use **4 hub stacks** via `.settings-hub.is-active`). Sticky `.settings-category-subnav` tabs are removed. Nav clicks write `#/settings/<area>`; inbound `#/settings/<category>` opens the category's first area. **Layout:** `.settings-content` beside a 220px sidebar; area `h2` + `settings-lead` visible when that area is the active page (`.settings-area--title-suppressed` overridden for single-area view). **Integrations** hubs unchanged in the DOM (`SETTINGS_INTEGRATIONS_HUBS`); `activateIntegrationsHub()` / `hubForArea()` in `settings-search-navigate.ts`. Legacy `#/settings/<area>` hashes still resolve. **Field catalog:** `SETTINGS_FIELD_CATALOG` in `src/ui/settings-catalog.ts`; `createSettingsField()` adds `data-settings-search-key` rows. **Control primitives (Phase B–D):** `createSettingsSelectRow`, `createSettingsInputRow`, `createSettingsTextareaRow`, `createSettingsRadioRow` (`.settings-segments`), `createSettingsActionsRow`, `appendSettingsOfflineHint`, `appendSettingsDangerZone`, `createSettingsKvList` in `src/ui/settings-controls.ts` — unified `.settings-row` vocabulary with `data-settings-search-key` on every row; interactive states and motion tokens in `src/styles/settings-controls.css` (imported by settings page, Compare, Scheduler). Phase D deduped layout rules from `settings-page.css` (~3.3k → ~2.7k lines); MinnowOS adds `.settings-page--os-embedded` for in-shell layout. **Live filter:** finder query applies `.is-filtering` on the active category (reveal-all areas with row hide/dim); match-count badges on sidebar group headers and items (`src/ui/settings-filter.ts`). **Chat deep-link:** `launch_minnow_app` accepts `settings_query` and `settings_section`; opens Settings and flashes the target control. Boolean settings use **toggle switches** via `createSettingsSwitch` / `createSettingsToggleRow` / `upgradeSettingsCheckboxes`. Compare and Scheduler import `settings-controls.css` for shared `.settings-group` / `.settings-toggle-row` primitives. Plan: [`documentation/plans/settings-page-rebuild-min-130.md`](plans/settings-page-rebuild-min-130.md).

**Cost / token observability (Feature #14 / MIN-50):** Per-chat `chat.tokenLedger` persists in the session blob (`entries[]` capped at 200; `totals` and `bySource` always accumulate). Ingestion: main tool loop and legacy `sendMessage` (`recordMainChatTurnUsage`), each sub-agent SSE turn on the parent chat (`recordSubAgentTurnUsage`), title job (`source: title`), Reef `callLLM` (`source: reef-widget`). Provider optional `pricing` on `~/.minnow/providers/<id>/profile.json` (validated server-side; exposed on `ProviderPublic`). USD via `src/usage/pricing.ts` (`inputPer1M` / `outputPer1M`, model → `*` → default). UI: Settings → **Usage** (`#/settings/usage`, `src/ui/settings-usage.ts`); pricing editor on provider edit forms (`src/ui/settings-providers.ts`). Stats strip `#stripCost` shows last entry cost when priced. Distinct from Feature 25 prompt **estimate** and MIN-13 **next-send** context ring. Plan: [`documentation/plans/Build out/feature-14-cost-token-observability.md`](plans/Build%20out/feature-14-cost-token-observability.md). Verification: [`documentation/plans/verification/feature-14.md`](plans/verification/feature-14.md).

**Chat code change stats:** Server tools return optional `codeChange` (`additions`, `deletions`, `path`/`paths`, `source`, capped `diffLines`) from [`server/tools/line-diff-stats.js`](../server/tools/line-diff-stats.js), [`server/tools/git-change-stats.js`](../server/tools/git-change-stats.js), and [`server/tools/workspace-change-snapshot.js`](../server/tools/workspace-change-snapshot.js) via [`server/runtime/tools-middleware.js`](../server/runtime/tools-middleware.js). Sources: file mutations (`file-tool`), `git_commit` (`git-commit`), foreground `execute_command` git snapshot (`command-snapshot`) or single-file heuristic (`command-heuristic`). `POST /api/tools` includes `codeChange` in JSON; backfill uses `POST /api/tools/code-change-for-commit` `{ sha }`. [`src/tools/client.ts`](../src/tools/client.ts) records `chat.codeChangeTotals` and `sessionState.codeChangeTotalsByWorkspace` ([`src/usage/code-change-ledger.ts`](../src/usage/code-change-ledger.ts)) when `context.chatId` is set (sub-agents use `parentChatId`). History backfill: [`src/usage/code-change-backfill.ts`](../src/usage/code-change-backfill.ts) on session load. Per-file rollup for the composer strip: `getPerFileChangeSummary(chat)` in the ledger. UI: `+`/`−` badges and unified diff in [`src/ui/tool-messages.ts`](../src/ui/tool-messages.ts); compact centered `#codeChangeStrip` pill (`N files +X −Y`) opens `#codeChangePanel` with per-file stats, inline diffs, and file-viewer links ([`src/ui/code-change-strip.ts`](../src/ui/code-change-strip.ts), `initCodeChangeStrip()` in `main.ts`); hub **Agent changes** + sidebar stats ([`src/ui/workspace-code-change.ts`](../src/ui/workspace-code-change.ts)). Plan: [`documentation/plans/chat-code-change-tracking.md`](plans/chat-code-change-tracking.md).

**Settings global finder:** `#settingsSearchInput` in `#settingsSearchFinder` inside `#settingsSearchFinderSlot` in `.settings-page-header` (registry-driven index in `src/ui/settings-search-index.ts`, ranking in `settings-search-rank.ts`, navigation in `settings-search-navigate.ts`, UI in `settings-search-finder.ts`). **Ctrl/Cmd+K** focuses the finder while settings is open. Choosing a result opens the section via existing hash routing, awaits `refreshSettingsSection`, then scrolls to `data-settings-search-key` (tool rows/categories, toggle rows, groups) or falls back to the section’s first `.settings-group`.

**Prompt token estimate (Feature 25 / F4):** While settings is open, `#settingsPromptTokenEstimate` in `.settings-page-header` (right-aligned via `margin-left: auto`, `aria-label="Approximate prompt config token estimate"`) shows **~N tokens (estimate)** for the fixed prompt config (composed system + user rules + mode-filtered tools) — **excludes chat history**. **Prompting** adds `#settingsPromptTokenBreakdown` (System · Tools · Rules). Heuristic: `chars ÷ 4` (`estimateTokensFromText` / `computePromptConfigTokenTotal()` in `src/chat/prompts/token-estimate-core.ts`). `resolveOutboundPromptEstimate()` in `src/chat/prompts/token-estimate.ts` resolves system via `resolveOutboundSystemMessages()` and tools via work-agent allowlist + UI Designer filter. UI: `src/ui/settings-prompt-estimate.ts`. Refreshes on settings open, section change, and profile/part toggles (300 ms debounce). Full send size (incl. history) is in the in-chat context usage ring. Not provider `usage.prompt_tokens`. Verification: [`documentation/plans/verification/feature-25.md`](plans/verification/feature-25.md).

**Context budgets per agent (MIN-39):** Optional `maxInputTokens` and `contextEnforcementPolicy` (`summarize` | `slide` | `truncate` | `archive`) on **work agents** (`WorkAgentDefinition` / `~/.minnow/work-agents.json`) and **sub-agent types** (`SubAgentTypeConfig` / `sub-agents.json`). Enforcement runs in [`src/chat/context-budget.ts`](../src/chat/context-budget.ts) immediately before each provider payload: `applyContextBudget()` after `buildApiMessages()` in [`src/tools/loop.ts`](../src/tools/loop.ts) (main tool loop) and before each sub-agent completion in [`src/agents/sub-agent-runner.ts`](../src/agents/sub-agent-runner.ts). Effective ceiling is `min(agentCap, modelLimit) × 0.9` when both are set; **v1 only enforces when `maxInputTokens` is set** on the active agent/type. Leading `system` messages stay pinned; policies drop whole user→assistant/tool turns to preserve `tool_calls` / `tool` pairs. Settings → Work agents / Sub-agents expose cap + policy in the **Agents center** lightbox. Tests: [`test/chat/context-budget.test.mts`](../test/chat/context-budget.test.mts). Plan: [`documentation/plans/Build out/feature-03-context-budgets.md`](plans/Build%20out/feature-03-context-budgets.md).

**Archive context policy (MIN-139, Brain-backed):** Fourth policy `archive` offloads stale turns into per-chat Brain wiki pages under `~/.minnow/brain/pages/workspaces/<workspaceKey>/archive/<chatId>/` (`turns-XXXX-YYYY.md` + `entities/`, optional `index.md` rollup when ≥3 bundles, `similarTo` frontmatter for vector neighbors), then collapses bundled ranges to zero-cost `<archived_context turns="…" />` system placeholders in the live API view. **Pre-pass** (main chat only): [`src/chat/archive/index.ts`](../src/chat/archive/index.ts) `applyArchivePolicy()` runs once per send (`turn === 0`) in [`src/tools/loop.ts`](../src/tools/loop.ts) before `applyContextBudget`; later tool turns reuse `applyMemoizedCollapse()`. Staleness: [`staleness.ts`](../src/chat/archive/staleness.ts) (`stalenessTurns`, `pressureThreshold`, `minRecentTurns` — editable in Settings → Work agents when policy is archive). **Chunking:** [`chunking.ts`](../src/chat/archive/chunking.ts) splits oversized ranges before enqueue. Bundling: server [`server/brain/archive.js`](../server/brain/archive.js) (`POST /api/brain/archive`, LLM retry on bad quotes, entity promotion to `workspaces/<key>/concepts/`, similarity links, rollup index). **Retrieve:** scoped `POST /api/brain/retrieve` with `scope.chatId` (auto-prelude) or opt-in `scope.mode: workspace` via `recall_chat_context`; recency rerank + optional `llmRerank` on `ArchiveConfig`; `embeddingModelId` override threads to Brain embedder. **Settings:** archive policy gated when Brain embeddings unhealthy (`fetchBrainEmbeddingsStatus`); self-disable banner via `getArchiveDisabledReason()`. **Researcher** work agent ships `archive` defaults + `recall_chat_context`. **Brain UI:** graph/tree hide `workspaces/*/archive/` by default (`minnow.brain.showArchives`, toolbar **Archives**). **Sub-agents:** `archive` normalized to `slide` at load (no `applyArchivePolicy` in sub-agent runner). QA: [`documentation/plans/brain/MN-139-qa-checklist.md`](plans/brain/MN-139-qa-checklist.md). Tests: [`test/chat/archive/`](../test/chat/archive/), [`test/tools/recall-chat-context.test.mts`](../test/tools/recall-chat-context.test.mts), [`test/brain/archive-routes.test.mjs`](../test/brain/archive-routes.test.mjs). Linear: [MIN-139](https://linear.app/minnowai/issue/MIN-139/archive-context-token-reduction-policy-backed-by-brain).

**Context window usage (MIN-13 / BUG-019 / MIN-159):** In-chat **context fill** indicator distinct from the bottom metrics strip (tok/s, TTFT). `#contextUsageRing` in `#composerControls` (after the thinking toggle): compact 24px SVG ring (no button chrome); light grey track, ink `--accent` stroke fill for used share; warning fill at ≥85% (`--warning`). Hover tooltip: model name, limit, used/remaining (approx.). Click opens `#contextUsageBreakdown` popover with per-section token rows and bars (System, Rules, Tools, History, optional **In progress (estimate)**, Composer/Attachments) plus **Full / Lite / Custom** prompt profile tabs (same `activePromptProfile` as Settings → Prompts; updates ring estimate on switch). Data: `getContextBudget()` / `assembleContextBudget()` in [`src/chat/context-usage.ts`](../src/chat/context-usage.ts) merges `resolveOutboundPromptEstimate()` (API-shaped history without assistant `thinking`, context-budget-trimmed when agent cap set) + pending `#msgInput` + `getPendingAttachments()` (`estimateAttachmentTokens` caps images at `ESTIMATE_IMAGE_URL_TOKENS` = 256, not full base64) + in-flight overlay from [`src/chat/context-in-flight.ts`](../src/chat/context-in-flight.ts) (**pending tool-call JSON only** — streaming completion prose and live reasoning are output, not prompt). **Context limit** uses the effective window, not catalog max: `resolveContextLimit()` prefers live `loaded_context_length` / `max_context_length` from the in-memory model catalog (`getModelRowForSelectOrCanonicalId` / `fetchModels()`), then falls back to `chat.modelInfo.context_length` from the last completion when the cache is unavailable (MIN-183), then a **known-model lookup** for `openai-v1` providers when upstream `/v1/models` omits context metadata (MIN-199; table in [`src/lib/known-context-windows.ts`](../src/lib/known-context-windows.ts); server proxy preserves upstream context fields via `normalizeOpenAiModelRow()` in [`server/providers/paths.js`](../server/providers/paths.js)). **OpenCode Zen / Go:** upstream `/v1/models` lists ids only (no `context_length`); `proxyModels()` enriches `opencode.ai` providers from [`models.dev/api.json`](https://models.dev/api.json) → `opencode.models[id].limit.context` ([`server/providers/models-dev-context.js`](../server/providers/models-dev-context.js), 24h cache). Models absent from models.dev still rely on the substring known-window table (may be missing or stale for new Go-only ids). Row precedence matches `contextLengthFromModelRow()` in [`src/lib/context-length.ts`](../src/lib/context-length.ts). `resolveModelInfo()` applies the same live-first rule for the stats strip `#iCtx` field. Shows **last turn API** `prompt_tokens` when `chat.lastStats` has them; section sizes stay heuristic. UI: [`src/ui/context-usage-ring.ts`](../src/ui/context-usage-ring.ts), [`src/ui/context-usage-breakdown.ts`](../src/ui/context-usage-breakdown.ts), [`src/styles/context-usage.css`](../src/styles/context-usage.css). Refreshes on history paint (`renderChatFromHistory` / `renderStatsForChat`), model change, composer input, attachments, tool permission changes, and **during active turns** via debounced `scheduleContextUsageRefresh()` from [`src/tools/loop.ts`](../src/tools/loop.ts) (SSE prose/reasoning deltas, each `chat.history.push`, turn `finally`). `updateStrip()` alone does not refresh the ring. Tests: [`test/chat/context-usage.test.mts`](../test/chat/context-usage.test.mts), [`test/chat/outbound-prompt-estimate.test.mts`](../test/chat/outbound-prompt-estimate.test.mts).

**Models hub (Feature 02):** Settings → **Models** (`#/settings/model-routing`, nav label; hash id unchanged) covers **Main chat** (top-bar provider/model + global sampler + per-chat thinking), background jobs (UI Designer, titles, goal evaluator), and Reef widget LLM on the **active chat**. Work agent and sub-agent model bindings moved to **Agents center** lightboxes. Each routing row has provider/model selects, **Effective** readout, **Advanced (sampler · thinking)** where applicable, and per-row **Fallback chain** (`config.json` → `fallbackChains.roles[<routing-row-id>]`). **Model fallback (Odysseus port #04 / MIN-128):** global enable toggle, dead-host cooldown, **Global fallback chain** (`fallbackChains.roles._global`), diagnostics (`GET /api/system/host-health`); default **off**. Catalog: `src/settings/model-routing-catalog.ts`; UI: `src/ui/settings-model-routing.ts` (`mountStandaloneRoutingEditor` for agent lightboxes); config client: `src/config/fallback-chains-meta.ts`.

**Agents center:** Settings → **Agents** (`#/settings/agent-center`; legacy `#/settings/prompting`, `#/settings/modes`, `#/settings/work-agents`, `#/settings/sub-agents` redirect here) shows **three grouped card grids** — **Modes**, **Work agents**, and **Sub-agents** — with a shared search filter, per-section counts, and match summary (`src/ui/settings-agent-center.ts`, `#settingsAgentCenterBody`). Mode cards show composer mode icons and default work-agent chips; grids cap column width on ultrawide layouts and use a uniform card size (260×148px) across all sections (`src/styles/settings-agent-center.css`). Global sub-agent limits live in a compact horizontal row inside the Sub-agents section (`settings-kv--row`). Clicking a card opens a **lightbox** (`src/ui/settings-agent-center-lightbox.ts`) with entity settings, model binding (sampler/thinking/fallback via `mountStandaloneRoutingEditor`), and a large prompt editor. **Base system prompt** (Full/Lite/Custom parts, setup bundles) lives in a collapsed disclosure below the grids (`#settingsBasePromptPanel`, lazy-loaded on first open). Token estimate stays in the settings header. Experts, Agent packs, Autopilot, and Rules remain separate nav items.

**Sampler (Feature 09):** Settings → **Sampler** (`#/settings/sampler`) holds **global defaults** only (`config.json` → `sampler` via `src/config/sampler-meta.ts`, merged with `DEFAULT_SAMPLER_GLOBAL` in `parseSamplerGlobalBlock()` so sparse persisted blocks still populate all fields in the UI; mirrored to composer `#temperature` / `#maxTokens`). Per-role sampler overrides moved to **Models** → Advanced. UI: `src/ui/settings-sampler.ts`, fields: `src/ui/settings-sampler-fields.ts`.

**Thinking mode toggles:** Settings → **Thinking** (`#/settings/thinking`) sets global default **`on`** (`config.json` → `thinking.defaultMode` via `src/config/thinking-meta.ts`) and optional global **thinking budget** (`thinking.thinkingBudgetTokens` — blank/off at global, clamped 10–200k approximate tokens). Per-role tri-state overrides (`inherit` | `on` | `off`) and per-role **thinking budget** (`thinkingBudgetTokens`: blank = inherit, `0` = off) live in **Models** → Advanced and Agents center lightboxes. Composer `#composerThinkingControl` is a single brain icon toggle (`src/ui/composer-thinking.ts`, `/icons/thinking-brain.png`) inline after mode segments: **on** / **off** appearance (muted when off), default **inherit** (shows resolved stack + budget hint in tooltip), click cycles override (`inherit` → opposite of resolved → toggle explicit → back to inherit). Writes `Chat.thinkingMode`. Resolution: `src/agents/resolve-thinking.ts` (`resolveThinkingBudgetTokens` — sub-agent type → work-agent override → global; `0` disables at any tier); send merge: `src/agents/merge-thinking-body.ts` (dropdown models always resolve effort via `resolveEffectiveReasoningEffort` — inherited **on** beats catalog `off`); watchdog: `src/agents/thinking-budget.ts` + `src/tools/loop.ts` / `src/agents/sub-agent-runner.ts` (prefill-continue for inline-thinking models, cumulative `reasoning_content` dedupe, ephemeral retry fallback for Harmony/`reasoning_content` remotes); request fields: `src/agents/thinking-to-body.ts` (`thinking_budget_tokens` for `openai-v1` when budget set; kept only for `llama-cpp-local` after sanitization; default `8192` when llama supports per-request budget and no tier budget is set; Anthropic explicit `budgetTokens` with 1024 floor; adaptive models skip native budget). **llama.cpp local:** per-request budget only (never CLI `--reasoning-budget`); feature-detect at serve start → `supportsThinkingBudget` on provider capabilities (`server/models/serve.js`). Plan: [`documentation/plans/Build out/feature-thinking-mode-toggles.md`](plans/Build%20out/feature-thinking-mode-toggles.md).

**Composer reasoning controls:** In the Code composer row (`#composerThinkingWrap` in `index.html`), reasoning is controlled per chat based on model capabilities (`ModelCapabilities.reasoningAllowedOptions` from catalog or **openai-v1 provider inference** via `inferReasoningOptionsFromModelId` in `src/lib/reasoning-effort.ts` — all models on OpenAI-compatible APIs get `off`/`low`/`medium`/`high`, or `off`/`on` for Kimi/DeepSeek/MiniMax). **Level options** (`low` / `medium` / `high`): **brain icon** plus compact **reasoning effort** `<select>` (`#composerReasoningEffortWrap` / `#composerReasoningEffortSelect`, wired by `src/ui/composer-reasoning-effort.ts`). Clicking the brain **off** sets `Chat.reasoningEffort` to `off` and hides the dropdown; clicking **on** restores the catalog default level (usually `medium`) and shows the dropdown again. **Binary off/on only**: brain icon alone (`#composerThinkingControl`, `src/ui/composer-thinking.ts`, `/icons/thinking-brain.png`) with the inherit/on/off cycle. The wrap hides when neither applies. Selection is persisted per chat as `Chat.reasoningEffort` or `Chat.thinkingMode` for binary-only models; effective value at send time merges chat override → catalog `reasoningDefault` → resolved brain toggle → first allowed option (`resolveEffectiveReasoningEffort`). Send path: `reasoningEffortToCompletionBody` in `src/agents/thinking-to-body.ts` maps effort to provider fields — `lm-studio-v0`: `enable_thinking`, `reasoning_effort`, nested `reasoning.effort`; `openai-v1`: `thinking.type` for off/on and `reasoning.effort` (or top-level `reasoning_effort`) for `low`/`medium`/`high`. Catalog widening: `reasoningCatalogFromRow` in `src/providers/model-capabilities.ts` preserves full `allowed_options`. `TurnSnapshot.reasoningEffort` records the level used per turn. **Sub-agents** inherit the parent chat's `reasoningEffort` via `resolveEffectiveReasoningEffort` in [`src/agents/sub-agent-runner.ts`](../src/agents/sub-agent-runner.ts) when the bound model exposes level options (same send path as main turns). Plan: [`documentation/plans/model-reasoning-effort-dropdown.md`](plans/model-reasoning-effort-dropdown.md). Tests: `test/lib/reasoning-effort.test.mjs`, `test/agents/thinking-to-body.test.mts`, `test/ui/composer-reasoning-effort.test.mts`, `test/sub-agents/sub-agent-reasoning-effort.test.mts`.

**Editable agents (structural):** `#/settings/modes` (tool policy, plan granularity, Reef widget LLM), `#/settings/experts` (Experts hub + roster), `#/settings/work-agents` (enable, context budget), `#/settings/sub-agents` (global limits + per-type concurrency/timeouts), `#/settings/autopilot` (**MIN-218:** orchestrate board defaults — execution mode, concurrency, isolation, test/build retry caps, **Continue smart-route**, heartbeat thresholds, planner model fallback; `config.json` → `autopilot` via [`src/config/autopilot-meta.ts`](../src/config/autopilot-meta.ts)). Prompts and model bindings: **Prompts** and **Models** hubs. **Prompt diffing (#12):** compare UI on hub editors and Prompting part panels. APIs unchanged: `GET/PUT/DELETE /api/prompts/...`, `/api/work-agents/:id/prompt`, `PUT /api/work-agents/:id`, `PUT /api/config/sub-agents`.

**Tests:** `npm test`, `npm run build`, `test/ui/settings-sections.test.mjs`, `test/ui/settings-page-html.test.mjs`, `test/ui/settings-search-index.test.mjs`, `test/ui/settings-search-rank.test.mjs`, `test/ui/settings-search-finder.test.mjs`. Verification: [`documentation/plans/verification/step-20.md`](plans/verification/step-20.md).

**Tests:** `npm run test:skills`; `node scripts/s13-skills-smoke.mjs` (set `MINNOW_HOME` for override fixture). Verification: [`documentation/plans/verification/step-13.md`](plans/verification/step-13.md).

**Vite-only (`npm run dev`):** picker uses `builtin-manifest.json` + lazy `import.meta.glob` in `client.ts` for built-in bodies (glob is no-op under Node/tsx tests); user skills need `npm start`.

### Programmatic prompts (Step 04)

Composable system prompt at send time via `composeSystemPrompt()` ([`src/chat/prompts/prompt-composer.ts`](../src/chat/prompts/prompt-composer.ts)).

| Profile | `config.json` | Behavior |
|---------|---------------|----------|
| **full** | `activePromptProfile: "full"` | All applicable parts, full templates |
| **lite** | `activePromptProfile: "lite"` | Short/lite bodies, `info`/`memory` off by default |
| **custom** | `"custom"` + `activePromptConfigId` | Per-part enable + `contentOverride` from `prompt-configs/<id>.json` |

**Setup profiles (#13):** Settings → Prompting → **Setup profile** toolbar applies a bundle that can set the rows above plus work-agent/sub-agent bindings and global tool permissions. `prompt-configs/` remains the low-level custom composer store; profiles may embed `parts` or reference an existing prompt-config id.

**Composition order** (single `system` message, `\n\n---\n\n` separators):

`base → mode → expert → work-agent → tool-usage → info → skill → memory`

**Tool definitions:** Enabled tools are **not** duplicated in composed prompt bodies — the model receives full schemas via the outbound JSON `tools` array only. `tool-usage/default.*.md` points at that array; `ComposeContext.enabledToolIds` still drives tool gating and conditional fragments (`ask_question` enforcement, launch-minnow-app, **sub-agent-delegation**). The **browser allowlist** fragment is **gated**: `buildComposeContext()` scans `chat.history` for prior `browser_*` / `request_browser_origin_access` tool calls (`browser-allowlist-gate.ts` → `browserActivated`); until then `composeSystemPrompt()` injects `browser-allowlist.lite.md` (~120 tok); after first browser tool use it switches to the full `browser-allowlist.md` (~711 tok). **Cross-section overlap (MIN-335 / MIN-379):** read-before-write / never-invent-output / smallest-correct-change live only in `tool-usage/default` §Core rules; session context (`cwd`, `date`, `os`) only in `base/default`; `ask_question` JSON shape is authoritative in the tool schema with mandatory `ask-question-enforcement.md` when enabled (browser flow only in `browser-allowlist.md` when preview tools are enabled); mode handoff uses a one-liner in `tool-usage/default` plus the `mode-handoff.md` table (`mode_id` / `plan_path` snake_case); sub-agent spawn mechanics live in `sub-agent-delegation.md`; memory save/search rules live only in the `memory` part (base = one-line pointer); fact-verification ladder in its own fragment. **MIN-379:** when the active work-agent is the mode default (`builder`/`planner`/`orchestrator`), `composeSystemPrompt()` **omits the mode body**; HTML comments stripped at compose; lite profile omits parts without `.lite.md` (no mid-sentence truncation); dead `chat_history_summary` interpolation removed. Ownership matrix: [`documentation/prompt-ownership-matrix.md`](prompt-ownership-matrix.md). Reef mode prompt diet (~1754 → ~700 words). Measured Build+builder stack ~24% fewer input tokens vs pre-MIN-379 (~5900 → ~4500 tok). Tests: `test/prompts/prompt-overlap-collapse.test.mjs`, `test/modes/compose-mode.test.mts`.

**Shipped tree:** `src/chat/prompts/` (`base/`, `tool-usage/`, `info/` presets from `SYSTEM_PROMPT_PRESETS`, `modes/` full+lite pairs, `experts/`, …). Reference-only: `_example/`, `modes/_template/MODE_TEMPLATE.md`.

### Operating modes (Step 05)

Six primary composer modes per chat: **General**, **Build**, **Plan**, **Orchestrate**, **Reef**, **Debug**, plus **Super Plan** as a Plan-segment sub-mode (caret dropdown in `mode-selector.ts`; not a top-level composer segment), and two surface-bound modes outside the composer strip: **Desktop** (MinnowOS desktop chat) and **Onboarding** (first-run wizard guide chat; safe tool subset: util-basic, web, files-read, brain, ask — prompts in `modes/onboarding.*.md`). **General** is first in the mode picker; new Code chats still default to **Build** (`DEFAULT_MODE_ID`). Each mode uses an explicit **tool group allowlist** (`src/chat/modes/tool-groups.ts`) with `{ default: 'deny', tools: { …: 'allow' } }` — email/calendar are **Desktop-only** among built-in modes; bug-board tools are **General + Debug + Desktop**; brain tools ship in every mode. **Desktop** uses every built-in tool group (`DESKTOP_ALLOWED_GROUPS`). **`mcp__*` and `plugin__*` tools bypass the mode matrix** (gated only by Settings **Full** / **Ask** / **Off**); board member chats also pass MCP/plugin tools through the role filter. Tool permissions still follow the catalog on top of mode policy. **Debug** uses `modes/debug.*.md` (bug investigation, `bug_*` tools, All bugs board). Global bug Kanban remains at sidebar **All bugs** / `#/bugs` ([`src/ui/bug-board.ts`](../src/ui/bug-board.ts)). **Reef** builds inline chat widgets via `reef-widget` fences; prompts in `modes/reef.*.md`, copy-paste templates in `src/chat/reef/widgets/*.md` — **15 full templates:** calculator, calculator-with-chart, slider-graph, tabs, form, data-table, comparison, checklist, stats-dashboard, pie-chart, heatmap, quiz, qa-callllm, timeline, unit-converter; **6 composable snippets** (`snippet-*.md`): chart-line, chart-bar, table, stat-card, input-row, sparkline.

| Concern | Location |
|---------|----------|
| Registry + tool policy | `src/chat/modes/registry.ts`, `tool-policy.ts`, `tool-groups.ts` — per-mode group allowlists (`MODE_ALLOWED_GROUPS`); **orchestrator board members** (builder / tester / fixer) intersect via `injectBoardMemberSubsetTools` + `applyBoardMemberToolFilter` + `BOARD_ROLE_ALLOWED_GROUPS` / `resolveBoardMemberRole` in [`orchestrate-tool-filter.ts`](../src/chat/modes/orchestrate-tool-filter.ts) (MIN-333 — build-mode task chats always get `board_get_state` + `board_report` injected before role filtering; fixer has no browser; `ask_question` excluded from all roles); Plan and **Super Plan** allow **`save_file`** / **`make_directory`** only under `documentation/plans/` (`plan-write-guard.ts` in the browser; mirrored in `server/tools/plan-write-guard.js` on `POST /api/tools` when `modeId` is `plan`, `super-plan`, or `planMode: true`; Super Plan additionally permits `documentation/plans/references/**/research-artifact.md` and `build-spec.md`; prompts in `modes/plan.*.md` and `modes/super-plan.*.md`; `save_file` creates parent dirs on the server); Plan includes **code-exec** per MIN-332 matrix |
| Plan clarifying questions (Phase 1) | **Planner** work agent (`src/chat/prompts/work-agents/planner/agent.*.md`): after restating the request, **`ask_question`** yes/no — "Want me to ask a few clarifying questions first to sharpen scope?" On yes, a lightweight **grill** (5–8 questions, one card at a time, recommended answer per question — `/grilling` discipline) via [`question-cards-modal.ts`](../src/ui/question-cards-modal.ts); on no, proceed directly to exploration and drafting |
| Super Plan controller (Phase 3) | [`src/chat/super-plan/`](../src/chat/super-plan/) — `controller.ts` sequences grill → spec_confirm → research → draft1/review1 → draft2/review2 → impeccable → finalize → present; stage state on **`Chat.superPlan`**; **`subscribeSuperPlanController`** listeners receive updates when stages start (`running`), advance (`setSuperPlanActiveStage`), or stall-resume after stream end (`resumeStalledSuperPlanStage`); pauses at **spec_confirm** and **present** (`blocked_user`); artifacts under `documentation/plans/references/<slug>-{spec,research}.md` and `documentation/plans/<slug>.md`; **`no-code-guard.ts`** rejects fenced implementation blocks on `save_file`; **`startSuperPlan`** when mode is `super-plan` — from Orchestrate plan screen **Start planning** or from chat composer via **`startPlanningFromComposer`** (`shouldRouteComposerSendToSuperPlan` in [`sendMessageWithTools`](../src/tools/loop.ts)); prompt contract in [`modes/super-plan.full.md`](../src/chat/prompts/modes/super-plan.full.md); stage controller prompts are pushed as **`UserMessage.superPlanStage`** rows (hidden from the chat transcript via [`hidden-user-messages.ts`](../src/chat/super-plan/hidden-user-messages.ts) + `renderChatFromHistory` skip; legacy rows match the `Super Plan pipeline —` prefix) |
| Super Plan settings (Phase 6) | Settings → Modes → **Super Plan** — `config.planning.superPlan` via [`super-plan-meta.ts`](../src/config/super-plan-meta.ts): review rounds (default **2**), grill question budget (**~20**), research scope (**web / codebase / both**), rounds + depth presets, Impeccable (**auto when UI** / always / never), optional per-stage model overrides (research, plan-reviewer, planner). Controller reads config through [`pipeline.ts`](../src/chat/super-plan/pipeline.ts) (stage skip) and [`stages.ts`](../src/chat/super-plan/stages.ts). UI: [`super-plan-settings.ts`](../src/ui/super-plan-settings.ts). Tests: `test/super-plan/config-meta.test.mts` |
| Plan progress UI (Phase 4) | [`src/ui/plan-progress-screen.ts`](../src/ui/plan-progress-screen.ts) — Deep Research-style animated stepper in the plan screen working phase: **Super Plan** seven nodes (Interview → Spec → Research → Draft → Review → Polish → Final) driven by **`subscribeSuperPlanController`** + `Chat.superPlan.activeStage`; **regular Plan** reduced three nodes (Explore → Draft → Ready) from main-turn activity + `findLastPlanSavePath`; research stage embeds live **`ResearchProgressPanel`** feed via `SuperPlanState.researchId` + `subscribeToResearchStream`; **`prefers-reduced-motion`** disables pulse/feed animations; preview phase **popout** (`buildPlanPreviewPopoutDom`) — **Revise** (`resumeSuperPlanAfterUser` / re-run prompt), **Start Orchestrator** (`launchBoardFromPlan`), **Build** (`createChatWithMode`), **Close** (`teardownOrchestratePlanScreen`); plan rendered with `mountPlanPreviewContent`. Styles: [`src/styles/plan-progress.css`](../src/styles/plan-progress.css). Tests: `test/ui/plan-progress-screen.test.mts` |
| Super Plan review + UI stage (Phase 5) | **`plan-reviewer`** sub-agent (`src/agents/prompts/sub-agents/plan-reviewer.*.md`, registered in `sub-agents.json`): read-only critique of draft plan vs build spec, research, and codebase — two sequential passes via [`buildPlanReviewerTask()`](../src/chat/super-plan/review-helpers.ts) in [`stages.ts`](../src/chat/super-plan/stages.ts) (pass 2 receives pass 1 structured critique on `SuperPlanState.review1Critique`). Shipped default has **no** `maxInputTokens` cap (MIN-424); users may still set one in Settings → Sub-agents. Optional **Impeccable** stage when [`planInvolvesUi()`](../src/chat/super-plan/review-helpers.ts) detects UI signals (components, routes, CSS, `src/ui`, `index.html`, …): [`composeSuperPlanImpeccableStage()`](../src/chat/super-plan/impeccable-stage.ts) wraps `composeImpeccableSkillBody('shape'|'critique', planContext)`; skipped cleanly when no UI. Tests: `test/prompts/plan-reviewer-prompt.test.mjs`, `test/chat/super-plan/stages.test.mts`, `test/sub-agents/sub-agent-config.test.mts` |
| Prompt bodies | `src/chat/prompts/modes/{id}.full.md`, `{id}.lite.md` |
| Template pack | `src/chat/prompts/modes/_template/` |
