### File panel (Step 11)

Project file explorer (right) and editable CodeMirror viewer in a horizontal split with chat.

| Concern | Location |
|---------|----------|
| File tree | `src/ui/file-tree.ts` — lazy `list_directory`, expand/collapse, `refreshFileTree()` after manual refresh, workspace switch, viewer save, and tree CRUD. **Worktree sync:** [`src/ui/file-tree-listing-root.ts`](../src/ui/file-tree-listing-root.ts) tracks `listingWorkspaceRoot` (mirrors git panel cwd via [`resolvePanelWorktreeCwd`](../src/ui/panel-worktree-cwd.ts)); `syncFileTreeToPanelWorktree(panelCwd?)` resets navigation/viewer state and reloads when the root changes; `buildFileTreeToolContext()` passes `workspaceRoot` on tree-scoped `executeTool` calls (`list_directory`, CRUD, viewer read/save). `#fileSidebarTitle` shows a muted worktree suffix (`Files · task-abc`) when browsing a non-main root. `invalidateFileTreeCache()` busts `list_directory` / `find_files` tool-cache for the active listing scope. **`src/ui/file-tree-auto-refresh.ts`** schedules a debounced `refreshFileTree()` after successful mutating `executeTool` results (`save_file`, `append_file`, `insert_at_line`, `replace_text_in_file`, `make_directory`, `move_file`, `copy_file`, `delete_path`) when the file-tree server is up **and** the tool's `workspaceRoot` matches the visible listing root; wired via `runWithFileTreeAutoRefresh` in `src/tools/client.ts`. |
| **Name filter (F19)** | `#fileTreeSearch` — debounced subsequence match on basename; filter mode BFS-indexes via `list_directory` (skips `.git`, `node_modules`, `dist`, `.minnow`) and shows flat results; browse mode when query empty. `src/ui/file-tree-filter.ts`, `src/ui/file-tree-search.ts`. Phase 2 content search not shipped. |
| Viewer | `src/ui/file-viewer.ts` — multi-tab strip via unified bar (`src/ui/unified-right-tabs.ts`, `#unifiedTabs`; file chips use file icon); per-tab state in `src/ui/file-viewer-tab-store.ts` (one CodeMirror mount for the active tab; outgoing buffer snapshotted via `editorViewPath` + `beforeActivate` before tab switch — MIN-186; stale async mounts discarded with `mountGeneration` — MIN-179). **Workspace tabs** persist in `filePanel.openViewerTabs` + `activeViewerTab` (max 20); **chat attachment / image tabs** are ephemeral (`.minnow/attachments/…`, not restored on reload). Re-open same workspace path **focuses** the existing tab. **MIN-224 polish:** drag-reorder (`reorderViewerTab`), middle-click / context-menu close variants, Ctrl+W / Ctrl+Tab in editor scope, overflow scroll-into-view, in-app Save/Discard/Cancel unsaved dialog ([`file-viewer-unsaved-dialog.ts`](../src/ui/file-viewer-unsaved-dialog.ts)). `read_file` / `read_file_range` / `save_file`; workspace images as read-only preview; **`.md` / `.markdown`** GFM preview by default with right-click **Open as code** / **Open as preview**; code-ref / go-to-definition activate the target tab; selection right-click **Add selection to chat** / **Quick edit**; Save + Ctrl/Cmd+S (active tab); header **Close** closes active tab and hides split when empty; large files (>512 KB) read-only excerpt; LSP only on active document |
| Layout | `src/ui/file-layout.ts`, `src/ui/init-file-panel.ts`, `src/ui/workspace-split-resize.ts`, `src/ui/sidebar-resize.ts` — `#chatSidebarResizer` / `#fileSidebarResizer` drag handles on `#appBody` (desktop, expanded rails only) set `--sidebar-w` / `--file-sidebar-w` on `#appBody`; widths persist as `sessions.sidebarWidth` and `filePanel.fileSidebarWidth`. `#splitResizer` drag uses a wider live range (5–95% main column); releasing past **12%** collapses the chat column so the preview/viewer pane is full width (`.chat-column-collapsed` on `#workspaceSplit`); selecting a sidebar chat (`switchChat` → `restoreChatColumnOnChatSelect`) or dragging the resizer back right restores the split. `viewer-open` follows visible `#rightPaneColumn` DOM; **MIN-224 unified tabs:** `#unifiedTabBar` / `#unifiedTabs` span the top of `#rightPaneColumn`; `#fileViewerPane` and `#previewPane` stack **below** the strip (`repairRightPaneDomStructure()` fixes desktop-mount restore that misplaced panes on `#workspaceSplit`); `rightPaneMode` selects content pane without clearing the other tab family; `openViewerTabs` and `previewTabs` persist independently |
| **Preview browser (MIN-105 / MIN-112)** | `src/ui/preview-panel.ts` — toggle `#btnPreviewToggle` in `#fileSidebar` header (`.file-sidebar-preview-toggle`; stays visible on collapsed rail, stacked under `#btnFileSidebarCollapse`; `is-active` when `rightPaneMode === 'preview'`). Right split `#previewPane` shares `#unifiedTabs` with the file viewer (content panes swap; tabs stay open). Workspace HTML/assets via `GET /api/preview/file/*` ([`server/preview/middleware.js`](../server/preview/middleware.js), same `resolveSafePath` as tools; blocks `node_modules`, `dist`, `.git`, `.vite`, `.minnow`; caps concurrent streams). **Electron:** embedded guest is a `WebContentsView` (`partition: persist:minnow-preview`, sandbox + context isolation) positioned over `#previewBody` through `window.minnow.preview` IPC ([`electron/preview-host.ts`](../electron/preview-host.ts), [`electron/preload.ts`](../electron/preload.ts)); [`preview-electron-visibility.ts`](../src/ui/preview-electron-visibility.ts) waits for a **stable `#previewBody` rect** (two matching animation frames), then calls **`show(bounds)`** so the main process positions the guest before `setVisible(true)`; resize updates use `setBounds` only. **Design Mode (MIN-365):** `#btnPreviewDesignToggle` mounts [`src/design/design-mode.ts`](../src/design/design-mode.ts) overlay + tool strip; **Select** is armed by default on enable (persisted per-instance when the user switches tools). **Code only** — the toggle and annotations panel are hidden on the MinnowOS desktop browser drawer (`syncPreviewDesignToolbarForSurface` in [`preview-panel.ts`](../src/ui/preview-panel.ts); active sessions tear down when preview reparents to desktop). Mount targets are resolved in [`preview-design-mode-mount.ts`](../src/ui/preview-design-mode-mount.ts): the overlay and strip both start in `#previewBody`; **Electron** reparents the strip to `#previewDesignChrome` only while the native WebContentsView is the guest (cross-origin Select), otherwise it floats as a bottom-centered overlay over the iframe guest (same as browser mode). While Design Mode is on in Electron, the native guest is hidden and a same-origin iframe in `#previewBody` drives the page so SVG/capture/panel DOM stacks above the guest ([`preview-panel.ts`](../src/ui/preview-panel.ts) `syncDesignModeElectronGuest` (guest swap completes before Select arms; `refreshDesignModeArmedToolGuest` re-binds the picker after iframe load/auto-reload), [`design-mode.css`](../src/styles/design-mode.css)). Tool strip **Clear all** (`.mn-design-strip__clear-all`) wipes select markers, drawn shapes, comment pins, persisted annotations, and queued elementRef/designRef composer chips for the current page (`clearAllDesignModeMarks` in [`design-tool.ts`](../src/design/design-tool.ts)). Tests: `npm run test:design`. **`preview.clear()`** (`PREVIEW_CLEAR` → `about:blank`) runs on panel close and when opening with an empty address bar so the last page does not resurrect. Hide resets guest bounds to zero. Layout hooks: `ResizeObserver` on `#previewBody` / `#previewPane` / `#workspaceSplit`; `applyFileSidebarVisuals` / `applySidebarVisuals` schedule bounds when preview is open; `showPreviewSplit()` applies `viewer-open` before unhiding the pane. **`openPreviewPanel()`** awaits `showPreviewHost()` before `loadPreviewSource()` (MIN-206). **Visibility gate (MIN-192 / MIN-205 / MIN-179):** hides the native guest while fullscreen overlays or menubar/chrome popovers are open, when Code is not foreground (`document.documentElement.dataset.osApp !== 'code'` or hidden `#appBody`), **while Design Mode is enabled**, and resets the main-process guest on renderer `did-finish-load` so a stale `WebContentsView` cannot block input after reload. **Desktop (default):** `npm start` auto-launches Electron. Preview guest uses [`electron/preview-session.ts`](../electron/preview-session.ts) (strips `X-Frame-Options` / `frame-ancestors`). Workspace files load via renderer `/api/preview/file/` URLs. Address bar: workspace paths, `https://…`, `file://…`. Set `MINNOW_BROWSER=1` for a system browser tab. **Browser-only fallback:** per-tab `#previewFrame` iframes in `#previewBody` (inactive tabs `hidden`; embed-block hints via [`preview-embed-detect.ts`](../src/ui/preview-embed-detect.ts)). **MIN-224 multi-tab:** store [`preview-tab-store.ts`](../src/ui/preview-tab-store.ts) + unified strip [`unified-right-tabs.ts`](../src/ui/unified-right-tabs.ts) (max 6 browser tabs; globe icon; `+` new tab; at capacity evicts oldest **background** tab for agent/UI new-tab, otherwise toast); persist `filePanel.previewTabs` + `activePreviewTab` (legacy `previewSource` migrates to one tab); Settings → browser **Restore browser tabs** (`browser.restoreBrowserTabs`). Electron: one `WebContentsView` per tab ([`electron/preview-host.ts`](../electron/preview-host.ts) keyed by `tabId`; only active tab attached); IPC + `window.minnow.preview.tabs` (`create` / `close` / `activate` / `list`); lazy guest load via `loadedTabGuests` until tab activation. **Close last tab:** `closePreviewTabUi` removes the final preview tab from the strip (not just blank the URL); collapses the right split when no file tabs remain, otherwise switches to the file viewer pane. **MIN-224 phase 4 agent tools:** `browser_list` (alias `browser_list_tabs`) lists all tabs with `[active]` marker; `browser_new_tab`, `browser_switch_tab`, `browser_close_tab` (wired in `tool-groups` + `browser-preview-tools.ts`). **MIN-224 phase 5 hardening:** per-tab `render-process-gone` → `PREVIEW_GUEST_CRASHED` IPC + `.preview-tab--crashed` strip state (other tabs unaffected); tab-limit eviction/toast; MIN-342 lazy restore (`collapsePreviewPanelKeepingSource` clears `loadedTabGuests`; `resyncOpenPreviewPanelFromState` no-ops on Code foreground unless desktop drawer restore). Topbar back/forward/reload, address bar, loading spinner, **Auto-reload** (`preview.reload()` in Electron, 250ms debounce on `emitFileSaved`). Persisted: `filePanel.rightPaneMode`, `previewSource`, `previewAutoReload`. **Code entry (MIN-342):** [`preview-restore-policy.ts`](../src/ui/preview-restore-policy.ts) — Code foreground and boot default to a **closed** right split (`resetRightSplitForCodeEntry` / `collapsePreviewPanelKeepingSource` clear both `#previewPane` and `#fileViewerPane` plus the Electron guest; `previewSource` and `openViewerTabs` stay for explicit reopen). Desktop drawer restore unchanged. `reconcileRightSplitDomWithState` hides both panes when `rightPaneMode` is null (fixes desktop reparent stripping `.hidden`). Plan: [`documentation/plans/MIN-342-code-browser-panel-state.md`](plans/MIN-342-code-browser-panel-state.md). Distinct from CDP screencast mirror (POLISH-011). |
| **Crash diagnostics (MIN-262 / MIN-407)** | Crash logging + OOM recovery for long orchestrator AFK runs. **Phase 1 (observe):** [`electron/main.ts`](../electron/main.ts) — `crashReporter.start`, `uncaughtException` / `unhandledRejection`, `child-process-gone`, `render-process-gone` / `unresponsive` / `responsive`; anti-reload-loop recovery (≤3 reloads / 60s, then inline `data:` recovery page). **Log:** append-only JSONL at `~/.minnow/logs/crash.jsonl` via [`electron/crash-log.ts`](../electron/crash-log.ts); `last-crash.json` for boot recovery; `oom-pause.json` (24h) when `reason === 'oom'`. `render-process-gone` calls `flushCrashLogSync()`. **Renderer:** [`src/boot/diagnostics.ts`](../src/boot/diagnostics.ts) — global errors → bug card + notification; OOM-specific recovery banner. **Phase 2 (memory):** [`board-boot-resume.ts`](../src/chat/orchestrate/board-boot-resume.ts) pauses `autoRunning` boards on OOM resume via [`oom-recovery.ts`](../src/chat/orchestrate/oom-recovery.ts); [`task-history-trim.ts`](../src/chat/orchestrate/task-history-trim.ts) collapses board task chat tool bodies (terminal tasks + **`testing` transition** + **idle trim after every board task stream-end** via `trimIdleBoardTaskChats` in [`orchestrate-board-actions.ts`](../src/state/orchestrate-board-actions.ts); skips chats still streaming); board log ring 100 rows / 200-char previews ([`orchestrate-board-store.ts`](../src/state/orchestrate-board-store.ts)); `resolveEffectiveMaxConcurrent` caps at 2 during OOM recovery; board-log mirror retry + `GET /api/orchestrate/board-log` boot ping ([`board-log-disk.ts`](../src/state/board-log-disk.ts)); safe preview `execJs` wrap ([`preview-guest-actions.ts`](../electron/preview-guest-actions.ts)). **IPC:** `minnow:diagnostics:report-error`, `last-crash`, `oom-pause`, `clear-oom-pause`. Plan: [`documentation/plans/orchestrate-board-oom-fix.md`](plans/orchestrate-board-oom-fix.md). **Tests:** `test/electron/crash-log.test.mjs`, `test/chat/orchestrate/board-boot-resume-oom.test.mts`, `test/orchestrate/task-history-trim.test.mts`. |
| **Local diagnostics viewer (MIN-401)** | Privacy-first capture + in-app viewer (no telemetry). **Capture:** renderer `window.onerror` / `unhandledrejection` → `POST /api/diagnostics/report` + Electron IPC; server `uncaughtException` / `unhandledRejection` + PTY/LSP/voice child exits → rotating JSONL [`server/diagnostics/ring-log.js`](../server/diagnostics/ring-log.js) at `~/.minnow/logs/diagnostics.jsonl` (2MB cap, 3 rotations; EMFILE-safe write queue). Merges with `crash.jsonl` for reads. **API:** [`server/diagnostics/middleware.js`](../server/diagnostics/middleware.js) — `/api/diagnostics/ping`, `/health`, `/errors`, `/logs`, `/report` (redacted markdown), `POST /api/diagnostics/clear` (delete diagnostics + crash JSONL). **UI:** Settings → **About** ([`src/ui/settings-about.ts`](../src/ui/settings-about.ts)) — health strip ([`src/ui/health-strip.ts`](../src/ui/health-strip.ts)), grouped errors, log tail, **Copy report**, **Clear**. **Agent:** `read_diagnostics` server tool (Debug mode `diagnostics` group; default permission `ask`). Redaction: [`server/diagnostics/redact.js`](../server/diagnostics/redact.js). Guide: [`documentation/guides/troubleshooting.md`](guides/troubleshooting.md) § Local diagnostics. **Tests:** `test/diagnostics/*.test.mjs`. |
| Preview events | `src/state/preview-events.ts` — `emitFileSaved` / `onFileSaved` |
| Parser | `src/lib/list-directory-parse.ts` |
| State / prefs | `src/state/file-panel.ts` → `config.json` `filePanel` via `GET/PUT /api/config/meta` (`mergeConfigMeta` in [`server/config/validators.js`](../server/config/validators.js) whitelists `rightPaneMode`, `previewSource`, `previewAutoReload`, `openViewerTabs`, `activeViewerTab`, `previewTabs`, `activePreviewTab`, `fileSidebarWidth`; `browser.restoreBrowserTabs` in [`browser-meta.ts`](../src/config/browser-meta.ts)) |
| Styles | `src/styles/file-panel.css`, `src/styles/preview-panel.css` |
| Markup | `index.html` — `#fileSidebar`, `#workspaceSplit`, `#rightPaneColumn`, `#unifiedTabs`, `#fileViewerPane`, `#previewPane` |

**Tree row density (E4 / feature-21):** Default rows are compact (`min-height: 0`, tighter padding in `file-panel.css`); `@media (pointer: coarse)` restores `min-height: var(--touch-min)` (44px) and touch padding. Row hover/selection matches chat sidebar (`.chat-item-row`): fine-pointer `--surface-elevated` hover, `--accent` border when selected. Depth indent: `src/ui/file-tree-indent.ts` (`FILE_TREE_DEPTH_INDENT_PX` = 12, dir/file base 6 / 24), re-exported from `file-tree.ts`.

**Server:** Tree and viewer call `executeTool()` directly (`POST /api/tools`); tool catalog toggles in Settings are **not** required. Offline (`npm run dev`): empty state “Start with `npm start`…”. On boot, `detectLocalServer()` runs in `startApp()` **before** `initOsRouter()` so Code reload does not initialize the tree while the ping flag is still false (**MIN-436**); `initFilePanel()` + `onFilePanelServerAvailabilityChanged()` load the tree when the server is up (desktop Files drawer calls `ensureCodeWorkspaceModules()` first).

**Persistence (`filePanel`):** `fileSidebarCollapsed`, `fileSidebarWidth` (220–560px), `viewerOpen` (legacy; kept in sync with `rightPaneMode`), `rightPaneMode` (`viewer` | `preview` | null), `previewSource` (legacy; synced from active preview tab), `previewTabs` + `activePreviewTab` (MIN-224), `previewAutoReload`, `splitRatio` (0.35–0.75), `expandedDirs`, `selectedPath`, `openViewerTabs`, `activeViewerTab` (workspace paths only; `selectedPath` stays in sync with the active tab for tree highlight), `treeRoot`. **Workspace switch** clears viewer tabs (`applyWorkspaceSwitch` in `workspace-button.ts`). Legacy configs with only `selectedPath` migrate into `openViewerTabs` on load. No dedicated `localStorage` key when config API is up.

**Phase 2 — drag to composer:** File and folder rows in `src/ui/file-tree.ts` are draggable via `wireTreeRowDrag` (`effectAllowed` `copyMove` for composer copy + tree move; `suppressClick` after drag so clicks still open the viewer). Drop on Code (`#msgInput` / `.input-bar`), Chat app (`#chatAppInput`), or desktop (`#desktopInput` / `.mn-os-desktop-composer`) calls `attachWorkspacePathToComposer()` in `src/ui/workspace-composer-link.ts` (wired from `src/ui/composer-drop.ts`, MIME `application/x-minnow-workspace-file`): `composer-drop.ts` resolves the drop target to a textarea (`resolveComposerInputFromDropTarget`); `workspace-composer-link.ts` falls back to `getActiveComposerSurface()` when no override is passed. **Text/code files** insert the project-relative path into that composer (agents use `read_file`; matches `path/to/file` in the base prompt). **Images** still queue a workspace chip (`kind: workspace`) and on send `resolveWorkspaceReferences()` loads bytes via `src/attachments/workspace-image-read.ts` → `kind: image` + `dataUrl` for VLM (`[image: name]` + `image_url` parts in `src/tools/loop.ts`). Paperclip / OS file picker attachments are unchanged (inlined `<file>` blocks for text/PDF).

**OS file drag-import:** Drag files from Windows Explorer / Finder onto any composer (`#msgInput`, `#chatAppInput`, `#desktopInput`, `.mn-os-desktop-composer`) → [`addAttachments()`](../src/attachments/store.ts) via [`classifyFileDrag`](../src/attachments/external-file-drop.ts) in `composer-drop.ts` (OS `Files` type takes precedence over `text/plain` path hints). `initComposerDrop()` binds **one drop handler per composer surface** (outermost of nested targets like `.input-bar` / `.input-bar-composer` / `#msgInput`) so a bubbling drop does not queue duplicate attachments. Drop onto a **folder row** or the file-tree host background in `#fileTreeHost` → [`importExternalFilesToWorkspace()`](../src/ui/import-external-files.ts) POSTs base64 bytes to UI-only server tool `import_workspace_file` in [`server/runtime/tools-middleware.js`](../server/runtime/tools-middleware.js) (10MB cap, same as attachments). Folders from Explorer are skipped with a status message (files only in v1).

**Tree CRUD (E1 / feature-18):** Context menu + shortcuts on file/folder rows call `executeTool` (`.file-tree-context-menu` uses light `--bg` popover like `workspace-menu`, not `--surface-elevated`) (`delete_path`, `move_file`, `copy_file`, `save_file`, `make_directory`) through [`src/ui/file-tree-ops.ts`](../src/ui/file-tree-ops.ts) with the same permission/approval gate as chat tools. **Rename (BUG-018):** **Rename…** / **F2** use inline edit on the tree row ([`file-tree-rename.ts`](../src/ui/file-tree-rename.ts) → `commitRename` → `move_file`), not `window.prompt`; cancel/unchanged names show status text; **F2** works with CodeMirror focused when a file is open in the viewer. **New file / folder:** folder row or empty-tree background → **New File…** / **New Folder…** opens an inline name field ([`file-tree-create.ts`](../src/ui/file-tree-create.ts) → `commitCreate` → `save_file` / `make_directory`), same input styling as rename; parent folder auto-expands; blocked while the file filter is active. Clicks and key events on rename/create inputs stop propagation so caret placement and typing (including Space) do not open files or toggle folders. `EBUSY`/`EPERM` from `move_file` map to actionable status messages. Feedback via top-bar `setStatus` (not a floating toast). Path helpers: [`src/ui/file-tree-path.ts`](../src/ui/file-tree-path.ts), clipboard: [`src/ui/file-tree-clipboard.ts`](../src/ui/file-tree-clipboard.ts). Menu UI: [`src/ui/file-tree-context-menu.ts`](../src/ui/file-tree-context-menu.ts) — executable plan files (`documentation/plans/*.md`, excluding `references/` / `verification/`) get **Open in orchestrator** (MIN-228) → [`launchBoardFromPlan`](../src/ui/orchestrate-launch.ts). Server flag for browse/CRUD: [`src/ui/file-tree-server.ts`](../src/ui/file-tree-server.ts) (synced from `init-file-panel.ts`). **Tests:** `test/file/file-tree-ops.test.mts`; UI modules under tsx use [`test/test-loader.mjs`](../test/test-loader.mjs) (stubs xterm CSS).

**Internal tree move (E3 / feature-20):** Drop a file or folder onto a **folder row** in `#fileTreeHost` → [`showMoveConfirmDialog`](../src/ui/file-tree-move-dialog.ts) (inline `#fileTreeMoveConfirm` strip in `#fileSidebar`, not a native dialog) → [`movePath`](../src/ui/file-tree-ops.ts) via existing `move_file` (no new REST route). Delegation: [`src/ui/file-tree-dnd.ts`](../src/ui/file-tree-dnd.ts) (`initFileTreeDnD` from `init-file-panel.ts`; `dragover` uses `activeDragSourcePath` from capture `dragstart` because `DataTransfer.getData` is empty during `dragover`). Invalid drops (cycle, same parent) use `computeMoveDestination` in `file-tree-path.ts`.

**Tests:** `test/file/list-directory-parse.test.mjs`, `test/file/file-tree-boot.test.mjs`, `test/file/file-tree-filter.test.mjs`, `test/file/file-tree-search.test.mjs`, `test/file/file-tree-filter-render.test.mjs`, `test/file/file-tree-layout.test.mjs` (E4 indent constants), `test/file/file-viewer-save.test.mjs` (happy-dom + tsx), `test/file/file-viewer-tab-store.test.mts`, `test/ui/preview-tab-store.test.mts`, `test/file/path-utils.test.mjs` (path + `computeMoveDestination`), `test/file/file-tree-move-dialog.test.mjs`, `test/file/file-tree-dnd.test.mjs`, `test/file/file-tree-ops.test.mts`, `test/file/file-tree-auto-refresh.test.mts`, `test/workspace-ref.test.ts`, `test/attachments/image-path.test.ts`, `test/attachments/external-file-drop.test.mjs`, `test/ui/composer-drop.test.mts`, `test/server/preview-middleware.test.mjs`, `test/ui/preview-panel.test.mts`, `test/ui/preview-close-tab.test.mts`, `test/ui/preview-restore-policy.test.mts`, `test/ui/file-layout-right-split.test.mts`, `test/ui/workspace-split-resize.test.mts`, `test/state/file-panel-preview.test.mts`, `scripts/step-11-smoke.mjs`.

### Vibe Coding Hub (empty chat landing)

When the active chat has **no messages**, `renderChatFromHistory()` paints the **Vibe Coding Hub** into `#chatArea` instead of the legacy empty state ([`src/ui/hub.ts`](../src/ui/hub.ts), [`src/styles/hub.css`](../src/styles/hub.css)). **Responsive (2026-07):** `#chatArea.chat-area--hub` is a **`container-type: inline-size`** query root (`container-name: hub`) so compact layout follows the **Code column width** (sidebar open, file split) — not only viewport `max-width`. At `720px` column width: project strip → 2-column grid (dev server full width), intent chips 5→2 (last odd chip spans full width), mode segments scroll horizontally inside the hub composer, recent threads 3→2 columns; `560px` stacks dev-server port/network controls and hides intent subtitles; `420px` → single-column intents and recent tiles. Viewport `600px` rules remain for safe-area padding; `@supports not (container-type)` falls back to viewport `720px`. Earlier **2026-06** notes: `44px` touch targets on coarse pointers; hover styles gated behind `(hover: hover)`. **Composer layout:** `.input-bar--hub` spans full `hub-inner` width; mode controls + tools (`composer-controls__trail`, far right) on the top row; attach + mic as inset actions inside `#msgInput`; send on row 2. The hub relocates `.input-bar` into `#hubComposerSlot` (class `input-bar--hub`, `main-column--hub`) so mode segments sit inside the bordered composer shell; `#contextUsageRing` lives in `#composerControls` after the thinking toggle (`index.html`). In **Orchestrate** mode the plan `<select>` fills the hub input row (not the mode toolbar) via `input-bar--orchestrate-plan`. **Recent threads** (`.hub-tile`) use a title + mode chip row, a single-line relative time, and a single-line last-run stats row (`tok/s · TTFT · tok` spans, nowrap). **Dev server cell** ([`src/ui/hub-dev-server.ts`](../src/ui/hub-dev-server.ts)): workspace-scoped lifecycle driven by **`{workspaceRoot}/startup.md`** (YAML frontmatter: `command`, optional `cwd`, `healthUrl`, `port`, `stop.command`; template [`documentation/templates/startup.md`](templates/startup.md)). **Port** and **network** (This PC vs LAN) are editable in the hub strip and persist under `config.json` → `workspace.devServerSettingsByPath` (debounced on `input`, flushed on `blur` and **before** `POST …/start` so the port field does not revert to the default (`3000`, distinct from Minnow's own Vite port `5173`); [`server/dev-server/effective-guide.js`](../server/dev-server/effective-guide.js) merges them at start (health probe on `127.0.0.1`, spawn env `PORT`/`HOST`, Vite-style `--port`/`--host` when applicable). `mergeConfigMeta` preserves `devServerSettingsByPath` / `devServerByPath` when run state is written. Without `startup.md` → **Set up** spawns a `generalPurpose` agent to author it. **Stopped** → primary click **`POST /api/workspace/dev-server/start`** (managed spawn; shell sub-agent fallback if the API fails). Status polls promote **`starting` → `running`** when `healthUrl` becomes healthy. Agents that call **`start_background_command`** with the guide command (or `register_dev_server: true`) sync into the same managed row. **Running** → primary click **`POST /api/workspace/dev-server/stop`**; when **starting** or **running**, a clickable **`#hubDevServerUrl`** link shows the dev URL (`localhost` + hub port, derived from `healthUrl` when set) and opens it in the **in-app preview panel** ([`openUrlInPreviewPanel`](../src/ui/preview-panel.ts)); logs stream in the background via [`ensureDevServerStream`](../src/ui/terminal-panel.ts) into the **Dev server** tab; **Console** opens that tab ([`openDevServerConsole`](../src/ui/terminal-panel.ts)); hub teardown calls [`stopDevServerStream`](../src/ui/terminal-panel.ts). Requires **`npm start`** (`isLocalServerAvailable`); Vite-only shows **server offline**. APIs: `GET /api/workspace/startup`, `GET /api/workspace/dev-server/status`, `GET|PUT /api/workspace/dev-server/settings`, `POST …/start`, `POST …/stop` ([`server/dev-server/manager.js`](../server/dev-server/manager.js)). Run state: `workspace.devServerByPath`. **Mode changes** call `setChatMode()` → `renderChatFromHistory()`; empty chats re-mount the hub. **Orchestrate board** must call `teardownHub()` before replacing `#chatArea` children. `teardownHub()` always **restores `.input-bar` to `#mainColumn` before** removing `#vibeHub` (the bar lives in `hub-composer-slot`). Empty chats selecting **Orchestrate** keep `viewMode: 'chat'` (hub visible) until there is history or an `orchestrateBoard`; use the board toggle to open kanban. Live metrics: `GET /api/workspace/loc`, `GET /api/system/vram`. Intent chips: [`src/ui/hub-intents.ts`](../src/ui/hub-intents.ts) — Build / Plan / Debug / Explain → modes + composer prefill; **Orchestrate a board** (MIN-229) opens [`renderOrchestrateHub`](../src/ui/orchestrate-hub.ts) instead of prefilling (icons: `/icons/hub-build.png`, `/icons/hub-plan.png`, `/icons/benchmark.png`, `/icons/expert-lab.png`, `/icons/orchestrate.png`). Tests: `test/ui/hub.test.mts`, `test/ui/hub-dom.test.mts`, `test/ui/hub-dev-server.test.mts`, `test/workspace/startup-parse.test.js`, `test/workspace/dev-server-api.test.js`, `test/workspace/dev-server-manager.test.js`. Plan: [`documentation/plans/hub-dev-server-startup.md`](plans/hub-dev-server-startup.md).

### Sub-agent orchestration (Step 09)

Parent tool loop can spawn **isolated sub-agents** (separate messages, model, tool subset). Results return as JSON aggregate tool results; child transcripts are **not** appended to parent `chat.history`.

**Visibility (feature 30 / MIN-167):** Each spawn shows a **sub-agent card** in the parent chat (`src/ui/sub-agent-cards.ts`) with live status; cards mount via **`getActiveChatMountElement()`** (`#desktopChatCol`, `#chatAppMessageCol`, or Code `#chatArea` / orchestrate split) — not a hardcoded `#chatArea`. After `renderChatFromHistory` rebuilds the transcript, **`renderPersistedSubAgentCardsForChat`** re-upserts both **`chat.subAgentRuns`** and in-flight rows from **`listActiveSubAgentRuns()`** so live runs survive thread switches and history refresh. Clicking opens a **slide-over drawer** with a **structured outcome** (summary, findings, artifacts) and a collapsible read-only transcript (`src/ui/sub-agent-drawer.ts`, `src/styles/sub-agent-drawer.css`). While a run is active, `src/agents/sub-agent-runner.ts` pushes transcript snapshots through `onMessagesChange` (system/user seed, streaming assistant deltas, tool rounds); `src/agents/controller/controller.ts` (re-exported from `orchestrator.ts`) copies them onto `run.messages` and emits `sub-agent-events`; the open drawer re-renders on each emit. **`list_sub_agents`** / **`get_sub_agent_status`** are **session-scoped** (any parent turn in the same chat; merged with `chat.subAgentRuns` after reload). Terminal runs are copied into `chat.subAgentRuns` (`PersistedSubAgentRun[]` in `src/types.ts`, including `structuredOutcome` / `budgetEvents`) via `src/state/sub-agent-session-sync.ts` so the drawer works after reload. Spawn rows anchor after the parent tool bubble using `data-tool-call-id` on `.tool-call-msg` (`src/tools/loop.ts`).

**Event-driven completion push (Build / General / Plan / Reef):** `spawn_sub_agent` defaults to **`wait: false`**. When a run settles, [`sub-agent-completion-push.ts`](../src/agents/sub-agent-completion-push.ts) coalesces pending completions and resumes the parent via `resumeParentChatWithMessage` (`suppressUserEcho: true`) once the chat is idle (`notifyChatStreamEnded` from [`loop.ts`](../src/tools/loop.ts)). Copy: [`sub-agent-resume-message.ts`](../src/chat/orchestrate/sub-agent-resume-message.ts). **Orchestrate** mode is excluded (supervisor owns stall recovery). Optional **check-in nudge** (`checkInNudgeMs`, default **120000**, Settings → Sub-agents; `0` disables) fires one non-cancelling parent note while a run is still active. Idempotent delivery tracks `deliveredRunIds`; transport errors retry once.

**Budgets + structured handoff (MIN-43 / feature #7):** Per-type `maxInputTokens` and `contextEnforcementPolicy` (`summarize` | `slide` | `truncate`) are enforced in `sub-agent-runner.ts` via shared [`src/chat/context-budget.ts`](../src/chat/context-budget.ts) before each completion; unrecoverable overflow sets `terminalReason: context_budget`. On success the runner runs a **final non-tool turn** that must parse to `SubAgentStructuredOutcome` (`summary`, `findings[]`, `artifacts[]`) validated against preset `summarySchema` ids in [`sub-agent-summary-schemas.ts`](../src/agents/sub-agent-summary-schemas.ts) (default `minnow.sub-agent.v1`). When the provider probe reports structured output, that final turn may attach `response_format` from [`sub-agent-outcome-response-format.ts`](../src/agents/sub-agent-outcome-response-format.ts) (strip-and-retry on upstream rejection, same pattern as constrained tool calls). **Tool rounds:** if the model returns no `tool_calls` deltas but puts JSON `{"tool_calls":[...]}` in assistant text (common with some local stacks), [`constrained-tool-content.ts`](../src/providers/constrained-tool-content.ts) merges those into normal `ToolCall[]` in both the sub-agent runner and main [`loop.ts`](../src/tools/loop.ts) stream path. If tools are enabled and **no tool round has run yet**, a one-line user **tool-use nudge** (`SUB_AGENT_TOOL_USE_NUDGE_INSTRUCTION` in [`turn-continuation.ts`](../src/tools/turn-continuation.ts)) requests another round before finalization (when `turn < maxToolTurns - 1`). **Preflight:** with the **default** runner only, an empty resolved `modelId` fails fast (`orchestrator.ts` after binding) so runs do not hit the provider with no model; injected test runners skip this gate. **Diagnostics:** empty final completion → `Empty response from provider on final turn`; parse/validation failures include a short text preview in `run.error` / `structuredOutcomeParseError`. The runner uses `StreamingContentAccumulator` plus reasoning-channel fallback (same pattern as benchmarks) for SSE and non-streaming fallback; if the work turn already returned valid outcome JSON in assistant prose, it is accepted without a separate finalization call; when `response_format` yields an empty body, finalization retries once without `response_format`. While a run is **running**, `get_sub_agent_status` / the drawer use `lastMessagePreview` for `summary` and omit `outcome` (no premature “no text output” placeholder). Dev console: `localStorage.minnowDebugSubAgent = '1'` logs work turns and finalization previews (`[sub-agent]` prefix). Parent tools receive `outcome` plus top-level `summary` in aggregate JSON (~32 KB cap in `formatAggregateResult`); full child transcripts stay in the drawer only. Sub-agents are mode-agnostic: any composer mode that exposes `spawn_sub_agent` (Build, Orchestrate, General, etc.) shares this runner.

| Concern | Location |
|---------|----------|
| Types | `src/agents/types.ts` |
| Config merge | `src/agents/sub-agent-config.ts`, `src/agents/defaults/sub-agents.json` |
| Controller | `src/agents/controller/{controller,scheduler,registry,wrapper,watchdog,persistence}.ts` — spawn, cancel, queue, `restartSubAgent`, `cancelAllForParentTurn`, parent-chat/turn indexes, list/status helpers; `deriveSubAgentTerminalReason` + `terminalReason` on `get_sub_agent_status` / aggregate JSON. **`src/agents/orchestrator.ts`** is a thin re-export shim (naming maps to the prompt's "controller"). **MIN-140 Phase 1 (shipped):** observe-only run supervision in `wrapper.ts` — `lastHeartbeatAt` / `lastProgressAt` / `progressSeq` on `SubAgentRun`, periodic heartbeat (H, default 7s), progress bumps from tool calls + transcript/stream deltas; thresholds `heartbeatIntervalMs`, `progressStallMs`, `heartbeatDeadMs` in `sub-agents.json`; chat-backed board tasks tracked via `chat-task:<chatId>` parallel map in `orchestrate-board-actions.ts`; heartbeat age badge on kanban cards (`orchestrate-board.ts`). **MIN-140 Phase 2 (shipped):** `RunLifecycle` on `SubAgentRun`; `watchdog.ts` ~5s tick — heartbeat/progress stall → `suspect`, `detectRepetition` on tool log, `committedResultRef` → `done_unacked`→`completed` (in-memory); tier-1 auto-retry once for non-mutating (`research`/`test` category or `explore`/`researcher` type), tier-2 `blocked` board task + cancel for mutating (`build`/`fix`/`shell`); progress-stall paused while tool-approval modal pending (`hasPendingToolApproval` / `isUserPromptLocked`); lifecycle badges on board cards (suspect amber, recovering blue, blocked red). **MIN-140 Phase 3 (shipped):** `persistence.ts` mirrors registry rows to `~/.minnow/runs/registry/<runId>.json` on spawn/lifecycle/settle; write-ahead aggregate commits to `~/.minnow/runs/results/<boardTaskId>__<attempt>.json` before `completed`; APIs `GET/PUT /api/config/runs/registry`, `GET/PUT /api/config/runs/results/:key` (`server/runs/`); startup `initControllerPersistence()` reconciles non-terminal rows — committed result → `completed` + board `complete`, else `interrupted` + tier-1 re-dispatch once for idempotent types or `blocked` on board; `supersedeOlderAttempts` on respawn. **MIN-140 Phase 4 (shipped):** `OrchestrateBoardState.executionMode` (`manual` \| `auto`); **`delegate_tasks`** tool + board **Auto-pilot** toggle; `autoDelegateNext` wave-ordered dispatch; `report.ts` planner lifecycle messages + dedupe. |
| Repetition detector | `src/agents/self-healing/detector.ts` — pure `detectRepetition` (wired by `watchdog.ts`) |
| Completion push | `src/agents/sub-agent-completion-push.ts` — terminal delivery + check-in nudge (non-orchestrate); **`report.ts`** — auto-pilot task reports to planner |
| Events | `src/agents/sub-agent-events.ts` |
| Runner | `src/agents/sub-agent-runner.ts` — headless tool loop (no tool-turn cap; MIN-428); context budget enforcement; **`resolveSamplerPreset({ kind: 'sub-agent' })`** (not parent drawer temperature; default `max_tokens` 2048); optional `response_format` on final JSON turn when probed; content-JSON tool merge; tool nudge before premature finalization; structured JSON final turn; accumulates `usage` / `stats` on `SubAgentRun` for Orchestrate metrics rollup (MIN-36) |
| Content-json tool merge | `src/providers/constrained-tool-content.ts` + `harmony-tool-calls.ts` — `mergeContentJsonToolCalls` prefers fuller assistant-content JSON over partial SSE `arguments`; recovers gpt-oss Harmony `commentary` channel tool calls (`to=functions.*` / `repo_browser.*`, `<\|message\|>{json}` or `code{json}`) when SSE `tool_calls` are empty; `HarmonyChannelRouter` suppresses commentary from prose; wired in `loop.ts` + `sub-agent-runner.ts` |
| Structured outcome | `src/agents/sub-agent-structured-outcome.ts`, `src/agents/sub-agent-summary-schemas.ts` |
| Sampler presets | `src/agents/sampler-types.ts`, `resolve-sampler.ts` |
| Tool subset | `src/agents/sub-agent-tools.ts`, `src/agents/resolve-sub-agent-tool-mode.ts` — Plan / Super Plan parent modes resolve sub-agent tools with **Build** policy |
| Prompts | `src/agents/shipped-sub-agent-prompts.ts`, `src/agents/prompts/sub-agents/*.md` |
| Parent tools | `spawn_sub_agent`, `cancel_sub_agent`, `list_sub_agents`, `get_sub_agent_status` in `src/tools/definitions.ts` |
| Executor | `src/tools/sub-agent-executor.ts`; routed in `src/tools/client.ts` |
| Parent abort | `src/tools/loop.ts` — `parentTurnId` + `cancelAllForParentTurn` on `AbortError` |
| Session hydrate | `src/state/sessions.ts` — `ensurePersistedSubAgentRuns` (preserves `structuredOutcome` / `budgetEvents`) |
| Boot | `src/main.ts` — `initSubAgentUi()` + `startOrchestrateWatchdog()` after `loadSessionsFromStorage()` |

**Built-in types:** `generalPurpose`, `explore`, `researcher`, `shell`, `explorer`, `debugger`, `bug-planner`, `plan-reviewer`, `reef-widget`.

**Config (`sub-agents.json`):** root `enabled`, `globalMaxConcurrent`, `defaultTimeoutMs`, **`checkInNudgeMs`** (parent check-in while sub-agent runs; default 120000; `0` off), optional `defaultMaxInputTokens`, `defaultContextEnforcementPolicy`, `defaultSummarySchema`; per-type `providerId`, `modelId`, `maxConcurrent`, `timeoutMs`, `maxInputTokens`, `contextEnforcementPolicy`, `summarySchema`, optional **`sampler`** (`temperature`, `topP`, `topK`, `minP`, `repetitionPenalty`, `maxTokens`) (Settings → **Sampler** per-type overrides), `allowedTools` (whitelist or null), `deniedTools`, optional `workAgentId`. Legacy `maxToolTurns` / `defaultMaxToolTurns` keys are stripped on load (MIN-428). Context budget exhaustion uses **`context_budget`** terminal reason. `get_sub_agent_status` exposes **`success: false`** and **`outcome`** when terminal; linked board tasks **`failed`** — never **`complete`** when the run did not succeed (`src/agents/sub-agent-outcome.ts`, `syncBoardTaskOnSettle`, `board_update_task` guard) (MIN-15 / MIN-10). Orchestrate supervisor R2 empty-summary detection prefers `structuredOutcome.summary` (`src/agents/supervisor/detector.ts`).

**Concurrency:** Over-cap spawns stay **`queued`** until a slot frees (FIFO global queue). Slots are tracked with `holdsConcurrencySlot` so cancelled queued runs do not corrupt the cap; `executeRun` wraps prompt setup + runner in one `try/catch` so a failed start always releases the slot and calls `drainQueue()`. **Run wall-clock timeout** and **check-in nudge** timers arm only when execution actually starts (`armRunTimers` in `registry.ts`, called from `executeRun` at the `running` transition) — queued wait time does not count toward `defaultTimeoutMs` / `checkInNudgeMs`. **`waitForSubAgent`** resolves on `subscribeSubAgentRuns` events (3s fallback for eviction/persistence races only) and **follows `supersededByRunId`** when the watchdog tier-1/tier-2 recovery cancels a run and respawns a replacement (Super Plan `plan-reviewer` waits no longer stop at the empty cancelled row). Terminal runs trim transcript tails on settle and evict from live maps after 60s (skipped in `MINNOW_TEST`). Registry mirrors are coalesced per `runId` (microtask flush). Empty per-type `providerId` / `modelId` inherit the parent chat binding (then global active provider/model) via [`resolveSubAgentModelBinding`](../src/agents/resolve-sub-agent-binding.ts) before `POST /api/generations`. Shipped defaults use empty `providerId` (legacy `lm-studio-local` + empty model migrates on merge). **OpenAI finalization:** when `response_format` is attached and `structuredOutputStreaming` is false, finalization uses non-streaming completions; `extractAssistantCompletionText` reads `message.parsed` / `refusal`. Empty post-tool prose retries once via `EMPTY_POST_TOOL_CONTINUE_INSTRUCTION` (parity with main chat). `sanitizeCompletionBodyForProvider` strips LM Studio-only fields for `openai-v1` upstream bodies.

**Step 19 hooks (exported, not wired):** `restartSubAgent`, `recordToolCallForRun`, `getRunToolCallFingerprint`.

**Persistence:** `GET/PUT /api/config/sub-agents` when `npm start`; client mirror `minnow.subAgents` in `localStorage` when Vite-only. Settled sub-agent transcripts also persist on **`chat.subAgentRuns`** in `sessions/state.json` (capped message list).

**Tests:** `test/sub-agents/**/*.test.mts`, `test/agents/sampler-resolve.test.mts`, `test/agents/sampler-server.test.mjs`, `test/ui/sub-agent-cards.test.mts` (Code + desktop mount). Verification: [`documentation/plans/verification/step-09.md`](plans/verification/step-09.md).

| Method | Path | Purpose |
|--------|------|---------|
| `GET/PUT` | `/api/config/sub-agents` | User overrides for `sub-agents.json` |

### Setup profiles API (Feature #13 / MIN-49)

Portable **Minnow setup profiles** (distinct from terminal **shell profiles**). Stored as `~/.minnow/profiles/<id>.json` (`schemaVersion: 1`). `config.json` tracks `activeSetupProfileId`, `workspaceProfiles` (normalized path → profile id), and `workspaceProfileAutoApply` (default **false**).

| Method | Path | Purpose |
|--------|------|---------|
| `GET` | `/api/profiles` | List `{ id, label, updatedAt }` |
| `GET/PUT/DELETE` | `/api/profiles/:id` | CRUD bundle |
| `POST` | `/api/profiles/:id/duplicate` | `{ newId, newLabel? }` |
| `POST` | `/api/profiles/:id/activate` | Apply bundle to `config.json`, `tools.json`, `work-agents.json`, `sub-agents.json`, optional `rules.json` / `skills.json`; may mirror embedded `parts` into `prompt-configs/` |
| `POST` | `/api/profiles/capture` | Snapshot current live settings into new/existing id |
| `POST` | `/api/profiles/import` | `{ bundle, mode: 'create' \| 'replace' }` |
| `GET` | `/api/profiles/:id/export` | Download `*.minnow-profile.json` |
| `POST` | `/api/profiles/migrate-from-prompt-configs` | One-shot import `prompt-configs/*.json` → profiles |
| `PUT` | `/api/profiles/workspace-default` | `{ workspacePath, profileId \| null }` |

**Client:** [`src/config/profiles-client.ts`](../src/config/profiles-client.ts). **Server:** [`server/profiles/`](../server/profiles/). **Tests:** `test/profiles/*.test.mjs`.

**Send path:** `resolveOutboundSystemMessages()` (expert routing + `resolveComposedSystemPrompt()` + `loadUserRules()`) → `pushOutboundSystemMessages()` in [`api-system-messages.ts`](../src/tools/api-system-messages.ts) via `buildApiMessages()` in [`loop.ts`](../src/tools/loop.ts) and plain send in [`chat.ts`](../src/api/chat.ts). Produces **one or two** leading `role: system` messages: composed programmatic stack first, then optional global user rules when `rules.json` has `enabled: true` and non-empty `text`. Legacy `#systemPrompt` textarea is fallback when compose returns empty. User rules are **not** a `PART_ORDER` composer part. Sub-agent runs do not receive global user rules (v1).

**User rules (Feature 24):** Settings → **Agents → Rules** (`#/settings/rules`; legacy `#/settings/knowledge` redirects here). Client: [`src/config/user-rules.ts`](../src/config/user-rules.ts) (`loadUserRules`, `saveUserRules`, `getUserRulesPayloadForSend`); localStorage key `minnow.userRules` when Vite-only. **Tests:** `test/config/rules-crud.test.js`, `test/tools/build-api-messages-rules.test.mts`.

**Tests:** `test/prompts/*.test.mjs` + `test/prompts/*.test.js`. Verification: [`documentation/plans/verification/step-04.md`](plans/verification/step-04.md).

**Step 05 tests:** `test/modes/*.test.mts`. Verification: [`documentation/plans/verification/step-05.md`](plans/verification/step-05.md).

### Providers API (`npm start` only)

Registered in [`server/providers/routes.js`](../server/providers/routes.js) before Vite SPA. LLM **secrets never** returned from GET; proxy routes attach auth server-side.

| Method | Path | Purpose |
|--------|------|---------|
| `GET` | `/api/providers` | `{ providers: ProviderPublic[], activeProviderId }` |
| `GET` | `/api/providers/:id` | Public profile + `hasApiKey` / `hasBearer` flags |
| `POST` | `/api/providers` | Create provider dir + `profile.json` |
| `PUT` | `/api/providers/:id` | Update profile (non-secret) |
| `DELETE` | `/api/providers/:id` | Remove provider (**409** if last) |
| `PUT` | `/api/providers/:id/secrets` | Update encrypted `secrets.json`; response redacts values |
| `POST` | `/api/providers/:id/set-active` | Sets `config.json` `activeProviderId` |
| `GET` | `/api/providers/:id/models` | Proxy upstream models (auth injected) |
| `POST` | `/api/providers/:id/models/load` | Proxy model load (LM Studio v0) |
| `POST` | `/api/providers/:id/models/unload` | Proxy model unload (LM Studio v0) |
| `GET` | `/api/providers/:id/capabilities` | Read `capabilities.json` (or `{ models: {} }` when missing) |
| `POST` | `/api/providers/:id/capabilities/probe` | Per-model matrix probe; optional `{ modelIds?, selectedModelId? }` |
| `POST` | `/api/providers/:id/probe-capabilities` | Structured-output probe (`response_format` / json_schema); body optional `{ modelId?, selectedModelId? }` — requires a **loaded** model (resolved from catalog when omitted) |

**`capabilities.json`:** Provider-level `structuredOutput`, `structuredOutputWithTools`, `structuredOutputStreaming`, `probeError` (#10). Per-model `vision`, `tools`, `streaming`, `grammar`, `contextLength`, `loadState`, `api` (resolved upstream API per model — MIN-322), `sources`, `probeErrors` (MIN-48). **Vision catalog signal:** `type === 'vlm'` **or** LM Studio 0.4.8+ upstream `capabilities.vision: true` on `type: llm` rows — normalized to `catalogVision` in [`normalizeModelsResponse`](../server/providers/paths.js) (upstream `capabilities` stripped before merge). Probes run only from **Settings → Providers → Edit provider**: **Probe models** (`POST .../capabilities/probe`; on `lm-studio-v0`, defaults to **loaded** models only, up to 8 — no chat probes against unloaded rows; per-model **`api`** resolution picks OpenAI fetch vs Messages bridge) and **Probe structured output** (`POST .../probe-capabilities`; requires a **loaded** model on `openai-v1` paths; models resolved to **`anthropic-v1`** record `structuredOutput: false` with an explicit `probeError` — v1 bridge does not map `response_format` yet) are separate actions. **Refresh models** loads the list and merges any existing `capabilities.json` from disk but does not probe. Server: [`capabilities-store.js`](../server/providers/capabilities-store.js), [`capability-probe.js`](../server/providers/capability-probe.js). Client: [`model-capabilities.ts`](../src/providers/model-capabilities.ts), [`capability-probe.ts`](../src/providers/capability-probe.ts) (structured output), [`vision-model.ts`](../src/providers/vision-model.ts), picker [`capability-badges.ts`](../src/providers/capability-badges.ts).

**`apiKind`:** Three kinds — `lm-studio-v0` (default paths `/api/v0/...`), `openai-v1` (`/v1/chat/completions`), and **`anthropic-v1`** (Anthropic Messages API). For `anthropic-v1`, **`chatCompletionsPath` is the messages path** (Settings UI labels it **Messages path**); default `/v1/messages`. **Mixed gateways (MIN-322):** keep `apiKind: openai-v1`, set **`autoApi: true`**, and add a separate **`messagesPath`** (derived from `chatCompletionsPath` when omitted). [`resolveModelApi`](../server/generations/resolve-model-api.js) / [`src/lib/resolve-model-api.mjs`](../src/lib/resolve-model-api.mjs) picks `anthropic-v1` vs `openai-v1` per model (catalog `owned_by`, `anthropic/` namespace, `claude-*` heuristics; optional **`modelApiOverrides`** map wins). Each catalog row carries resolved **`api`** for client thinking/sanitization. Model load/unload is disabled (`supportsModelLoadUnload: false`) for non–`lm-studio-v0`. **`baseUrl`** may include a gateway path prefix (e.g. `https://opencode.ai/zen`, `https://openrouter.ai/api`); path fields stay relative (`/v1/models`, `/v1/chat/completions`, `/v1/messages`). OpenCode Zen path normalization in [`opencode-zen.js`](../server/providers/opencode-zen.js) avoids duplicate `/v1` when the base already ends with `/v1`.

**Auth (`authStyle`):** `bearer` (OpenCode Zen OpenAI chat path — key in `Authorization: Bearer …`; Claude Messages bridge sends the same key as `x-api-key`), `x-api-key` (native Anthropic — key in `X-Api-Key`), or legacy `api-key`. Secrets live in encrypted `secrets.json` (`apiKey` field from Settings; optional `bearerToken`).

**Example `profile.json` snippets:**

OpenCode Zen (mixed GPT + Claude gateway — recommended):

```json
{
  "label": "OpenCode Zen",
  "baseUrl": "https://opencode.ai/zen",
  "apiKind": "openai-v1",
  "autoApi": true,
  "authStyle": "bearer",
  "modelsPath": "/v1/models",
  "chatCompletionsPath": "/v1/chat/completions",
  "messagesPath": "/v1/messages"
}
```

OpenRouter (mixed catalog):

```json
{
  "label": "OpenRouter",
  "baseUrl": "https://openrouter.ai/api",
  "apiKind": "openai-v1",
  "autoApi": true,
  "authStyle": "bearer",
  "modelsPath": "/v1/models",
  "chatCompletionsPath": "/v1/chat/completions",
  "messagesPath": "/v1/messages"
}
```

OpenCode Zen (Claude-only — legacy `anthropic-v1` entry):

```json
{
  "label": "OpenCode Zen",
  "baseUrl": "https://opencode.ai",
  "apiKind": "anthropic-v1",
  "authStyle": "bearer",
  "modelsPath": "/zen/v1/models",
  "chatCompletionsPath": "/zen/v1/messages"
}
```

Native Anthropic:

```json
{
  "label": "Anthropic",
  "baseUrl": "https://api.anthropic.com",
  "apiKind": "anthropic-v1",
  "authStyle": "x-api-key",
  "modelsPath": "/v1/models",
  "chatCompletionsPath": "/v1/messages",
  "customHeaders": { "anthropic-version": "2023-06-01" }
}
```

**Generations bridge (`anthropic-v1`):** Chat is not proxied raw to `chatCompletionsPath`. [`server/generations/upstream.js`](../server/generations/upstream.js) calls [`resolveModelApi`](../server/generations/resolve-model-api.js) **per failover candidate** and branches to [`server/generations/anthropic/`](../server/generations/anthropic/) (`pump.js`, `openai-to-core-messages.js`, `openai-sse-encoder.js`, `provider-runtime.js`, `gateway-fetch.js`) when the resolved API is `anthropic-v1`; otherwise raw OpenAI SSE proxy. The bridge uses the **`ai`** package + **`@ai-sdk/anthropic`** (`streamText` / `generateText`) to accept the existing OpenAI-shaped `ChatCompletionBody` and emit OpenAI-compatible SSE for the client. `deriveAnthropicBaseUrl` maps `baseUrl` + **`messagesPath`** to the SDK `baseURL` (e.g. `https://opencode.ai` + `/zen/v1/messages` → `https://opencode.ai/zen/v1`). **Gateway proxies (OpenCode Zen):** non-native hosts wrap the provider with `supportsNativeStructuredOutput: false` / `supportsStrictTools: false` (prevents the AI SDK from attaching `structured-outputs-2025-11-13` on tool requests); `createAnthropicGatewayFetch` + `transformRequestBody` strip unsupported `anthropic-beta` values, `output_config`, `thinking: { type: "disabled" }`, `strict`, `tool_choice.disable_parallel_tool_use` (Console/Bedrock upstream rejects it), `eager_input_streaming`, and JSON Schema draft metadata / unsupported constraints (`additionalProperties`, `maxItems`, `minItems` > 1, `minLength`, `maxLength`, string `pattern`, etc.) from outbound bodies; `adjustAnthropicRequestForGateway` omits thinking/effort/`body.thinking` and sets `toolStreaming: false` when tools are attached. On non-2xx gateway responses, `createAnthropicGatewayFetch` best-effort dumps the sanitized outbound body + upstream response text to `~/.minnow/debug/anthropic-gateway-last-error.json` (local diagnostics only, never transmitted) so opaque `"Upstream request failed"` Console errors can be root-caused without a live repro script. **Extended thinking:** newer Claude families (`claude-sonnet-5`, `claude-opus-4-6`, `claude-sonnet-4-6`, …) require `thinking.type: adaptive` (not `enabled` + `budget_tokens`); catalog inference + [`normalizeAnthropicProviderOptions`](../src/lib/anthropic-thinking-style.mjs) in `pump.js` rewrite legacy enabled budgets before upstream calls. **Tool loops:** Anthropic requires assistant `tool_use` history to be preceded by signed thinking blocks when thinking stays enabled — the bridge forwards `signature_delta` as SSE `reasoning_signature`, persists it on `AssistantToolCallMessage.thinkingSignature`, replays via `reasoning` + `reasoning_signature` on outbound API rows, and maps to AI SDK `reasoning` parts in `openai-to-core-messages.js`. When unsigned tool history is detected, thinking is omitted for that request instead of 400ing. Sampler `temperature` / `top_p` are omitted whenever thinking is active. Tool-result messages map `output` as `{ type: 'text', value }` for AI SDK schema compliance. **Tool-call streaming dedup (bug fix):** the AI SDK's `fullStream` emits `tool-input-start` → `tool-input-delta`(s) → `tool-input-end` for incremental tool-call streaming, **then also a terminal `tool-call` part carrying the same already-complete name + input** (for non-streaming consumers). `createOpenAiSseEncoder` in `openai-sse-encoder.js` tracks which `toolCallId`s were already streamed incrementally and drops the terminal `tool-call` re-encode for those ids — otherwise the client's `mergeToolCallDelta` ([`src/api/chat.ts`](../src/api/chat.ts)) concatenates the full name/arguments a second time (e.g. `read_file` → `read_fileread_file`, glued/invalid JSON input), which the tool executor then rejects as an unknown tool and which Anthropic gateways 400 on for any later turn that replays the corrupted `tool_use` block. This reproduced on **every** tool call turn (not just persisted history), so it looked identical across dev-server restarts until traced via the gateway failure dump above.

**v1 limitations (MIN-329):** Constrained tool calls (`response_format` / `json_schema`) and structured-output probes are **not mapped** for `anthropic-v1` yet — capability probe records `structuredOutput: false` with an explicit `probeError`; keep global/per-provider constrained decoding off for Claude providers. Anthropic-specific features (prompt cache control, web search, code execution) are out of scope. Plan: [`documentation/plans/anthropic-messages-api.md`](plans/anthropic-messages-api.md).

**Setup — Claude via OpenCode Zen:** (1) Obtain a Zen API key from OpenCode. (2) `npm start`. (3) **Settings → Providers → Add provider** — use preset **OpenCode Zen** (or manually: API style **OpenAI v1**, enable **Auto-detect API per model**, base URL `https://opencode.ai/zen`, paths `/v1/models`, `/v1/chat/completions`, `/v1/messages`, auth **Bearer**, paste key under secrets). For Zen Go, use base URL `https://opencode.ai/zen/go` with the same `/v1/...` paths. (4) **Refresh models**, pick a `claude-*` or `gpt-*` model in the composer. (5) Optional: **Probe models** to populate capability badges.

For `lm-studio-v0` and `openai-v1`, **chat completions** are not proxied on `/api/providers`; all LLM streams use [`/api/generations`](#backend-owned-generations-phase-1) (raw upstream fetch for those kinds; `anthropic-v1` uses the bridge above).

**Seed:** On first `npm start` with empty `providers/`, creates `lm-studio-local` from legacy `config.json` `serverUrl` or `http://localhost:1234`.

Client: [`src/providers/`](../src/providers/) (`store.ts`, `resolve.ts`, `fetch-models.ts`, `model-capabilities.ts`, `fetch-chat.ts`). Models/load/unload always use `/api/providers/:id/...`; chat uses [`postChatCompletions`](../src/providers/fetch-chat.ts) → `/api/generations` (shim) or [`src/api/generations.ts`](../src/api/generations.ts) (main tool loop).

### Models app API (`npm start` only)

Full-page **Models** hub at `#/app/models/<section>` (`src/ui/models-page.ts`, `src/styles/models-page.css`). Nav groups: **Local library** (Recommendations, Installed, Library), **Inference** (Providers, Routing, Sampler, Thinking), **More** (Voice, Usage & cost). **Recommendations** (hardware-ranked catalog + local cache badges; recommend rows place fit badge, **Download** / **Serve**, and on-disk dot inline in `models-recommend-row__trailing`; download progress renders below the meta line; meta shows **file size** (`size_gb`, weights-only estimate from `estimateFileSizeGb()` in `src/models/quant.ts`) and **RAM to run** (`required_gb`, includes KV cache); **filter toolbar** in `renderFilters()` (`src/ui/models/recommend-panel.ts`) is a single-row instrument bar: search (borderless, flex-grow) | use-case / quant / sort selects | context label + mono value + range | **Fit only** checkbox (nested label without duplicate `htmlFor`; when unchecked, `rankModels()` in `src/models/fit.ts` reserves ~25% of the result limit for `too_tight` rows so they are not pushed out by score sort), groups separated by hairline dividers in `models-page.css`; **GPU budget** toggle in `src/ui/models/recommend-panel.ts` maps RAM-only vs GPU pool through `hardwareForGpuBudget()` in `src/models/fit.ts` — RAM sets `hasGpu: false`, GPU sets `gpu_only: true` on the selected `gpuGroups` pool; **use-case filter** dropdown values are `inferUseCase()` keys — `general`, `coding`, `reasoning`, etc. — not raw catalog `use_case` strings), **Installed** (downloads + serve), **Library** (HF token + extra scan folders), plus reparented provider/routing/sampler/thinking/usage settings. Server: [`server/models/`](../server/models/) via [`createModelsMiddleware()`](../server/models/routes.js).

| Method | Path | Purpose |
|--------|------|---------|
| `GET` | `/api/models/ping` | Health check |
| `GET` | `/api/models/cached` | Scan HF hub cache, `~/.minnow/models/artifacts`, Ollama tags, `config.json` → `models.modelDirs[]` |
| `GET` | `/api/models/config` | Masked HF token status + model dirs |
| `PUT` | `/api/models/config` | Save `hfToken` / `modelDirs` (plaintext v1) |
| `GET` | `/api/models/profiles?model=…` | llama.cpp Quality/Balanced/Speed presets from hardware |
| `GET` | `/api/models/downloads` | Download job list |
| `POST` | `/api/models/download` | Start HF GGUF download (SSE progress on `…/stream`; jobs left `queued`/`running` in `downloads.json` are marked failed on server restart; truncated HF streams surface as `failed` with error) |
| `POST` | `/api/models/download/:id/cancel` | Cancel download |
| `GET` | `/api/models/installed` | GGUF artifacts from `~/.minnow/models/artifacts/` **and** `config.json` → `models.modelDirs[]` (deduped by absolute path) + download history |
| `GET` | `/api/models/runtimes` | Detect llama-server (PATH, `vendor/llama-cpp`, or `~/.minnow/models-runtime/llama-cpp`), Ollama, LM Studio — includes installed `variant` + `gpuCapable` |
| `GET` | `/api/models/llama-runtime` | Managed llama.cpp binary path, variant, version, installable variants, preferred auto-detect |
| `POST` | `/api/models/llama-runtime/install` | Install/reinstall runtime `{ variant?, tag?, reinstall? }` (CUDA/Vulkan/CPU prebuilds) |
| `GET` | `/api/models/llama-runtime/install/stream` | SSE install progress (`phase`, `percent`, `message`) |
| `GET/PUT` | `/api/models/llama-cpp-config` | Read/write default serve settings in `~/.minnow/llama-cpp.json` |
| `GET/POST` | `/api/models/serve` | List / start serve (requires installed `llama-server`; no silent auto-install — use install API first; llama.cpp spawn with full args via [`buildLlamaServerArgs()`](../server/models/llama-args.js); single provider `llama-cpp-local`) |
| `POST` | `/api/models/serve/:id/stop` | Stop serve; disables `llama-cpp-local` when no active llama serves remain |

Hardware probe: `GET /api/system/hardware` (`?fresh=1` bypasses 24h cache) — [`server/system/hardware.js`](../server/system/hardware.js). Client fit ranking: [`src/models/fit.ts`](../src/models/fit.ts) + bundled [`catalog.json`](../src/models/catalog.json). **llama.cpp runtime:** [`server/models/llama-runtime.js`](../server/models/llama-runtime.js) + [`server/models/llama-variant.js`](../server/models/llama-variant.js) resolve `llama-server` from PATH → optional app `vendor/llama-cpp/` → managed `~/.minnow/models-runtime/llama-cpp/` (GPU-aware managed install: CUDA when `nvidia-smi` works, else Vulkan, else CPU; Windows CUDA pulls main + `cudart` companion zips; variant stored in `meta.json`). **Install prompt (MIN-163):** [`src/ui/models/llama-install-prompt.ts`](../src/ui/models/llama-install-prompt.ts) — when `llama-server` is missing, `openServeDialog()` shows a confirm modal with SSE progress (`subscribeLlamaInstallProgress`) before the serve-settings dialog; non-installable platforms get manual PATH guidance + link to Settings → Servers. **Serve dialog:** [`src/ui/models/serve-dialog.ts`](../src/ui/models/serve-dialog.ts) — preset/advanced launch controls before spawn (Installed + Recommendations **Serve** buttons). **Single provider:** all llama.cpp serves upsert [`llama-cpp-local`](../server/providers/store.js) (`llama.cpp (local)` label); legacy `models-xxxxxxxx` rows migrated away on startup. **Settings → Servers:** [`llama-cpp`](../server/servers/catalog.js) managed row — runtime install/variant picker + active serves list. Plan: [`documentation/plans/llama-cpp-runtime-control.md`](plans/llama-cpp-runtime-control.md). **Use in chat** after serve: [`selectProviderModel()`](../src/api/models.ts) sets `#modelSelect` composite key. Tests: `test/models/*.test.mjs`, `test/models/fit.test.mts`, `test/os/models-app.test.mts`.

### Built-in Voice system (`npm start` only)

Local Whisper STT + Qwen3-TTS with optional provider/browser fallbacks. Plan: [`documentation/plans/built-in-voice-system.md`](plans/built-in-voice-system.md). UI: **Settings → Audio** ([`src/ui/settings-audio.ts`](../src/ui/settings-audio.ts)) for mic/speaker device routing; **Models → Voice** ([`src/ui/models/voice-panel.ts`](../src/ui/models/voice-panel.ts), `#/app/models/voice`) for runtime health strip, STT/TTS tab panels, unified catalog/installed lists, configuration forms, and test bench. Styles: [`src/styles/models-page.css`](../src/styles/models-page.css) (`.models-voice-*`). Legacy `#/settings/voice` redirects via [`src/os/router.ts`](../src/os/router.ts) and [`src/ui/settings-page.ts`](../src/ui/settings-page.ts). Composer dictation: [`src/ui/composer-voice.ts`](../src/ui/composer-voice.ts) — mic button states in [`src/styles/voice.css`](../src/styles/voice.css): pulsing ring while **listening**, level-reactive bars + icon scale when **detecting audio**, spinner while **starting voice** (local worker boot) or **transcribing** (provider batch path only), brief **error** accent when boot fails. **Local backend auto-start (MIN-160):** when STT uses `backend === 'local'` and the Python worker is stopped, the mic button calls [`ensureVoiceWorker()`](../src/voice/api-client.ts) (`POST /api/voice/runtime/start` + poll `GET /api/voice/runtime/status` until `health === 'ready'`) before opening the mic — no manual **Start** in Models → Voice required when the runtime is already installed. **Local streaming STT** (`stt.backend === 'local'` + `streamingSupported`): 16 kHz PCM over WebSocket [`/api/stt/ws`](../server/stt/stt-ws.js) with live text in the composer via [`src/voice/dictation-range.ts`](../src/voice/dictation-range.ts) + [`src/voice/stt-stream-client.ts`](../src/voice/stt-stream-client.ts); provider STT keeps batch `POST /api/stt/transcribe`. Plan: [`documentation/plans/streaming-stt.md`](plans/streaming-stt.md). Read-aloud: [`src/ui/voice-controls.ts`](../src/ui/voice-controls.ts) — respects `voice.tts.backend` (`local` | `provider` | `browser`) and `setSinkId` when available; **re-fetches** `GET /api/tts/status` on each play click (avoids stale worker cache after Models → Voice start). **Local streaming TTS** (`tts.backend === 'local'` + `streamingSupported`): assistant read-aloud uses [`src/voice/tts-stream-client.ts`](../src/voice/tts-stream-client.ts) + [`src/voice/audio-playback-queue.ts`](../src/voice/audio-playback-queue.ts) over [`/api/tts/ws`](../server/tts/tts-ws.js) (Int16 PCM chunks, Hann crossfade gapless Web Audio); on stream failure, **falls back** to batch `POST /api/tts/synthesize` (same path as Models → Voice test bench). Batch synthesis also used for provider backend and when streaming is disabled. Toggle **Models → Voice → Text-to-speech → Configuration → Runtime → Streaming** (`voice.tts.streaming`, default on). Plan: [`documentation/plans/streaming-tts.md`](plans/streaming-tts.md). Plain-text extraction for TTS: [`src/ui/voice-speech-text.ts`](../src/ui/voice-speech-text.ts) (re-exported from voice-controls). **Voice chat hooks (deferred):** [`src/voice/voice-session.ts`](../src/voice/voice-session.ts) defines `VoiceSession` + `VoiceSessionStub` for half-duplex turn-taking; [`speakStreamingText()`](../src/ui/voice-controls.ts) is the chat integration hook for streaming playback without read-aloud UI — full duplex / push-to-talk wiring is not shipped.

**Config** (`config.json` → `voice`): nested `audio`, `stt`, `tts`, `limits` — normalized in [`server/config/validators.js`](../server/config/validators.js) (`normalizeVoiceConfig`). GPU-first defaults: CPU → `openai/whisper-base` + Qwen 0.6B `float32`; CUDA → `whisper-small` + `bfloat16`. **Migration:** when `providerId` is set but `~/.minnow/voice/installed.json` has no local STT/TTS models, backends stay `provider` (via `loadVoiceConfig()` + `installedManifest`).

**Paths under `~/.minnow/`:**

| Path | Purpose |
|------|---------|
| `voice/venv/` | Python venv (torch, torchaudio, transformers, faster-whisper, soundfile, optional qwen-tts + imageio-ffmpeg) |
| `voice/meta.json` | Provisioner metadata |
| `voice/installed.json` | Downloaded STT/TTS model manifest (auto-rebuilt from `models/voice/` when missing or empty) |
| `voice/downloads.json` | Active download job index |
| `voice/refs/` | Uploaded reference audio for voice clone |
| `voice/clone-prompts/` | Saved clone prompt JSON |
| `models/voice/<repo>/` | HF snapshot artifacts |

**Python worker** ([`server/voice/python/worker.py`](../server/voice/python/worker.py)): stdlib HTTP on ephemeral port — `GET /health`, `GET /voice/capabilities`, `POST /stt/transcribe`, `POST /stt/stream` (SSE segment/final), `POST /tts/synthesize` (batch WAV), `POST /tts/stream` (SSE PCM chunks — NDJSON body `{"op":"start"|"append"|"finish",...}` + header `X-Tts-Config: <JSON>`; events `chunk` / `final` / `error`), `POST /models/load`, `POST /models/unload`. TTS load calls `enable_streaming_optimizations()` when the streaming qwen-tts fork is installed (`MINNOW_TTS_USE_COMPILE=false` disables torch.compile for multi-threaded workers). Streaming tuning lives in `voice.tts.local.streaming` (emit/decode windows, overlap, repetition penalty). STT and TTS models can be **resident simultaneously** — `/models/load` evicts only the same kind; `/models/unload` requires `{ kind: "stt" | "tts" }`. Capabilities expose `sttLoaded`, `ttsLoaded`, `loadedSttModelId`, `loadedTtsModelId`, `loadedTtsMode` (legacy `modelLoaded` / `loadedKind` / `loadedModelId` retained). GPU inference is serialized via `threading.Lock`. Local STT uses **faster-whisper** (CTranslate2); batch and streaming share the same loaded model. Catalog ids like `openai/whisper-small` download **Systran/faster-whisper-small** (`model.bin`) via [`server/voice/stt-catalog.js`](../server/voice/stt-catalog.js); HF transformers snapshots (`model.safetensors`) are not passed to faster-whisper — the worker falls back to the size alias (`small`, `base`, …) and uses a local CTranslate2 dir only when `model.bin` is present. Lifecycle: [`server/voice/runtime-manager.js`](../server/voice/runtime-manager.js) (`installRuntime`, `repairRuntime`, `startWorker`, `stopWorker`); after health check, [`server/voice/preload.js`](../server/voice/preload.js) warms configured local STT/TTS models in parallel. Provisioner: [`server/voice/provision.js`](../server/voice/provision.js) installs the **qwen-tts streaming fork** (`xxddccaa/Qwen3-TTS-streaming`), optional **flash-attn** on CUDA (`MAX_JOBS=4` on Windows), then re-pins torch/torchaudio; reuses shared `python-build-standalone` from `~/.minnow/servers/python` and installs `faster-whisper` on Repair.

**Node bridges:** [`server/voice/local-stt.js`](../server/voice/local-stt.js) + [`server/stt/middleware.js`](../server/stt/middleware.js) + [`server/stt/stt-ws.js`](../server/stt/stt-ws.js) (`/api/stt/ws`); [`server/voice/local-tts.js`](../server/voice/local-tts.js) (`createLocalTtsStream` → worker `/tts/stream`) + [`server/tts/middleware.js`](../server/tts/middleware.js) + [`server/tts/tts-ws.js`](../server/tts/tts-ws.js) (`/api/tts/ws` — JSON control frames + Int16 PCM binary). Downloads: [`server/voice/download.js`](../server/voice/download.js) — TTS auto-installs `Qwen/Qwen3-TTS-Tokenizer-12Hz` when required; `listInstalledVoiceModels()` rebuilds `installed.json` from `~/.minnow/models/voice/` when the manifest is empty but snapshot folders exist on disk.

| Method | Path | Purpose |
|--------|------|---------|
| `GET` | `/api/voice/runtime/status` | Venv + worker health |
| `POST` | `/api/voice/runtime/install` | Provision venv (+ SSE stream) |
| `POST` | `/api/voice/runtime/repair` | Reinstall broken venv |
| `POST` | `/api/voice/runtime/start` / `stop` | Worker process |
| `POST` | `/api/voice/download` | Start HF download `{ modelId, kind: stt \| tts }` |
| `GET` | `/api/voice/download/:id/stream` | SSE progress |
| `POST` | `/api/voice/download/:id/cancel` | Cancel job |
| `GET` | `/api/voice/models/installed` | Installed manifest |
| `DELETE` | `/api/voice/models/:kind/:modelId` | Remove model |
| `POST` | `/api/voice/refs/upload` | Reference audio upload |
| `GET` | `/api/voice/clone-prompts` | List clone prompts |
| `POST` | `/api/stt/transcribe` | STT batch (local or provider) |
| `GET` | `/api/stt/status` | STT backend health (`streaming`, `streamingSupported`) |
| WS | `/api/stt/ws` | Live local STT PCM stream |
| `POST` | `/api/tts/synthesize` | TTS (local, provider, or browser) |
| `GET` | `/api/tts/status` | TTS backend health (`streaming`, `streamingSupported`) |
| WS | `/api/tts/ws` | Live local TTS PCM stream (Int16 binary frames) |

**GPU / CPU guidance:** Prefer **CUDA** when `GET /api/system/hardware` reports `backend === 'cuda'` — use `bfloat16` TTS and `whisper-small` STT. The voice provisioner ([`server/voice/provision.js`](../server/voice/provision.js)) installs **CUDA 12.4 PyTorch + matching torchaudio** (`cu124` wheels) on NVIDIA hosts and **CPU-only** builds otherwise, pins **`transformers==4.57.3`** (qwen-tts 0.1.1 is incompatible with transformers 5.x — missing `pad_token_id` on talker config), and after optional packages **always re-applies** idempotent post-install patches under `qwen_tts/`: restore `@check_model_inputs()` for transformers 4.57.3 (reverses legacy MIN-170 stripping) and `config.pad_token_id` → `getattr(config, "pad_token_id", None)` in `modeling_qwen3_tts.py`, then **re-pins** torch/torchaudio so pip cannot leave mismatched versions (Windows `WinError 127` on `torchaudio._extension`). Use **Repair** in Models → Voice after upgrading from an older CPU-only install or to pick up `faster-whisper` / qwen-tts compatibility fixes. Runtime status `cudaAvailable` reflects the worker’s actual `torch.cuda.is_available()` when the worker is running, otherwise `torchVariant` from `~/.minnow/voice/meta.json`. On CPU-only hosts, defaults use `float32` and `whisper-base`; expect slower first load and synthesis. **Flash attention** is used when the worker reports `flashAttnAvailable`. Fit badges in UI: [`src/voice/voice-fit.ts`](../src/voice/voice-fit.ts). **Mic STT:** provider path uses `MediaRecorder` → WebM → [`src/voice/recorded-audio.ts`](../src/voice/recorded-audio.ts) WAV → batch `POST /api/stt/transcribe`. **Local streaming** sends 16 kHz PCM over `/api/stt/ws` with live composer updates — toggle **Models → Voice → Speech-to-text → Configuration → Composer mic → Live dictation (streaming)** (`voice.stt.local.streamingEnabled`, default on). **Silence auto-submit:** [`src/voice/silence-detector.ts`](../src/voice/silence-detector.ts) ends dictation after `voice.limits.silenceTimeoutSeconds` (default **2.5s**) of silence following speech; set to **0** for manual stop only. Config: `voice.stt.local.streamingEnabled` (default true).

**Fallbacks:** `stt.backend === 'provider'` → OpenAI-compatible `/v1/audio/transcriptions` on the configured provider. `tts.backend === 'provider'` → `/v1/audio/speech`. `tts.backend === 'browser'` → `speechSynthesis` in [`src/ui/voice-controls.ts`](../src/ui/voice-controls.ts) (no server round-trip).

Tests: `test/voice/*.test.mjs`, `test/voice/*.test.mts`, `test/stt/*.test.mjs`, `test/tts/*.test.mjs`, `test/ui/voice-controls.test.mts`, `test/os/models-app.test.mts`. Streaming TTS coverage: `test/voice/dual-residency.test.mjs`, `test/voice/local-tts-stream.test.mjs`, `test/voice/voice-session.test.mts`, `test/tts/tts-protocol.test.mjs`, `test/tts/stream-session.test.mjs`, `test/tts/synthesize.test.mjs`.

**Vite-only (`npm run dev`):** No `/api/providers`; client synthesizes a fallback provider id. Settings → **Providers** shows an offline hint and hides the add form until `npm start` is running.

**Settings → Providers** (`#/settings/providers`): lists registered backends from `GET /api/providers`, **Set active**, **Remove** (when more than one), **Add provider**, and per-row **Edit provider** (label, base URL, API style — **LM Studio v0**, **OpenAI-compatible**, or **Anthropic Messages** — **models path**, **chat completions path** / **Messages path** for `anthropic-v1`, auth, enabled, **constrained tool calls** override, **Probe models** / **Probe structured output**, optional new API key → `POST`/`PUT /api/providers` + `PUT .../secrets`). Row badge: `Structured output: yes/no/unknown` from `capabilities.json`. Global **Constrained tool calls** lives under **Settings → Tools** (`config.json` → `toolCalls.useConstrainedDecoding`, default **off**); per-provider override on edit forms. Paths default per `apiKind` (`/v1/...`, `/api/v0/...`, or `/v1/messages` for Anthropic); changing API style resets paths when they still match the previous defaults (custom paths e.g. OpenCode Zen `/zen/v1/...` or OpenCode Go `/zen/go/v1/...` are preserved). **OpenCode Zen (MIN-330):** typical setup is base `https://opencode.ai` with paths `/zen/v1/models` and `/zen/v1/chat/completions` (or base `https://opencode.ai/zen/v1` with `/v1/...` defaults — server normalizes duplicate `/v1`). GPT models stay on **chat/completions**; Zen's gateway converts to the Responses API upstream. Server generations sanitize openai-v1 bodies (strip LM Studio sampler fields, map `max_tokens` → `max_completion_tokens` for gpt-5, drop temperature for gpt-5/o-series) via [`server/providers/sanitize-completion-body.js`](../server/providers/sanitize-completion-body.js) and [`server/providers/opencode-zen.js`](../server/providers/opencode-zen.js) (path normalization). Client: [`src/ui/settings-providers.ts`](../src/ui/settings-providers.ts), [`src/providers/paths.ts`](../src/providers/paths.ts), [`src/providers/store.ts`](../src/providers/store.ts), [`src/config/tool-calls-meta.ts`](../src/config/tool-calls-meta.ts).

**Constrained tool loop:** When enabled and probed capable, [`src/tools/loop.ts`](../src/tools/loop.ts) and [`src/agents/sub-agent-runner.ts`](../src/agents/sub-agent-runner.ts) attach per-turn `response_format` from [`buildToolCallResponseFormat`](../src/providers/tool-call-schema.ts) (enabled tools only, max 8 branches). Upstream **400** mentioning `response_format` / `json_schema` triggers one strip-and-retry without constraints. Invalid or blank tool JSON after a constrained turn surfaces errors via [`parseToolArguments`](../src/tools/parse-tool-arguments.ts) (`Tool arguments were not valid JSON.` / `Tool arguments were empty.`); legacy turns still coerce bad JSON to `{}`. Before `POST /api/tools`, [`validateToolRequiredArgs`](../src/tools/validate-tool-required-args.ts) rejects missing required schema fields (e.g. `read_file` without `path`) with a model-actionable message instead of the server’s `Path is required`. [`mergeContentJsonToolCalls`](../src/providers/constrained-tool-content.ts) keeps streamed tool-call ids but merges assistant `content` JSON when it is richer than SSE `arguments` (not only when SSE is `{}`) via [`toolArgumentsRichnessScore`](../src/providers/constrained-tool-content.ts) — fixes OpenCode Zen / constrained providers that stream partial `board_init` payloads missing `tasks`/`waves`. [`normalizeBoardInitArgs`](../src/tools/board-tools.ts) also JSON-parses stringified `tasks`/`waves` before validation. Generations upstream HTTP errors call `markError` instead of buffering HTML error pages; clients surface `event: end` `status: "error"` via [`postChatCompletions`](../src/providers/fetch-chat.ts) / [`streamCompletionTurn`](../src/tools/loop.ts). Harmony / `gpt-oss` model ids are denylisted. Dev log: `localStorage.minnowDebugConstrained = '1'`.

Client modules: [`src/config/storage-mode.ts`](../src/config/storage-mode.ts), [`api-client.ts`](../src/config/api-client.ts), [`migrate.ts`](../src/config/migrate.ts), [`tool-security-meta.ts`](../src/config/tool-security-meta.ts) (`toolSecurity.filesystemAccess`).

### Migration from `localStorage`

On first load with config API available, the client reads legacy keys and `POST /api/config/migrate`, then removes:

| localStorage key | File |
|------------------|------|
| `minnow-sessions-v1` | `sessions/state.json` |
| `minnow.tools` | `tools.json` |
| `minnow.systemPrompt` | `system-prompt.json` |

Re-run is **idempotent** (`skipped: true` when `config.json` has `migratedFromLocalStorage: true`).

### Vite-only fallback (`npm run dev`)

No `/api/config/*` → client uses **`storageMode: 'localStorage'`** (same keys as before). Settings drawer shows **`#configStorageBanner`**: file-backed config requires `npm start`. **No dual-write.** `detectConfigServer()` re-probes until server mode succeeds (2.5s timeout × 3 attempts); successful server mode is cached. Models → **Routing** re-renders on every tab activation and refreshes when already open after `initApp` (fixes OS-router deep link racing session init).
